function catalogSeed_(key, platform, countryCode, country, appName, appId, packageName, isBbva, order, segment, section, scope, enabled) {
  return [key, platform, platform === 'Android' ? 'Google Play' : platform === 'iOS' ? 'App Store' : 'Huawei AppGallery',
    countryCode, country, appName, appId || '', packageName || '', Boolean(isBbva), enabled !== false,
    platform === 'Huawei' ? 'es_' + countryCode : 'es', '', 0.30, 0.50, 5, true, '', '', order,
    segment || 'BANK', section || 'COUNTRY_BANKS', '2026Q3', scope || 'BUSINESS', '', '',
    true, '', '', ''];
}

function commercialSeed_(key, platform, countryCode, country, appName, appId, packageName, order, evidenceUrl, storeUrl) {
  const row = catalogSeed_(key, platform, countryCode, country, appName, appId, packageName, false, order,
    'FINTECH', 'COUNTRY_COMMERCIAL_DIGITAL', 'COMMERCIAL');
  row[25] = true; row[26] = evidenceUrl; row[27] = storeUrl; row[28] = '2026-07-16';
  return row;
}

========================================
ARCHIVO: 10_Main.gs

/** Punto de entrada diario. Huawei se captura como fuente complementaria. */
function runDailyRatingMonitor() {
  const startedAt = new Date();
  const runId = Utilities.formatDate(startedAt, MARKETS_CONFIG.timeZone, 'yyyyMMdd-HHmmss') + '-' + Utilities.getUuid().slice(0, 8);
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  if (!lock.tryLock(MARKETS_CONFIG.lockTimeoutMs)) throw new Error('Otra ejecucion del monitor sigue activa.');

  let ss;
  try {
    ss = openMarketsSpreadsheet_();
    ensureWorkbookSchema_(ss);
    const control = readControl_(ss);
    const catalog = readAppCatalog_(ss);
    const referenceKey = Utilities.formatDate(startedAt, trimString_(control.TIME_ZONE) || MARKETS_CONFIG.timeZone, 'yyyy-MM-dd');
    const referenceDate = dateFromKey_(referenceKey);
    const results = fetchCatalogRatings_(catalog);
    try { updateMarketsLogoCache_(results); } catch (logoError) {
      console.warn('No se pudieron actualizar los logos: ' + errorToString_(logoError));
    }
    appendFetchLog_(ss, runId, referenceDate, startedAt, results);
    const rows = buildRatingRows_(results, referenceDate, startedAt);
    const ratingSheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.ratings);
    const upserted = upsertRowsByKey_(ratingSheet, rows, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.ratings.length);
    const lookback = toNonNegativeInteger_(control.RATING_LOOKBACK_DAYS) || 30;
    rebuildRatingMetrics_(ss, lookback, catalog);
    const ratings = readRatingObjects_(ss);
    const dashboard = rebuildDashboardDatasets_(ss, catalog, ratings,
      { referenceDate: referenceDate, results: results });
    const alerts = buildAndProcessAlerts_(ss, referenceDate, catalog, runId, control, ratings);
    const operationalResults = marketsCriticalItems_(results);
    const stats = summarizeFetchResults_(operationalResults);
    const dataStatus = !operationalResults.length ? 'ERROR' :
      stats.error === 0 ? (stats.noRating > 0 ? 'PARTIAL' : 'OK') : (stats.ok > 0 ? 'PARTIAL' : 'ERROR');
    const status = ['ERROR', 'PARTIAL'].indexOf(alerts.deliveryStatus) >= 0 && dataStatus === 'OK' ? 'PARTIAL' : dataStatus;
    appendRunLog_(ss, [
      startedAt, runId, status, operationalResults.length, stats.ok, stats.noRating, stats.error,
      upserted, alerts.length, new Date().getTime() - startedAt.getTime(), buildRunDetail_(operationalResults, alerts.deliveryDetail),
      0, dashboard.trendRows, dashboard.competitionRows,
      stats.ok, validateWeightConfiguration_(catalog), alerts.subscriberCount || 0, alerts.deliveryCount || 0,
      alerts.deliveryStatus || 'UNKNOWN', alerts.deliveryDetail || ''
    ]);
    try {
      refreshMarketsDashboardCache_(ss, catalog);
    } catch (cacheError) {
      try { invalidateMarketsDashboardCache_(); } catch (ignored) {}
      console.error('No se pudo actualizar la cache del dashboard: ' + errorToString_(cacheError));
    }
    return { runId: runId, status: status, apps: operationalResults.length, rowsUpserted: upserted,
      alerts: alerts.length, alertSubscribers: alerts.subscriberCount || 0, emailsSent: alerts.deliveryCount || 0,
      alertDeliveryStatus: alerts.deliveryStatus || 'UNKNOWN', alertDeliveryDetail: alerts.deliveryDetail || '' };
  } catch (error) {
    if (ss) {
      try {
        appendRunLog_(ss, [startedAt, runId, 'ERROR', 0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0,
          new Date().getTime() - startedAt.getTime(), errorToString_(error), 0, 0, 0, 0, 'UNKNOWN',
          0, 0, 'NOT_RUN', errorToString_(error)]);
      } catch (ignored) {}
    }
    throw error;
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

function summarizeFetchResults_(results) {
  return (results || []).reduce(function (out, result) {
    if (result.ok) out.ok += 1;
    else if (result.noRating) out.noRating += 1;
    else out.error += 1;
    return out;
  }, { ok: 0, noRating: 0, error: 0 });
}

function buildRunDetail_(results, alertDeliveryDetail) {
  const errors = (results || []).filter(function (result) { return !result.ok; }).map(function (result) {
    return result.app.platform + '/' + result.app.countryCode + '/' + result.app.appName + ': ' + result.error;
  });
  const fetchDetail = errors.slice(0, 20).join(' | ') + (errors.length > 20 ? ' | +' + (errors.length - 20) + ' errores' : '');
  const alertDetail = trimString_(alertDeliveryDetail);
  return [fetchDetail, alertDetail ? 'Canal de alertas: ' + alertDetail : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' | ');
}

function runNow() {
  return runDailyRatingMonitor();
}


========================================
ARCHIVO: 20_Adapters.gs
========================================

function fetchCatalogRatings_(catalog) {
  const grouped = { Android: [], iOS: [], Huawei: [] };
  catalog.forEach(function (app) { grouped[app.platform].push(app); });
  return []
    .concat(fetchAndroidRatings_(grouped.Android))
    .concat(fetchIosRatings_(grouped.iOS))
    .concat(fetchHuaweiRatings_(grouped.Huawei));
}

function fetchAndroidRatings_(apps) {
  if (!apps.length) return [];
  const requests = apps.map(function (app) {
    return {
      url: 'https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=' + encodeURIComponent(app.packageName) +
        '&hl=' + encodeURIComponent(app.locale || 'en') + '&gl=' + encodeURIComponent(app.countryCode),
      method: 'get', followRedirects: true, muteHttpExceptions: true,
      headers: {
        Accept: 'text/html,application/xhtml+xml',
        'Accept-Language': (app.locale || 'en') + ',en;q=0.8',
        'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (compatible; BBVA-Markets-Monitor/' + MARKETS_CONFIG.codeVersion + ')'
      }
    };
  });
  return parseBatchWithRetry_(apps, requests, parseAndroidResponse_);
}

function parseAndroidResponse_(app, response) {
  const status = responseCode_(response);
  if (status < 200 || status >= 300) return fetchFailure_(app, 'HTTP ' + status, retryableStatus_(status), status);
  const parsed = parsePlayRatingHtml_(response.getContentText());
  if (parsed.rating === null) {
    const failure = fetchFailure_(app, 'No se encontro un rating publico valido.', false, status, true);
    failure.logoUrl = parsed.logoUrl;
    return failure;
  }
  return fetchSuccess_(app, parsed.rating, parsed.ratingCount, status, parsed.logoUrl);
}

function parsePlayRatingHtml_(html) {
  const source = String(html || '');
  const scriptRegex = /<script[^>]+type=["']application\/ld\+json["'][^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/script>/gi;
  let match;
  while ((match = scriptRegex.exec(source)) !== null) {
    const json = safeJsonParse_(match[1]);
    const found = findAggregateRating_(json);
    if (found.rating !== null) return found;
  }
  const valueMatch = source.match(/["']ratingValue["']\s*:\s*["']?([0-5](?:[.,]\d+)?)/i);
  const countMatch = source.match(/["'](?:ratingCount|ratingReviewCount|reviewCount)["']\s*:\s*["']?([\d.,\s]+)/i);
  const imageMatch = source.match(/["']image["']\s*:\s*["'](https:\/\/[^"']+)/i);
  return {
    rating: valueMatch ? normalizeRating_(valueMatch[1]) : null,
    ratingCount: countMatch ? parseStoreInteger_(countMatch[1]) : null,
    logoUrl: imageMatch ? imageMatch[1].replace(/\\u0026/g, '&') : ''
  };
}

function findAggregateRating_(node) {
  if (!node || typeof node !== 'object') return { rating: null, ratingCount: null, logoUrl: '' };
  if (Array.isArray(node)) {
    for (let i = 0; i < node.length; i += 1) {
      const found = findAggregateRating_(node[i]);
      if (found.rating !== null) return found;
    }
    return { rating: null, ratingCount: null, logoUrl: '' };
  }
  if (node.aggregateRating) {
    const rating = normalizeRating_(node.aggregateRating.ratingValue);
    if (rating !== null) {
      return {
        rating: rating,
        ratingCount: parseStoreInteger_(node.aggregateRating.ratingCount || node.aggregateRating.reviewCount),
        logoUrl: storeImageUrl_(node.image || node.logo)
      };
    }
  }
  if (node['@graph']) return findAggregateRating_(node['@graph']);
  return { rating: null, ratingCount: null, logoUrl: '' };
}

function storeImageUrl_(value) {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) value = value[0];
  if (value && typeof value === 'object') value = value.url || value.contentUrl;
  const url = trimString_(value);
  return /^https:\/\//i.test(url) ? url : '';
}

function parseStoreInteger_(value) {
  const raw = trimString_(value).replace(/\s/g, '');
  if (!raw) return null;
  const separators = (raw.match(/[.,]/g) || []).length;
  if (separators > 1 || /[.,]\d{3}$/.test(raw)) return toNonNegativeInteger_(raw.replace(/[.,]/g, ''));
  return toNonNegativeInteger_(raw.replace(',', '.'));
}

function fetchIosRatings_(apps) {
  if (!apps.length) return [];
  const requests = apps.map(function (app) {
    return {
      url: 'https://itunes.apple.com/lookup?id=' + encodeURIComponent(app.appId) +
        '&country=' + encodeURIComponent(app.countryCode.toLowerCase()),
      method: 'get', followRedirects: true, muteHttpExceptions: true,
      headers: { Accept: 'application/json' }
    };
  });
  return parseBatchWithRetry_(apps, requests, parseIosResponse_);
}

function parseIosResponse_(app, response) {
  const status = responseCode_(response);
  if (status < 200 || status >= 300) return fetchFailure_(app, 'HTTP ' + status, retryableStatus_(status), status);
  const data = safeJsonParse_(response.getContentText());
  if (!data || !Array.isArray(data.results) || !data.results.length) {
    return fetchFailure_(app, 'App no disponible en la App Store del pais.', false, status);
  }
  const item = data.results[0];
  const logoUrl = storeImageUrl_(item.artworkUrl512 || item.artworkUrl100 || item.artworkUrl60);
  const count = toNonNegativeInteger_(item.userRatingCount);
  const rating = count > 0 ? normalizeRating_(item.averageUserRating) : null;
  if (rating === null) {
    const failure = fetchFailure_(app, 'Sin rating publico.', false, status, true);
    failure.logoUrl = logoUrl;
    return failure;
  }
  return fetchSuccess_(app, rating, count, status, logoUrl);
}

function fetchHuaweiRatings_(apps) {
  if (!apps.length) return [];
  const interfaceResult = getHuaweiInterfaceCode_();
  if (!interfaceResult.ok) {
    return apps.map(function (app) {
      return fetchFailure_(app, interfaceResult.error, interfaceResult.retryable, interfaceResult.status);
    });
  }
  const identityId = Utilities.getUuid();
  const infoRequests = apps.map(function (app) { return buildHuaweiInfoRequest_(app, interfaceResult.code, identityId); });
  const infoResponses = fetchInChunks_(infoRequests);
  const infoResults = apps.map(function (app, index) {
    return retryParsedRequest_(app, infoRequests[index], infoResponses[index], parseHuaweiInfoResponse_);
  });
  const validApps = infoResults.filter(function (result) { return result.ok; });
  if (!validApps.length) return infoResults;
  const ratingRequests = validApps.map(buildHuaweiRatingRequest_);
  const ratingResponses = fetchInChunks_(ratingRequests);
  const ratingsByKey = {};
  validApps.forEach(function (appResult, index) {
    ratingsByKey[appResult.app.appKey] = retryParsedRequest_(appResult.app, ratingRequests[index], ratingResponses[index], function (app, response) {
      return parseHuaweiRatingResponse_(appResult, response);
    });
  });
  return infoResults.map(function (result) {
    return result.ok ? ratingsByKey[result.app.appKey] : result;
  });
}

function getHuaweiInterfaceCode_() {
  const request = {
    url: 'https://web-dre.hispace.dbankcloud.com/edge/webedge/getInterfaceCode',
    method: 'post', muteHttpExceptions: true, headers: huaweiOriginHeaders_()
  };
  let response;
  try { response = fetchRequestWithRetry_(request); } catch (error) {
    return { ok: false, code: '', status: 0, retryable: true, error: errorToString_(error) };
  }
  const status = responseCode_(response);
  if (status < 200 || status >= 300) return { ok: false, code: '', status: status, retryable: retryableStatus_(status), error: 'HTTP ' + status + ' obteniendo Interface-Code.' };
  let code = safeJsonParse_(response.getContentText());
  if (code === null) code = response.getContentText();
  code = trimString_(code).replace(/^"|"$/g, '');
  return code
    ? { ok: true, code: code, status: status, retryable: false, error: '' }
    : { ok: false, code: '', status: status, retryable: false, error: 'Interface-Code vacio.' };
}

function buildHuaweiInfoRequest_(app, interfaceCode, identityId) {
  const headers = huaweiOriginHeaders_();
  headers['Interface-Code'] = interfaceCode + '_' + Date.now();
  headers['Identity-Id'] = identityId;
  return {
    url: 'https://web-dre.hispace.dbankcloud.com/edge/webedge/appinfo',
    method: 'post', contentType: 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded;charset=UTF-8',
    payload: 'pkgName=' + encodeURIComponent(app.packageName), followRedirects: true,
    muteHttpExceptions: true, headers: headers
  };
}

function parseHuaweiInfoResponse_(app, response) {
  const status = responseCode_(response);
  if (status < 200 || status >= 300) return fetchFailure_(app, 'HTTP ' + status + ' consultando ficha Huawei.', retryableStatus_(status), status);
  const item = safeJsonParse_(response.getContentText());
  if (!item || !trimString_(item.appId)) return fetchFailure_(app, trimString_(item && item.message) || 'Ficha Huawei no disponible.', false, status);
  if (app.appId && trimString_(item.appId) !== app.appId) {
    return fetchFailure_(app, 'APP_ID Huawei inesperado. Esperado ' + app.appId + ', obtenido ' + trimString_(item.appId) + '.', false, status);
  }
  return {
    ok: true, retryable: false, status: status, app: app,
    huaweiAppId: trimString_(item.appId),
    logoUrl: storeImageUrl_(item.icon || item.iconUrl || item.logo),
    error: ''
  };
}

function buildHuaweiRatingRequest_(infoResult) {
  const app = infoResult.app;
  return {
    url: 'https://web-dre.hispace.dbankcloud.com/edge/index/commentlist3',
    method: 'post', contentType: 'application/json;charset=UTF-8',
    payload: JSON.stringify({
      method: 'internal.user.commenList3', serviceType: 20, reqPageNum: 1,
      maxResults: 25, appid: infoResult.huaweiAppId, version: '10.0.0',
      zone: app.countryCode, locale: app.locale || huaweiLocale_(app.countryCode)
    }),
    followRedirects: true, muteHttpExceptions: true, headers: huaweiOriginHeaders_()
  };
}

function parseHuaweiRatingResponse_(infoResult, response) {
  const app = infoResult.app;
  const status = responseCode_(response);
  if (status < 200 || status >= 300) return fetchFailure_(app, 'HTTP ' + status + ' consultando ratings Huawei.', retryableStatus_(status), status);
  const summary = safeJsonParse_(response.getContentText());
  if (!summary || Number(summary.rtnCode) !== 0) return fetchFailure_(app, trimString_(summary && summary.rtnDesc) || 'Huawei rechazo la consulta de ratings.', false, status);
  if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(summary, 'count') || !Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(summary, 'score')) {
    return fetchFailure_(app, 'Respuesta Huawei incompleta.', true, status);
  }
  const count = toNonNegativeInteger_(summary.count);
  const rating = count > 0 ? normalizeRating_(summary.score) : null;
  if (rating === null) {
    const failure = fetchFailure_(app, 'Sin rating publico.', false, status, true);
    failure.logoUrl = infoResult.logoUrl;
    return failure;
  }
  return fetchSuccess_(app, rating, count, status, infoResult.logoUrl);
}

function huaweiOriginHeaders_() {
  return { Origin: 'https://appgallery.huawei.com', Referer: 'https://appgallery.huawei.com/' };
}

function huaweiLocale_(countryCode) {
  const locales = { ES: 'es_ES', AR: 'es_AR', PE: 'es_PE', CO: 'es_CO', UY: 'es_UY', MX: 'es_MX', VE: 'es_VE', TR: 'tr_TR' };
  return locales[countryCode] || 'en';
}

function parseBatchWithRetry_(apps, requests, parser) {
  const responses = safeFetchAll_(requests);
  return apps.map(function (app, index) {
    return retryParsedRequest_(app, requests[index], responses[index], parser);
  });
}

function safeFetchAll_(requests) {
  try { return UrlFetchApp.fetchAll(requests); } catch (batchError) {
    console.warn('fetchAll fallo: ' + errorToString_(batchError));
    return requests.map(function (request) {
      try { return UrlFetchApp.fetch(request.url, request); } catch (error) { return error; }
    });
  }
}

function fetchInChunks_(requests) {
  const output = [];
  for (let offset = 0; offset < requests.length; offset += MARKETS_CONFIG.huaweiBatchSize) {
    if (offset > 0) Utilities.sleep(MARKETS_CONFIG.huaweiBatchDelayMs);
    Array.prototype.push.apply(output, safeFetchAll_(requests.slice(offset, offset + MARKETS_CONFIG.huaweiBatchSize)));
  }
  return output;
}

function retryParsedRequest_(app, request, initialResponse, parser) {
  let result = parser(app, initialResponse);
  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= MARKETS_CONFIG.requestRetries && result.retryable; attempt += 1) {
    Utilities.sleep(MARKETS_CONFIG.retryBaseDelayMs * Math.pow(2, attempt - 1));
    let response;
    try { response = UrlFetchApp.fetch(request.url, request); } catch (error) { response = error; }
    result = parser(app, response);
  }
  return result;
}

function fetchRequestWithRetry_(request) {
  let response;
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= MARKETS_CONFIG.requestRetries; attempt += 1) {
    if (attempt > 0) Utilities.sleep(MARKETS_CONFIG.retryBaseDelayMs * Math.pow(2, attempt - 1));
    response = UrlFetchApp.fetch(request.url, request);
    const status = responseCode_(response);
    if (!retryableStatus_(status)) return response;
  }
  return response;
}

function responseCode_(response) {
  return response && typeof response.getResponseCode === 'function' ? response.getResponseCode() : 0;
}

function fetchSuccess_(app, rating, ratingCount, status, logoUrl) {
  return { ok: true, noRating: false, retryable: false, status: status, app: app, rating: rating,
    ratingCount: ratingCount, logoUrl: storeImageUrl_(logoUrl), error: '' };
}

function fetchFailure_(app, message, retryable, status, noRating) {
  return { ok: false, noRating: Boolean(noRating), retryable: Boolean(retryable), status: status || 0, app: app, rating: null, ratingCount: null, error: message };
}


========================================
ARCHIVO: 30_Ratings.gs
========================================

function buildRatingRows_(fetchResults, observedDate, ingestedAt) {
  const monthDate = dateFromKey_(dateKey_(observedDate).slice(0, 7) + '-01');
  return fetchResults.filter(function (result) {
    return result.ok && normalizeRating_(result.rating) !== null;
  }).map(function (result) {
    const app = result.app;
    const rating = normalizeRating_(result.rating);
    return [
      makeRatingKey_(observedDate, app.appKey), observedDate, observedDate.getFullYear(), monthDate,
      app.platform, app.store, app.countryCode, app.country, app.appKey, app.appName,
      app.appId || app.packageName, app.isBbva, rating,
      result.ratingCount === null ? '' : result.ratingCount,
      '', '', '', false, app.store + ' API', ingestedAt,
      app.marketSegment || 'BANK', app.displayOrder === null ? '' : app.displayOrder,
      '', '', '', false, '', ''
    ];
  });
}

/**
 * Recalcula meses, delta y ranking. El ranking solo se publica cuando todos los
 * miembros habilitados del benchmark plataforma/pais/segmento tienen rating.
 */
function rebuildRatingMetrics_(ss, lookbackDays, catalog) {
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.ratings);
  if (sheet.getLastRow() <= 1) return;
  const width = MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.ratings.length;
  const values = sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, width).getValues();
  const indexes = indexHeaders_(MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.ratings);
  const catalogByKey = {};
  const expectedByGroup = {};
  (catalog || []).forEach(function (app) {
    catalogByKey[app.appKey] = app;
    const groupKey = [app.platform, app.countryCode, app.marketSegment || 'BANK'].join('|');
    expectedByGroup[groupKey] = (expectedByGroup[groupKey] || 0) + 1;
  });
  const snapshotGroups = {};
  const seriesByApp = {};
  const maxDateByApp = {};
  const monthValues = values.map(function () { return ['']; });
  const catalogValues = values.map(function () { return ['', '', '', '', '', false]; });

  values.forEach(function (row, rowIndex) {
    const date = row[indexes.observed_date];
    const rating = normalizeRating_(row[indexes.rating]);
    const appKey = trimString_(row[indexes.app_key]);
    if (!(date instanceof Date) || !appKey || rating === null) return;
    monthValues[rowIndex][0] = dateFromKey_(dateKey_(date).slice(0, 7) + '-01');
    const app = catalogByKey[appKey];
    const segment = app ? app.marketSegment : (trimString_(row[indexes.market_segment]) || 'BANK');
    const displayOrder = app && app.displayOrder !== null ? app.displayOrder : toNonNegativeInteger_(row[indexes.display_order]);
    catalogValues[rowIndex][0] = segment;
    catalogValues[rowIndex][1] = displayOrder === null ? '' : displayOrder;
    if (app) {
      const groupKey = [app.platform, app.countryCode, segment].join('|');
      const snapshotKey = groupKey + '|' + dateKey_(date);
      (snapshotGroups[snapshotKey] || (snapshotGroups[snapshotKey] = {
        expected: expectedByGroup[groupKey] || 0, items: []
      })).items.push({ rowIndex: rowIndex, rating: rating });
    }
    const dateMillis = date.getTime();
    (seriesByApp[appKey] || (seriesByApp[appKey] = [])).push({
      rowIndex: rowIndex,
      dateMillis: dateMillis,
      rating: rating,
      ratingCount: toNonNegativeInteger_(row[indexes.rating_count])
    });
    maxDateByApp[appKey] = Math.max(maxDateByApp[appKey] || 0, dateMillis);
  });

  const metrics = values.map(function () { return ['', '', '', false]; });
  const countMetrics = values.map(function () { return ['', '']; });
  Object.keys(snapshotGroups).forEach(function (key) {
    const group = snapshotGroups[key];
    const observed = group.items.length;
    const expected = group.expected;
    const complete = expected > 0 && observed === expected;
    const coverage = expected > 0 ? round2_(observed / expected) : 0;
    const ranks = rankSnapshot_(group.items);
    group.items.forEach(function (item, index) {
      metrics[item.rowIndex][0] = complete ? ranks[index] : '';
      catalogValues[item.rowIndex][2] = expected;
      catalogValues[item.rowIndex][3] = observed;
      catalogValues[item.rowIndex][4] = coverage;
      catalogValues[item.rowIndex][5] = complete;
    });
  });
  Object.keys(seriesByApp).forEach(function (appKey) {
    const series = seriesByApp[appKey].sort(function (a, b) { return a.dateMillis - b.dateMillis; });
    series.forEach(function (item) {
      const target = item.dateMillis - lookbackDays * 86400000;
      const previous = findPreviousRating_(series, target);
      const previousCount = findPreviousValue_(series, target, 'ratingCount');
      metrics[item.rowIndex][1] = previous === null ? '' : previous;
      metrics[item.rowIndex][2] = previous === null ? '' : round2_(item.rating - previous);
      metrics[item.rowIndex][3] = item.dateMillis === maxDateByApp[appKey];
      countMetrics[item.rowIndex][0] = previousCount === null ? '' : previousCount;
      countMetrics[item.rowIndex][1] = previousCount === null || item.ratingCount === null
        ? '' : item.ratingCount - previousCount;
    });
  });
  sheet.getRange(2, indexes.year_month + 1, monthValues.length, 1).setValues(monthValues).setNumberFormat('yyyy-mm');
  sheet.getRange(2, indexes.ranking + 1, metrics.length, 4).setValues(metrics);
  sheet.getRange(2, indexes.market_segment + 1, catalogValues.length, 6).setValues(catalogValues);
  sheet.getRange(2, indexes.previous_rating_count_30d + 1, countMetrics.length, 2).setValues(countMetrics);
}

function readRatingObjects_(ss) {
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.ratings);
  if (sheet.getLastRow() <= 1) return [];
  const headers = MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.ratings;
  const indexes = indexHeaders_(headers);
  return sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, headers.length).getValues().map(function (row) {
    const observedDate = row[indexes.observed_date];
    return {
      recordKey: trimString_(row[indexes.record_key]), observedDate: observedDate,
      yearMonth: observedDate instanceof Date ? dateKey_(observedDate).slice(0, 7) : '',
      platform: trimString_(row[indexes.platform]), countryCode: trimString_(row[indexes.country_code]),
      country: trimString_(row[indexes.country]), appKey: trimString_(row[indexes.app_key]),
      appName: trimString_(row[indexes.app_name]), isBbva: parseBoolean_(row[indexes.is_bbva], false),
      rating: normalizeRating_(row[indexes.rating]), ratingCount: toNonNegativeInteger_(row[indexes.rating_count]),
      ranking: toNonNegativeInteger_(row[indexes.ranking]), previousRating30d: normalizeRating_(row[indexes.previous_rating_30d]),
      delta30d: toFiniteNumber_(row[indexes.delta_30d]), isLatest: parseBoolean_(row[indexes.is_latest], false),
      ingestedAt: row[indexes.ingested_at], marketSegment: trimString_(row[indexes.market_segment]) || 'BANK',
      displayOrder: toNonNegativeInteger_(row[indexes.display_order]),
      rankingExpectedApps: toNonNegativeInteger_(row[indexes.ranking_expected_apps]),
      rankingObservedApps: toNonNegativeInteger_(row[indexes.ranking_observed_apps]),
      rankingCoverage: toFiniteNumber_(row[indexes.ranking_coverage]),
      rankingIsComplete: parseBoolean_(row[indexes.ranking_is_complete], false),
      previousRatingCount30d: toNonNegativeInteger_(row[indexes.previous_rating_count_30d]),
      ratingCountDelta30d: toFiniteNumber_(row[indexes.rating_count_delta_30d])
    };
  }).filter(function (item) { return item.recordKey && item.rating !== null; });
}


========================================
ARCHIVO: 35_Dashboard.gs
========================================

/** Materializa las dos fuentes compactas consumidas por el Web App. */
function rebuildDashboardDatasets_(ss, catalog, ratings, execution) {
  const activeKeys = (catalog || []).reduce(function (out, app) { out[app.appKey] = true; return out; }, {});
  const activeRatings = (ratings || []).filter(function (item) { return Boolean(activeKeys[item.appKey]); });
  const competitionRows = buildCompetitionRows_(activeRatings, catalog, execution);
  const competitionSheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.competition);
  replaceSheetData_(competitionSheet, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.competition, competitionRows);

  const trendRows = buildMonthlyTrendRows_(activeRatings);
  const trendSheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.trends);
  replaceSheetData_(trendSheet, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.trends, trendRows);

  return { trendRows: trendRows.length, competitionRows: competitionRows.length };
}

function buildExecutionStatus_(execution) {
  const out = {};
  (execution && execution.results || []).forEach(function (result) {
    out[result.app.appKey] = result.ok ? 'OK' : result.noRating ? 'NO_RATING' : 'ERROR';
  });
  return out;
}

/** Solo publica consolidados con pesos positivos, documentados y completos. */
function weightedMetric_(configs, itemsByAppKey, field, weightField) {
  const criticalConfigs = marketsCriticalItems_(configs);
  const weightedConfigs = criticalConfigs.filter(function (app) {
    return nonNegativeNumberOrZero_(app[weightField]) > 0 && trimString_(app.weightSource) && app.weightEffectiveDate;
  });
  const totalWeight = weightedConfigs.reduce(function (sum, app) {
    return sum + nonNegativeNumberOrZero_(app[weightField]);
  }, 0);
  if (!totalWeight || weightedConfigs.length !== criticalConfigs.length) return { value: '', coverage: 0 };
  let availableWeight = 0;
  let weightedValue = 0;
  weightedConfigs.forEach(function (app) {
    const item = itemsByAppKey[app.appKey];
    const value = item ? toFiniteNumber_(item[field]) : null;
    if (value === null) return;
    const weight = nonNegativeNumberOrZero_(app[weightField]);
    availableWeight += weight;
    weightedValue += value * weight;
  });
  const coverage = round2_(availableWeight / totalWeight);
  return { value: Math.abs(availableWeight - totalWeight) < 0.0000001 ? round2_(weightedValue / totalWeight) : '', coverage: coverage };
}

function validateWeightConfiguration_(catalog) {
  const bbva = marketsCriticalItems_((catalog || []).filter(function (app) {
    return app.isBbva && (app.marketSegment || 'BANK') === 'BANK';
  }));
  const configured = bbva.filter(function (app) {
    return (app.countryPlatformWeight > 0 || app.groupUserWeight > 0) && app.weightSource && app.weightEffectiveDate;
  }).length;
  if (!configured) return 'MISSING';
  return configured === bbva.length ? 'OK' : 'PARTIAL';
}

function buildCompetitionRows_(ratings, catalog, execution) {
  const catalogByKey = (catalog || []).reduce(function (out, app) { out[app.appKey] = app; return out; }, {});
  const statusByApp = buildExecutionStatus_(execution);
  const referenceKey = execution && execution.referenceDate instanceof Date ? dateKey_(execution.referenceDate) : '';
  return (ratings || []).filter(function (item) { return Boolean(catalogByKey[item.appKey]); })
    .slice().sort(function (a, b) {
      return a.observedDate.getTime() - b.observedDate.getTime() ||
        a.countryCode.localeCompare(b.countryCode) || compareMarketsPlatforms_(a, b) ||
        a.marketSegment.localeCompare(b.marketSegment) ||
        (a.displayOrder || 0) - (b.displayOrder || 0);
    }).map(function (item) {
      const app = catalogByKey[item.appKey];
      const date = dateKey_(item.observedDate);
      return [item.observedDate, item.platform, item.countryCode, item.country, app.marketSegment,
        item.appKey, item.appName, item.isBbva, app.displayOrder === null ? '' : app.displayOrder,
        item.rating, item.ratingCount === null ? '' : item.ratingCount, item.rankingIsComplete,
        item.delta30d === null ? '' : item.delta30d,
        Boolean(date === referenceKey && statusByApp[item.appKey] === 'OK'),
        item.ratingCountDelta30d == null ? '' : item.ratingCountDelta30d, buildStoreWebUrl_(app)];
    });
}

/** URL web oficial con storefront explicito; nunca usa esquemas de aplicaciones nativas. */
function buildStoreWebUrl_(app) {
  const country = trimString_(app.countryCode).toUpperCase();
  const locale = (trimString_(app.locale).split(/[-_]/)[0] || 'es').toLowerCase();
  if (app.platform === 'Android' && app.packageName) {
    return 'https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=' + encodeURIComponent(app.packageName) +
      '&hl=' + encodeURIComponent(locale) + '&gl=' + encodeURIComponent(country);
  }
  if (app.platform === 'iOS' && app.appId) {
    return 'https://apps.apple.com/' + country.toLowerCase() + '/app/id' + encodeURIComponent(app.appId);
  }
  if (app.platform === 'Huawei' && app.appId) {
    return 'https://appgallery.huawei.com/app/' + encodeURIComponent(app.appId) +
      '?locale=' + encodeURIComponent(locale + '_' + country);
  }
  return '';
}

/** Agregacion mensual compacta; el mes siempre deriva de OBSERVED_DATE. */
function buildMonthlyTrendRows_(ratings) {
  const groups = {};
  (ratings || []).filter(function (item) { return item.observedDate instanceof Date && item.rating !== null; })
    .forEach(function (item) {
      const yearMonth = dateKey_(item.observedDate).slice(0, 7);
      const key = [yearMonth, item.platform, item.countryCode, item.marketSegment || 'BANK', item.appKey].join('|');
      const group = groups[key] || (groups[key] = { yearMonth: yearMonth, latest: null });
      if (!group.latest || item.observedDate.getTime() >= group.latest.observedDate.getTime()) group.latest = item;
    });
  return Object.keys(groups).sort().map(function (key) {
    const group = groups[key];
    const latest = group.latest;
    return [dateFromKey_(group.yearMonth + '-01'), latest.platform, latest.countryCode, latest.country,
      latest.appKey, latest.appName, latest.isBbva, latest.rating, latest.marketSegment || 'BANK',
      latest.ratingCount == null ? '' : latest.ratingCount];
  });
}

function openMarketsSpreadsheet_() {
  return SpreadsheetApp.openById(MARKETS_CONFIG.spreadsheetId);
}

function workbookDefinitions_() {
  return [
    [MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.control, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.control],
    [MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.catalog, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.catalog],
    [MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.ratings, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.ratings],
    [MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.competition, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.competition],
    [MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.trends, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.trends],
    [MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.fetchLog, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.fetchLog],
    [MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.alerts, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.alerts],
    [MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.alertPreferences, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.alertPreferences],
    [MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.runs, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.runs],
    [MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.usage, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.usage]
  ];
}

function ensureWorkbookSchema_(ss) {
  workbookDefinitions_().forEach(function (definition) {
    const sheet = getOrCreateSheet_(ss, definition[0]);
    if ([MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.competition, MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.trends].indexOf(definition[0]) < 0) {
      ensureCurrentSheetContract_(sheet, definition[1]);
    }
  });
  enforceCatalogPolicies_(ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.catalog));
}

/**
 * Mantiene decisiones de negocio que deben prevalecer sobre filas ya existentes.
 * El historico de RATINGS se conserva para auditoria; ENABLED controla el
 * perimetro vigente de captura, calculo y visualizacion.
 */
function enforceCatalogPolicies_(sheet) {
  if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() <= 1) return;
  const policyKeys = {
    'ANDROID|VE|BBVA': true,
    'IOS|VE|BBVA': true,
    'HUAWEI|VE|BBVA': true
  };
  const seedByKey = MARKETS_CATALOG_SEEDS.reduce(function (out, row) {
    if (policyKeys[row[0]]) out[row[0]] = row;
    return out;
  }, {});
  const width = MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.catalog.length;
  const rows = sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, width).getValues();
  rows.forEach(function (row, index) {
    const seed = seedByKey[trimString_(row[0])];
    if (!seed) return;
    const expected = seed.slice(5, 10);
    const current = row.slice(5, 10);
    const changed = expected.some(function (value, column) {
      return String(current[column]) !== String(value);
    });
    if (changed) sheet.getRange(index + 2, 6, 1, 5).setValues([expected]);
  });
}

function initializeWorkbook_(ss) {
  ensureWorkbookSchema_(ss);
  seedControl_(ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.control));
  seedCatalogDefaults_(ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.catalog));
  workbookDefinitions_().forEach(function (definition) {
    if ([MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.competition, MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.trends].indexOf(definition[0]) < 0) {
      applyTableStyle_(ss.getSheetByName(definition[0]), definition[1].length);
    }
  });
}

function getOrCreateSheet_(ss, name) {
  return ss.getSheetByName(name) || ss.insertSheet(name);
}

function ensureCurrentSheetContract_(sheet, expected) {
  ensureSheetCapacity_(sheet, 2, expected.length);
  const current = readSheetHeader_(sheet);
  const hasContent = current.some(function (value) { return Boolean(value); });
  if (!hasContent) {
    sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, expected.length).setValues([expected]);
    return;
  }
  assertCurrentSheetContract_(sheet, expected, current);
}

function requireCurrentSheetContract_(ss, name, expected) {
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(name);
  if (!sheet) throw new Error('Falta la hoja obligatoria ' + name + '. Ejecuta setupProject() con la version publicada.');
  assertCurrentSheetContract_(sheet, expected, readSheetHeader_(sheet));
  return sheet;
}

function readSheetHeader_(sheet) {
  const currentWidth = Math.max(1, sheet.getLastColumn());
  return sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, currentWidth).getValues()[0].map(trimString_);
}

function assertCurrentSheetContract_(sheet, expected, current) {
  const populatedWidth = current.reduce(function (last, value, index) {
    return value ? index + 1 : last;
  }, 0);
  const mismatch = populatedWidth !== expected.length || current.slice(0, expected.length).some(function (header, index) {
    return header !== expected[index];
  });
  if (mismatch) {
    throw new Error('Contrato incompatible en ' + sheet.getName() + '. La version publicada requiere exactamente: ' + expected.join(', '));
  }
}

function seedControl_(sheet) {
  const existingRows = sheet.getLastRow() > 1
    ? sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 3).getValues() : [];
  const rowByKey = existingRows.reduce(function (out, row, index) {
    const key = trimString_(row[0]);
    if (key) out[key] = index + 2;
    return out;
  }, {});
  const missing = [];
  MARKETS_CONFIG.controlDefaults.forEach(function (row) {
    if (rowByKey[row[0]]) {
      if (row[0] === 'CODE_VERSION') sheet.getRange(rowByKey[row[0]], 2).setValue(row[1]);
    } else {
      missing.push(row.slice());
    }
  });
  if (missing.length) {
    const start = sheet.getLastRow() + 1;
    ensureSheetCapacity_(sheet, start + missing.length - 1, 3);
    sheet.getRange(start, 1, missing.length, 3).setValues(missing);
  }
}

function seedCatalogDefaults_(sheet) {
  if (!MARKETS_CATALOG_SEEDS.length) return;
  const keyRows = sheet.getLastRow() > 1
    ? sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues() : [];
  const existing = keyRows.reduce(function (out, row) {
    const key = trimString_(row[0]);
    if (key) out[key] = true;
    return out;
  }, {});
  const missing = MARKETS_CATALOG_SEEDS.filter(function (row) { return !existing[row[0]]; })
    .map(function (row) { return row.slice(); });
  if (!missing.length) return;
  const start = sheet.getLastRow() + 1;
  ensureSheetCapacity_(sheet, start + missing.length - 1, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.catalog.length);
  sheet.getRange(start, 1, missing.length, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.catalog.length).setValues(missing);
  refreshFilter_(sheet, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.catalog.length);
}

function applyTableStyle_(sheet, width) {
  sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
  sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, width)
    .setBackground('#004481')
    .setFontColor('#FFFFFF')
    .setFontWeight('bold')
    .setWrap(true);
  sheet.setRowHeight(1, 34);
  sheet.setHiddenGridlines(true);
  if (sheet.getFilter()) sheet.getFilter().remove();
  const rows = Math.max(2, sheet.getLastRow());
  sheet.getRange(1, 1, rows, width).createFilter();
  if (sheet.getName() === MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.ratings) {
    sheet.getRange('B:B').setNumberFormat('yyyy-mm-dd');
    sheet.getRange('D:D').setNumberFormat('yyyy-mm');
    sheet.getRange('M:M').setNumberFormat('0.00');
    sheet.getRange('N:O').setNumberFormat('#,##0');
    sheet.getRange('P:Q').setNumberFormat('0.00');
    sheet.getRange('AA:AB').setNumberFormat('#,##0');
    sheet.getRange('T:T').setNumberFormat('yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm:ss');
  } else if (sheet.getName() === MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.trends) {
    sheet.getRange('A:A').setNumberFormat('yyyy-mm-dd');
    sheet.getRange('H:H').setNumberFormat('0.00');
    sheet.getRange('J:J').setNumberFormat('#,##0');
  } else if (sheet.getName() === MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.competition) {
    sheet.getRange('A:A').setNumberFormat('yyyy-mm-dd');
    sheet.getRange('J:J').setNumberFormat('0.00');
    sheet.getRange('K:K').setNumberFormat('#,##0');
    sheet.getRange('M:M').setNumberFormat('0.00');
    sheet.getRange('O:O').setNumberFormat('#,##0');
  } else if (sheet.getName() === MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.fetchLog) {
    sheet.getRange('B:B').setNumberFormat('yyyy-mm-dd');
    sheet.getRange('C:C').setNumberFormat('yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm:ss');
    sheet.getRange('J:K').setNumberFormat('0.00');
  } else if (sheet.getName() === MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.alerts) {
    sheet.getRange('A:A').setNumberFormat('yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm:ss');
    sheet.getRange('C:C').setNumberFormat('yyyy-mm-dd');
    sheet.getRange('N:O').setNumberFormat('0.00');
  } else if (sheet.getName() === MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.alertPreferences) {
    sheet.getRange('F:G').setNumberFormat('0.0');
    sheet.getRange('I:J').setNumberFormat('0.00');
    sheet.getRange('N:N').setNumberFormat('yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm:ss');
    sheet.getRange('S:T').setNumberFormat('0.00');
    sheet.getRange('V:W').setNumberFormat('0.00');
    sheet.getRange('Y:Z').setNumberFormat('0.00');
    sheet.getRange('AB:AE').setNumberFormat('yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm:ss');
  } else if (sheet.getName() === MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.runs) {
    sheet.getRange('A:A').setNumberFormat('yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm:ss');
  } else if (sheet.getName() === MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.usage) {
    sheet.getRange('A:A').setNumberFormat('yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm:ss');
  }
}

function ensureSheetCapacity_(sheet, requiredRows, requiredColumns) {
  if (sheet.getMaxRows() < requiredRows) {
    sheet.insertRowsAfter(sheet.getMaxRows(), requiredRows - sheet.getMaxRows());
  }
  if (sheet.getMaxColumns() < requiredColumns) {
    sheet.insertColumnsAfter(sheet.getMaxColumns(), requiredColumns - sheet.getMaxColumns());
  }
}

function readControl_(ss) {
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.control);
  const rows = sheet.getLastRow() > 1
    ? sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 2).getValues() : [];
  const control = rows.reduce(function (out, row) {
    const key = trimString_(row[0]);
    if (key) out[key] = row[1];
    return out;
  }, {});
  return control;
}

function readAppCatalog_(ss) {
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.catalog);
  if (sheet.getLastRow() <= 1) throw new Error('APP_CATALOG esta vacio. Importe el libro maestro antes de ejecutar.');
  const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.catalog.length).getValues()[0];
  const indexes = indexHeaders_(headers);
  const rows = sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, headers.length).getValues();
  const seen = {};
  const catalog = rows.filter(function (row) { return trimString_(row[indexes.app_key]); }).map(function (row) {
    const app = {
      appKey: trimString_(row[indexes.app_key]),
      platform: trimString_(row[indexes.platform]),
      store: trimString_(row[indexes.store]),
      countryCode: trimString_(row[indexes.country_code]).toUpperCase(),
      country: trimString_(row[indexes.country]),
      appName: trimString_(row[indexes.app_name]),
      appId: trimString_(row[indexes.app_id]),
      packageName: trimString_(row[indexes.package_name]),
      isBbva: parseBoolean_(row[indexes.is_bbva], false),
      enabled: parseBoolean_(row[indexes.enabled], true),
      locale: trimString_(row[indexes.locale]) || 'en',
      minObservations7d: toNonNegativeInteger_(row[indexes.min_observations_7d]) || 5,
      competitorAlerts: parseBoolean_(row[indexes.competitor_alerts], true),
      countryPlatformWeight: nonNegativeNumberOrZero_(row[indexes.country_platform_weight]),
      groupUserWeight: nonNegativeNumberOrZero_(row[indexes.group_user_weight]),
      displayOrder: toNonNegativeInteger_(row[indexes.display_order]),
      marketSegment: trimString_(row[indexes.market_segment]) || 'BANK',
      productScope: trimString_(row[indexes.product_scope]) || 'BUSINESS',
      weightSource: trimString_(row[indexes.weight_source]),
      weightEffectiveDate: row[indexes.weight_effective_date],
      commercialEligible: parseBoolean_(row[indexes.commercial_eligible], false),
      commercialEvidenceUrl: trimString_(row[indexes.commercial_evidence_url]),
      storeEvidenceUrl: trimString_(row[indexes.store_evidence_url]),
      verifiedAt: row[indexes.verified_at]
    };
    if (seen[app.appKey]) throw new Error('APP_KEY duplicada: ' + app.appKey);
    seen[app.appKey] = true;
    validateCatalogApp_(app);
    return app;
  });
  return catalog.filter(function (app) { return app.enabled; });
}

function replaceSheetData_(sheet, headers, rows) {
  if (sheet.getFilter()) sheet.getFilter().remove();
  sheet.clearContents();
  ensureSheetCapacity_(sheet, Math.max(2, rows.length + 1), headers.length);
  sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setValues([headers]);
  if (rows.length) sheet.getRange(2, 1, rows.length, headers.length).setValues(rows);
  applyTableStyle_(sheet, headers.length);
}

function validateCatalogApp_(app) {
  if (['Android', 'iOS', 'Huawei'].indexOf(app.platform) < 0) {
    throw new Error('Plataforma no soportada en ' + app.appKey + ': ' + app.platform);
  }
  if (!app.countryCode || !app.appName) throw new Error('Pais o aplicacion vacios en ' + app.appKey);
  if (app.platform === 'iOS' && !app.appId) throw new Error('APP_ID iOS vacio en ' + app.appKey);
  if ((app.platform === 'Android' || app.platform === 'Huawei') && !app.packageName) {
    throw new Error('PACKAGE_NAME vacio en ' + app.appKey);
  }
  if (['BANK', 'FINTECH'].indexOf(app.marketSegment) < 0) {
    throw new Error('MARKET_SEGMENT no soportado en ' + app.appKey + ': ' + app.marketSegment);
  }
  if (app.marketSegment !== 'BANK' && (!app.commercialEligible || app.productScope !== 'COMMERCIAL' ||
      !app.commercialEvidenceUrl || !app.storeEvidenceUrl || !app.verifiedAt)) {
    throw new Error('Competidor digital sin evidencia commercial completa en ' + app.appKey);
  }
}

function upsertRowsByKey_(sheet, rows, width) {
  if (!rows.length) return 0;
  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  const existingKeys = lastRow > 1 ? sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, 1).getValues() : [];
  const rowByKey = {};
  existingKeys.forEach(function (row, index) {
    const key = trimString_(row[0]);
    if (key) rowByKey[key] = index + 2;
  });
  const updates = [];
  const inserts = [];
  rows.forEach(function (row) {
    const existingRow = rowByKey[trimString_(row[0])];
    if (existingRow) updates.push({ rowNumber: existingRow, values: row });
    else inserts.push(row);
  });
  writeUpdatesInBlocks_(sheet, updates, width);
  if (inserts.length) {
    const start = sheet.getLastRow() + 1;
    ensureSheetCapacity_(sheet, start + inserts.length - 1, width);
    sheet.getRange(start, 1, inserts.length, width).setValues(inserts);
  }
  refreshFilter_(sheet, width);
  return updates.length + inserts.length;
}

function refreshFilter_(sheet, width) {
  if (sheet.getFilter()) sheet.getFilter().remove();
  sheet.getRange(1, 1, Math.max(2, sheet.getLastRow()), width).createFilter();
}

function writeUpdatesInBlocks_(sheet, updates, width) {
  if (!updates.length) return;
  updates.sort(function (a, b) { return a.rowNumber - b.rowNumber; });
  let block = [updates[0]];
  for (let i = 1; i < updates.length; i += 1) {
    if (updates[i].rowNumber === block[block.length - 1].rowNumber + 1) block.push(updates[i]);
    else {
      writeUpdateBlock_(sheet, block, width);
      block = [updates[i]];
    }
  }
  writeUpdateBlock_(sheet, block, width);
}

function writeUpdateBlock_(sheet, block, width) {
  sheet.getRange(block[0].rowNumber, 1, block.length, width)
    .setValues(block.map(function (item) { return item.values; }));
}

function appendRunLog_(ss, row) {
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.runs);
  ensureSheetCapacity_(sheet, sheet.getLastRow() + 1, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.runs.length);
  sheet.appendRow(row);
}

function appendFetchLog_(ss, runId, referenceDate, fetchedAt, results) {
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.fetchLog);
  const rows = (results || []).map(function (result) {
    const status = result.ok ? 'OK' : result.noRating ? 'NO_RATING' : 'ERROR';
    return [runId, referenceDate, fetchedAt, result.app.appKey, result.app.platform,
      result.app.countryCode, result.app.marketSegment, status, result.status || 0,
      result.ok ? result.rating : '', result.ok && result.ratingCount !== null ? result.ratingCount : '',
      Boolean(result.retryable), result.error || '', result.app.store + ' API'];
  });
  if (!rows.length) return 0;
  const start = sheet.getLastRow() + 1;
  ensureSheetCapacity_(sheet, start + rows.length - 1, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.fetchLog.length);
  sheet.getRange(start, 1, rows.length, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.fetchLog.length).setValues(rows);
  refreshFilter_(sheet, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.fetchLog.length);
  return rows.length;
}

ARCHIVO: 50_Alerts.gs
========================================

/** Evalua alertas personalizadas y envia un unico resumen BBVA por usuario. */
var MARKETS_ALERT_EMAIL = Object.freeze({
  senderName: 'BBVA Market Pulse',
  senderAddress: 'voc-commercial.group@bbva.com',
  senderMode: 'GROUP_ALIAS'
});

function buildAndProcessAlerts_(ss, referenceDate, catalog, runId, control, ratingObjects) {
  const ratings = ratingObjects || readRatingObjects_(ss);
  const subscribers = readAllMarketsAlertPreferences_(ss).filter(function (item) { return item.active; });
  const existing = readExistingAlertKeys_(ss);
  const catalogByKey = (catalog || []).reduce(function (out, app) { out[app.appKey] = app; return out; }, {});
  let events = [];
  subscribers.forEach(function (preferences) {
    events = events.concat(buildRatingDropAlerts_(ratings, referenceDate, preferences, existing));
    events = events.concat(buildCompetitiveCrossoverAlerts_(ratings, referenceDate, preferences, catalogByKey, existing));
  });
  events.forEach(function (event) { event.runId = runId; });
  events.subscriberCount = subscribers.length;
  events.deliveryCount = 0;
  events.deliveryStatus = events.length ? 'DISABLED' : 'NOT_REQUIRED';
  events.deliveryDetail = '';
  if (!events.length || !parseBoolean_(control.SEND_ALERT_EMAILS, false)) {
    appendAlertEvents_(ss, events);
    return events;
  }
  try {
    events.deliveryCount = sendAlertEmails_(events, ss);
    const delivered = events.deliveredRecipients || [];
    const acceptedEvents = events.filter(function (event) {
      return uniqueStrings_(trimString_(event.recipients).split(/[;,]/)).some(function (recipient) {
        return delivered.indexOf(normalizeEmailForComparison_(recipient)) >= 0;
      });
    });
    appendAlertEvents_(ss, acceptedEvents);
    const failed = events.deliveryFailures || [];
    events.deliveryStatus = failed.length ? (events.deliveryCount ? 'PARTIAL' : 'ERROR') : 'DELIVERED';
    events.deliveryDetail = failed.map(function (item) {
      return item.recipient + ': ' + item.error;
    }).join(' | ');
  } catch (error) {
    events.deliveryStatus = 'ERROR';
    events.deliveryDetail = errorToString_(error);
    console.error('Las alertas se conservaran para reintento porque Gmail no las ha aceptado: ' + events.deliveryDetail);
  }
  return events;
}

function buildRatingDropAlerts_(ratings, referenceDate, preferences, existing) {
  if (!preferences.active) return [];
  const definitions = marketsRatingDropDefinitions_(preferences);
  if (!definitions.length) return [];
  const byApp = marketsRatingSeriesByApp_(ratings, preferences);
  const referenceKey = dateKey_(referenceDate);
  return Object.keys(byApp).reduce(function (events, appKey) {
    const series = byApp[appKey];
    definitions.forEach(function (definition) {
      const observation = marketsRatingDropObservation_(series, referenceKey, definition);
      if (!observation) return;
      const baseline = observation.baseline;
      const latest = observation.latest;
      const drop = observation.drop;
      let severity = '';
      let threshold = null;
      if (drop >= definition.critical) {
        severity = 'CRITICAL'; threshold = definition.critical;
      } else if (drop >= definition.warning) {
        severity = 'WARNING'; threshold = definition.warning;
      }
      if (!severity) return;
      const eventParts = [definition.type, observation.periodKey, appKey, preferences.email, severity];
      if (preferences.repeatWhileBreached) eventParts.push(referenceKey);
      const eventKey = eventParts.join('|');
      if (existing[eventKey]) return;
      existing[eventKey] = true;
      events.push(marketsRatingDropEvent_(definition, observation, preferences, referenceDate, eventKey));
    });
    return events;
  }, []);
}

function marketsRatingDropDefinitions_(preferences) {
  return [
    { period: 'DAILY', enabled: preferences.dailyDropEnabled,
      warning: preferences.dailyWarningDrop, critical: preferences.dailyCriticalDrop,
      type: 'RATING_DROP_DAILY', ruleId: 'DAILY_DROP' },
    { period: 'MIDMONTH', enabled: preferences.mtdDropEnabled,
      warning: preferences.mtdWarningDrop, critical: preferences.mtdCriticalDrop,
      type: 'RATING_DROP_MTD', ruleId: 'MIDMONTH_DROP' },
    { period: 'YTD', enabled: preferences.ytdDropEnabled,
      warning: preferences.ytdWarningDrop, critical: preferences.ytdCriticalDrop,
      type: 'RATING_DROP_YTD', ruleId: 'YTD_DROP' }
  ].filter(function (definition) { return definition.enabled; });
}

function marketsRatingSeriesByApp_(ratings, preferences) {
  const byApp = {};
  (ratings || []).forEach(function (item) {
    if (!item.isBbva || !(item.observedDate instanceof Date) || item.rating === null ||
        !marketsAlertScopeMatches_(preferences, item)) return;
    (byApp[item.appKey] || (byApp[item.appKey] = [])).push(item);
  });
  Object.keys(byApp).forEach(function (appKey) {
    byApp[appKey].sort(function (a, b) { return a.observedDate - b.observedDate; });
  });
  return byApp;
}

function marketsRatingDropObservation_(series, referenceKey, definition) {
  const latest = ratingDropCurrent_(series, referenceKey, definition.period);
  if (!latest) return null;
  const baseline = ratingDropBaseline_(series, referenceKey, definition.period);
  if (!baseline || baseline.observedDate.getTime() >= latest.observedDate.getTime()) return null;
  const drop = round2_(baseline.rating - latest.rating);
  const periodKey = definition.period === 'DAILY' ? referenceKey :
    definition.period === 'MIDMONTH' ? marketsMidmonthAnchorKey_(referenceKey) : referenceKey.slice(0, 4);
  return { baseline: baseline, latest: latest, drop: drop, periodKey: periodKey };
}

function marketsRatingDropEvent_(definition, observation, preferences, referenceDate, eventKey) {
  const latest = observation.latest;
  return {
    timestamp: new Date(), eventKey: eventKey, referenceDate: referenceDate,
    platform: latest.platform, countryCode: latest.countryCode, type: definition.type,
    severity: observation.drop >= definition.critical ? 'CRITICAL' : 'WARNING',
    detail: ratingDropDetail_(definition.period, observation.baseline, latest, observation.drop),
    recipients: preferences.email, userEmail: preferences.email, ruleId: definition.ruleId,
    metricValue: observation.drop,
    threshold: observation.drop >= definition.critical ? definition.critical : definition.warning,
    warningThreshold: definition.warning, criticalThreshold: definition.critical,
    appKey: latest.appKey, direction: 'DOWN',
    runId: '', source: 'SELF_SERVICE_ALERTS', period: definition.period,
    appName: latest.appName || 'BBVA Empresas', country: latest.country || latest.countryCode,
    baselineRating: observation.baseline.rating, currentRating: latest.rating,
    baselineDate: observation.baseline.observedDate, currentDate: latest.observedDate
  };
}

/**
 * Al dar de alta a una cuenta no administradora reconoce las caídas YTD que
 * ya estaban activas. Se auditan, pero no se envían. Si la cuenta solicita
 * recordatorios, el siguiente día genera una clave nueva; una escalada
 * posterior de amarilla a roja también sigue siendo notificable.
 */
function buildMarketsEnrollmentYtdAcknowledgements_(ratings, referenceDate, preferences) {
  if (!preferences || !preferences.active || !preferences.ytdDropEnabled) return [];
  const baselinePreferences = Object.assign({}, preferences, {
    active: true,
    dailyDropEnabled: false,
    mtdDropEnabled: false,
    ytdDropEnabled: true
  });
  const events = buildRatingDropAlerts_(ratings, referenceDate, baselinePreferences, {});
  const acknowledged = [];
  events.forEach(function (event) {
    const levels = event.severity === 'CRITICAL' ? [
      { severity: 'WARNING', threshold: preferences.ytdWarningDrop },
      { severity: 'CRITICAL', threshold: preferences.ytdCriticalDrop }
    ] : [{ severity: event.severity, threshold: event.threshold }];
    levels.forEach(function (level) {
      const clone = Object.assign({}, event, level);
      const periodKey = dateKey_(referenceDate).slice(0, 4);
      const parts = ['RATING_DROP_YTD', periodKey, event.appKey, preferences.email, level.severity];
      if (preferences.repeatWhileBreached) parts.push(dateKey_(referenceDate));
      clone.eventKey = parts.join('|');
      clone.timestamp = new Date();
      clone.source = 'ADMIN_ENROLLMENT_BASELINE';
      clone.runId = 'ENROLL-' + Utilities.formatDate(clone.timestamp, MARKETS_CONFIG.timeZone, 'yyyyMMdd-HHmmss');
      clone.detail = 'Reconocida en el alta sin notificación. ' + event.detail;
      acknowledged.push(clone);
    });
  });
  return acknowledged;
}

function latestMarketsRatingDate_(ratings, preferences) {
  return (ratings || []).filter(function (item) {
    return item.isBbva && item.observedDate instanceof Date && item.rating !== null &&
      (!preferences || marketsAlertScopeMatches_(preferences, item));
  }).reduce(function (latest, item) {
    return !latest || item.observedDate.getTime() > latest.getTime() ? item.observedDate : latest;
  }, null);
}

function buildMarketsRealAlertPreviewEvents_(ratings, catalog, preferences, deliveryId) {
  const previewPreferences = Object.assign({}, preferences, { active: true, repeatWhileBreached: false });
  const referenceDate = latestMarketsRatingDate_(ratings, previewPreferences);
  if (!referenceDate) throw new Error('No hay ratings reales disponibles para el ámbito configurado.');
  const catalogByKey = (catalog || []).reduce(function (out, app) { out[app.appKey] = app; return out; }, {});
  let events = buildRatingDropAlerts_(ratings, referenceDate, previewPreferences, {});
  events = events.concat(buildCompetitiveCrossoverAlerts_(ratings, referenceDate, previewPreferences, catalogByKey, {}));
  if (!events.length) {
    events = buildMarketsBelowThresholdPreviewEvents_(ratings, referenceDate, previewPreferences);
  }
  const severityOrder = { CRITICAL: 0, WARNING: 1, PREVIEW: 2, POSITIVE: 3 };
  events.sort(function (a, b) {
    const aSeverity = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(severityOrder, a.severity) ?
      severityOrder[a.severity] : 9;
    const bSeverity = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(severityOrder, b.severity) ?
      severityOrder[b.severity] : 9;
    return aSeverity - bSeverity ||
      trimString_(a.countryCode).localeCompare(trimString_(b.countryCode)) ||
      compareMarketsPlatforms_(a, b);
  });
  if (!events.length) {
    const latest = (ratings || []).filter(function (item) {
      return item.isBbva && item.observedDate instanceof Date &&
        dateKey_(item.observedDate) === dateKey_(referenceDate) &&
        marketsAlertScopeMatches_(previewPreferences, item);
    }).sort(function (a, b) {
      return trimString_(a.countryCode).localeCompare(trimString_(b.countryCode)) ||
        compareMarketsPlatforms_(a, b);
    });
    if (!latest.length) throw new Error('No hay un cierre real utilizable para preparar la prueba.');
    events = latest.map(function (item) {
      return {
        timestamp: new Date(), referenceDate: referenceDate, currentDate: item.observedDate,
        platform: item.platform, countryCode: item.countryCode, country: item.country,
        type: 'TEST_SNAPSHOT', severity: 'POSITIVE', appKey: item.appKey,
        appName: item.appName || 'BBVA Empresas', currentRating: item.rating,
        ratingCount: item.ratingCount, metricValue: item.rating, threshold: null,
        detail: 'Ninguna regla activada ha superado los umbrales configurados en este cierre.',
        direction: 'SNAPSHOT'
      };
    });
  }
  const previewLimit = events.every(function (event) { return event.type === 'TEST_SNAPSHOT'; }) ? 24 : 8;
  return events.slice(0, previewLimit).map(function (event) {
    return Object.assign({}, event, {
      isTest: true, testId: deliveryId, recipients: preferences.email,
      userEmail: preferences.email, source: 'SELF_SERVICE_TEST_REAL_DATA',
      eventKey: 'TEST_PREVIEW|' + deliveryId + '|' + trimString_(event.eventKey || event.appKey)
    });
  });
}

function buildMarketsBelowThresholdPreviewEvents_(ratings, referenceDate, preferences) {
  const definitions = marketsRatingDropDefinitions_(preferences);
  const byApp = marketsRatingSeriesByApp_(ratings, preferences);
  const referenceKey = dateKey_(referenceDate);
  return Object.keys(byApp).reduce(function (candidates, appKey) {
    const observations = definitions.map(function (definition) {
      const observation = marketsRatingDropObservation_(byApp[appKey], referenceKey, definition);
      if (!observation || observation.drop <= 0 || observation.drop >= definition.warning) return null;
      return {
        definition: definition,
        observation: observation
      };
    }).filter(Boolean).sort(function (a, b) {
      return b.observation.drop - a.observation.drop;
    });
    if (!observations.length) return candidates;
    const selected = observations[0];
    const event = marketsRatingDropEvent_(
      selected.definition, selected.observation, preferences, referenceDate,
      ['TEST_BELOW_THRESHOLD', selected.observation.periodKey, appKey, selected.definition.ruleId].join('|'));
    event.type = selected.definition.type;
    event.severity = 'PREVIEW';
    event.threshold = selected.definition.warning;
    event.isBelowThresholdPreview = true;
    event.source = 'SELF_SERVICE_TEST_REAL_DATA';
    event.detail = ratingDropDetail_(
      selected.definition.period, selected.observation.baseline,
      selected.observation.latest, selected.observation.drop);
    candidates.push(event);
    return candidates;
  }, []);
}

function validateMarketsRealAlertPreviewEvents_(events, preferences) {
  if (!Array.isArray(events) || !events.length) {
    throw new Error('No hay datos reales utilizables para preparar la prueba.');
  }
  const hasNumber = function (value) { return toFiniteNumber_(value) !== null; };
  events.forEach(function (event) {
    const countryCode = trimString_(event && event.countryCode).toUpperCase();
    const platform = trimString_(event && event.platform);
    if (!countryCode || countryCode === 'GLOBAL' || !platform || platform.toUpperCase() === 'TODAS') {
      throw new Error('La prueba se ha cancelado: no existe una geografía y plataforma reales verificables.');
    }
    if (!marketsAlertScopeMatches_(preferences, { countryCode: countryCode, platform: platform })) {
      throw new Error('La prueba se ha cancelado: los datos no pertenecen al ámbito guardado.');
    }
    if (!(event.referenceDate instanceof Date) || !isFinite(event.referenceDate.getTime()) ||
        !trimString_(event.appKey)) {
      throw new Error('La prueba se ha cancelado: falta trazabilidad del cierre real.');
    }
    if (event.type === 'COMPETITIVE_CROSSOVER') {
      if (!hasNumber(event.bbvaRating) || !hasNumber(event.competitorRating)) {
        throw new Error('La prueba se ha cancelado: el cruce no contiene ratings reales comparables.');
      }
      return;
    }
    if (!hasNumber(event.currentRating)) {
      throw new Error('La prueba se ha cancelado: el cierre no contiene un rating real.');
    }
    if (event.type !== 'TEST_SNAPSHOT' &&
        (!hasNumber(event.baselineRating) || !hasNumber(event.metricValue) ||
         !hasNumber(event.threshold))) {
      throw new Error('La prueba se ha cancelado: la señal no puede justificarse con sus datos reales.');
    }
  });
  return events;
}

function ratingDropBaseline_(series, referenceKey, period) {
  if (period === 'MIDMONTH') {
    return marketsRatingAtDate_(series, marketsPreviousMidmonthAnchorKey_(referenceKey));
  }
  const previous = (series || []).filter(function (item) { return dateKey_(item.observedDate) < referenceKey; });
  if (period === 'DAILY') return previous.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return b.observedDate - a.observedDate; })[0] || null;
  const start = referenceKey.slice(0, 4) + '-01-01';
  return previous.filter(function (item) { return dateKey_(item.observedDate) >= start; })[0] || null;
}

function ratingDropCurrent_(series, referenceKey, period) {
  if (period === 'MIDMONTH') {
    return marketsRatingAtDate_(series, marketsMidmonthAnchorKey_(referenceKey));
  }
  return (series || []).find(function (item) { return dateKey_(item.observedDate) === referenceKey; }) || null;
}

function marketsRatingAtDate_(series, targetKey) {
  return (series || []).find(function (item) {
    return dateKey_(item.observedDate) === targetKey;
  }) || null;
}

function marketsMidmonthAnchorKey_(referenceKey) {
  const parts = trimString_(referenceKey).split('-').map(Number);
  const date = new Date(parts[0], parts[1] - 1, parts[2], 12);
  if (parts[2] < 15) date.setMonth(date.getMonth() - 1);
  date.setDate(15);
  return dateKey_(date);
}

function marketsPreviousMidmonthAnchorKey_(referenceKey) {
  const anchor = marketsMidmonthAnchorKey_(referenceKey).split('-').map(Number);
  const date = new Date(anchor[0], anchor[1] - 1, 15, 12);
  date.setMonth(date.getMonth() - 1);
  return dateKey_(date);
}

function ratingDropDetail_(period, baseline, latest, drop) {
  const label = period === 'DAILY' ? 'desde la observacion anterior' :
    period === 'MIDMONTH' ? 'en la ventana mensual del día 15 al día 15' : 'desde inicio de ano';
  return 'BBVA Empresas: ' + label + ' ' + baseline.rating.toFixed(2) + ' -> ' +
    latest.rating.toFixed(2) + ' (caida ' + drop.toFixed(2) + ')';
}

function buildCompetitiveCrossoverAlerts_(ratings, referenceDate, preferences, catalogByKey, existing) {
  if (!preferences.active || !preferences.crossoverEnabled) return [];
  const referenceKey = dateKey_(referenceDate);
  const byMarket = {};
  (ratings || []).forEach(function (item) {
    if (!(item.observedDate instanceof Date) || item.rating === null || !marketsAlertScopeMatches_(preferences, item)) return;
    const config = catalogByKey[item.appKey];
    if (config && config.competitorAlerts === false) return;
    const key = item.countryCode + '|' + item.platform;
    (byMarket[key] || (byMarket[key] = [])).push(item);
  });
  return Object.keys(byMarket).reduce(function (events, marketKey) {
    const rows = byMarket[marketKey];
    const current = rows.filter(function (item) { return dateKey_(item.observedDate) === referenceKey; });
    const bbva = current.find(function (item) { return item.isBbva; });
    if (!bbva) return events;
    current.filter(function (item) { return !item.isBbva; }).forEach(function (peer) {
      const previousPair = previousCommonAlertPair_(rows, bbva.appKey, peer.appKey, referenceKey);
      if (!previousPair) return;
      const previousDifference = round2_(previousPair.bbva.rating - previousPair.peer.rating);
      const currentDifference = round2_(bbva.rating - peer.rating);
      let direction = '';
      if (previousDifference >= 0 && currentDifference < 0) direction = 'LOST';
      else if (previousDifference <= 0 && currentDifference > 0) direction = 'GAINED';
      if (!direction || (preferences.crossoverDirection !== 'BOTH' && preferences.crossoverDirection !== direction)) return;
      const eventKey = ['COMPETITIVE_CROSSOVER', referenceKey, bbva.appKey, peer.appKey,
        preferences.email, direction].join('|');
      if (existing[eventKey]) return;
      existing[eventKey] = true;
      const peerName = peer.appName || peer.appKey;
      events.push({
        timestamp: new Date(), eventKey: eventKey, referenceDate: referenceDate,
        platform: bbva.platform, countryCode: bbva.countryCode, type: 'COMPETITIVE_CROSSOVER',
        severity: direction === 'LOST' ? 'WARNING' : 'POSITIVE',
        detail: direction === 'LOST' ? peerName + ' ha superado a BBVA Empresas (' +
          peer.rating.toFixed(2) + ' vs ' + bbva.rating.toFixed(2) + ')' :
          'BBVA Empresas ha superado a ' + peerName + ' (' + bbva.rating.toFixed(2) +
          ' vs ' + peer.rating.toFixed(2) + ')',
        recipients: preferences.email, userEmail: preferences.email, ruleId: 'COMPETITIVE_CROSSOVER',
        metricValue: currentDifference, threshold: 0, appKey: peer.appKey, direction: direction,
        runId: '', source: 'SELF_SERVICE_ALERTS', appName: bbva.appName || 'BBVA Empresas',
        country: bbva.country || bbva.countryCode, bbvaRating: bbva.rating,
        competitorName: peerName, competitorRating: peer.rating,
        previousBbvaRating: previousPair.bbva.rating, previousCompetitorRating: previousPair.peer.rating,
        baselineDate: previousPair.bbva.observedDate, currentDate: bbva.observedDate
      });
    });
    return events;
  }, []);
}

function previousCommonAlertPair_(rows, bbvaKey, peerKey, referenceKey) {
  const byDate = {};
  (rows || []).filter(function (item) { return dateKey_(item.observedDate) < referenceKey; })
    .forEach(function (item) {
      const key = dateKey_(item.observedDate);
      const bucket = byDate[key] || (byDate[key] = {});
      if (item.appKey === bbvaKey) bucket.bbva = item;
      if (item.appKey === peerKey) bucket.peer = item;
    });
  const date = Object.keys(byDate).filter(function (key) { return byDate[key].bbva && byDate[key].peer; }).sort().pop();
  return date ? byDate[date] : null;
}

function marketsAlertScopeMatches_(preferences, item) {
  return (preferences.countries.indexOf('ALL') >= 0 || preferences.countries.indexOf(item.countryCode) >= 0) &&
    (preferences.platforms.indexOf('ALL') >= 0 || preferences.platforms.indexOf(item.platform) >= 0);
}

function readExistingAlertKeys_(ss) {
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.alerts);
  if (sheet.getLastRow() <= 1) return {};
  const failedRuns = readFailedAlertRunIds_(ss);
  return sheet.getRange(2, 2, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 9).getValues().reduce(function (out, row) {
    const key = trimString_(row[0]);
    const runId = trimString_(row[8]);
    if (key && !failedRuns[runId]) out[key] = true;
    return out;
  }, {});
}

function readFailedAlertRunIds_(ss) {
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.runs);
  if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() <= 1) return {};
  const width = Math.min(sheet.getLastColumn(), MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.runs.length);
  const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, width).getValues()[0];
  const indexes = indexHeaders_(headers);
  return sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, width).getValues().reduce(function (out, row) {
    const runId = trimString_(row[indexes.run_id]);
    const status = trimString_(row[indexes.status]).toUpperCase();
    const delivery = indexes.alert_delivery_status === undefined ? '' :
      trimString_(row[indexes.alert_delivery_status]).toUpperCase();
    if (runId && (['ERROR', 'PARTIAL'].indexOf(delivery) >= 0 || (!delivery && status === 'ERROR'))) {
      out[runId] = true;
    }
    return out;
  }, {});
}

function appendAlertEvents_(ss, events) {
  if (!events.length) return;
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.alerts);
  const rows = events.map(function (event) {
    return [event.timestamp, event.eventKey, event.referenceDate, event.platform, event.countryCode,
      event.type, event.severity, event.detail, event.recipients, event.runId, event.source,
      event.userEmail, event.ruleId, event.metricValue, event.threshold, event.appKey, event.direction];
  });
  ensureSheetCapacity_(sheet, sheet.getLastRow() + rows.length, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.alerts.length);
  sheet.getRange(sheet.getLastRow() + 1, 1, rows.length, rows[0].length).setValues(rows);
}

function sendAlertEmails_(events, ss) {
  const grouped = {};
  (events || []).forEach(function (event) {
    uniqueStrings_(trimString_(event.recipients).split(/[;,]/)).forEach(function (recipient) {
      (grouped[recipient] || (grouped[recipient] = [])).push(event);
    });
  });
  const recipients = Object.keys(grouped);
  if (!recipients.length) return 0;
  const sender = requireMarketsAlertSender_(ss);
  const inlineImages = buildMarketsAlertInlineImages_();
  const deliveries = [];
  const failures = [];
  recipients.forEach(function (recipient) {
    try {
      const recipientEvents = grouped[recipient];
      const message = marketsAlertEmailMessage_(recipient, recipientEvents, inlineImages, sender);
      const accepted = Gmail.Users.Messages.send({ raw: message.raw }, 'me');
      if (!accepted || !trimString_(accepted.id)) {
        throw new Error('Gmail no ha devuelto un identificador de mensaje.');
      }
      deliveries.push({
        recipient: recipient, messageId: trimString_(accepted.id), threadId: trimString_(accepted.threadId),
        senderAddress: sender.address, senderName: sender.name,
        verificationStatus: sender.verificationStatus
      });
      if (ss) {
        try { touchMarketsAlertTimestamp_(ss, recipient, 'LAST_DELIVERY_AT', new Date()); }
        catch (auditError) { console.error('Email aceptado para ' + recipient + ', pero fallo LAST_DELIVERY_AT: ' + errorToString_(auditError)); }
      }
    } catch (error) {
      failures.push({ recipient: normalizeEmailForComparison_(recipient), error: errorToString_(error) });
    }
  });
  events.deliveryResults = deliveries;
  events.deliveryFailures = failures;
  events.deliveredRecipients = deliveries.map(function (item) {
    return normalizeEmailForComparison_(item.recipient);
  });
  return deliveries.length;
}

function marketsAlertEmailMessage_(recipient, events, inlineImages, sender) {
  const normalizedRecipient = normalizeMarketsAlertEmail_(recipient);
  const subject = marketsAlertEmailSubject_(events);
  const plainText = renderMarketsAlertPlainText_(events, normalizedRecipient);
  const html = renderMarketsAlertEmail_(events, normalizedRecipient);
  const image = inlineImages && inlineImages.bbvaLogo;
  if (!image || typeof image.getBytes !== 'function') {
    throw new Error('No se ha podido incorporar el logotipo oficial de BBVA al correo.');
  }
  const token = Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, '');
  const relatedBoundary = 'bbva_related_' + token;
  const alternativeBoundary = 'bbva_alternative_' + token;
  const crlf = '\r\n';
  const mime = [
    'From: ' + marketsAlertEncodedHeader_(sender.name) + ' <' + sender.address + '>',
    'To: ' + normalizedRecipient,
    'Subject: ' + marketsAlertEncodedHeader_(subject),
    'Date: ' + new Date().toUTCString(),
    'Message-ID: <' + token + '@voc-commercial.bbva.com>',
    'X-Market-Pulse-Code-Version: ' + MARKETS_CONFIG.codeVersion,
    'X-Market-Pulse-Web-Version: ' + marketsAlertWebVersion_(),
    'MIME-Version: 1.0',
    'Content-Type: multipart/related; boundary="' + relatedBoundary + '"',
    '',
    '--' + relatedBoundary,
    'Content-Type: multipart/alternative; boundary="' + alternativeBoundary + '"',
    '',
    '--' + alternativeBoundary,
    'Content-Type: text/plain; charset="UTF-8"',
    'Content-Transfer-Encoding: base64',
    '',
    marketsAlertMimeText_(plainText),
    '--' + alternativeBoundary,
    'Content-Type: text/html; charset="UTF-8"',
    'Content-Transfer-Encoding: base64',
    '',
    marketsAlertMimeText_(html),
    '--' + alternativeBoundary + '--',
    '',
    '--' + relatedBoundary,
    'Content-Type: image/png; name="bbva.png"',
    'Content-Transfer-Encoding: base64',
    'Content-ID: <bbvaLogo>',
    'Content-Disposition: inline; filename="bbva.png"',
    '',
    marketsAlertMimeBytes_(image.getBytes()),
    '--' + relatedBoundary + '--',
    ''
  ].join(crlf);
  return {
    raw: Utilities.base64EncodeWebSafe(mime, Utilities.Charset.UTF_8).replace(/=+$/g, ''),
    recipient: normalizedRecipient,
    subject: subject
  };
}

function marketsAlertEncodedHeader_(value) {
  return '=?UTF-8?B?' + Utilities.base64Encode(trimString_(value), Utilities.Charset.UTF_8) + '?=';
}

function marketsAlertMimeText_(value) {
  return marketsAlertWrapBase64_(Utilities.base64Encode(String(value || ''), Utilities.Charset.UTF_8));
}

function marketsAlertMimeBytes_(bytes) {
  return marketsAlertWrapBase64_(Utilities.base64Encode(bytes));
}

function marketsAlertWrapBase64_(value) {
  return (String(value || '').match(/.{1,76}/g) || ['']).join('\r\n');
}

function marketsAlertConfiguredSenderAddress_(ss) {
  let address = MARKETS_ALERT_EMAIL.senderAddress;
  if (ss) {
    const control = readControl_(ss);
    address = trimString_(control.ALERT_SENDER_ALIAS) || address;
  }
  return normalizeMarketsAlertEmail_(address);
}

function marketsAlertSenderStatus_(ss) {
  const address = marketsAlertConfiguredSenderAddress_(ss);
  let response;
  try {
    response = Gmail.Users.Settings.SendAs.list('me');
  } catch (error) {
    return {
      address: address, name: MARKETS_ALERT_EMAIL.senderName, mode: MARKETS_ALERT_EMAIL.senderMode,
      available: false, verificationStatus: 'unavailable', error: errorToString_(error)
    };
  }
  const sendAs = (response && response.sendAs || []).find(function (item) {
    return normalizeEmailForComparison_(item.sendAsEmail) === normalizeEmailForComparison_(address);
  });
  const verificationStatus = trimString_(sendAs && sendAs.verificationStatus).toLowerCase() || 'unknown';
  return {
    address: address, name: MARKETS_ALERT_EMAIL.senderName, mode: MARKETS_ALERT_EMAIL.senderMode,
    available: Boolean(sendAs) && verificationStatus === 'accepted',
    verificationStatus: verificationStatus, isDefault: Boolean(sendAs && sendAs.isDefault),
    treatAsAlias: Boolean(sendAs && sendAs.treatAsAlias), error: ''
  };
}

function requireMarketsAlertSender_(ss) {
  const status = marketsAlertSenderStatus_(ss);
  if (status.available) return status;
  if (status.verificationStatus === 'pending') {
    throw new Error('El remitente ' + status.address + ' sigue pendiente de verificacion en Gmail > Enviar como. Confirma el mensaje recibido por el grupo antes de probar.');
  }
  if (status.verificationStatus === 'unavailable') {
    throw new Error('No se ha podido validar el remitente de grupo ' + status.address + ': ' + status.error);
  }
  throw new Error('El remitente ' + status.address + ' no esta autorizado en Gmail > Enviar como para la cuenta que ejecuta el trigger. Añadelo, verifica el grupo y vuelve a probar.');
}

function validateMarketsAlertSender() {
  const ss = openMarketsSpreadsheet_();
  ensureWorkbookSchema_(ss);
  const status = marketsAlertSenderStatus_(ss);
  let executor = '';
  try { executor = normalizeEmailForComparison_(Session.getEffectiveUser().getEmail()); } catch (ignored) {}
  return Object.assign({ executor: executor }, status);
}

function marketsAlertEmailSubject_(events) {
  const tests = (events || []).filter(function (event) { return event.type === 'TEST' || event.isTest; });
  if (tests.length === (events || []).length && tests.length) {
    const result = marketsAlertPreviewHasNoSignals_(events) ? 'Sin alertas activas' :
      marketsAlertPreviewIsBelowThreshold_(events) ? 'Datos reales bajo umbral' : 'Datos reales';
