    return '[PRUEBA] Market Pulse · ' + result + ' · ' +
      trimString_(tests[0].testId || tests[0].ruleId || 'verificación');
  }
  return '[Market Pulse] Alertas personalizadas · ' + events.length + ' cambio' +
    (events.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' en ratings';
}

function renderMarketsAlertPlainText_(events, recipient) {
  const isTest = marketsAlertIsTest_(events);
  const noSignals = marketsAlertPreviewHasNoSignals_(events);
  const appUrl = marketsWebAppUrl_();
  const loginUrl = marketsWebAppLoginUrl_(recipient);
  const lines = (events || []).map(function (event) {
    if (event.type === 'TEST_SNAPSHOT') {
      return 'CIERRE REAL — NINGUNA REGLA ACTIVADA\n' +
        [event.country || event.countryCode, event.platform].filter(Boolean).join(' · ') +
        '\nRating actual: ' + marketsAlertNumberLabel_(event.currentRating) +
        (event.ratingCount === null || event.ratingCount === undefined ? '' :
          '\nValoraciones acumuladas: ' + Math.round(event.ratingCount)) +
        '\nLectura: ' + trimString_(event.detail);
    }
    if (event.isBelowThresholdPreview) {
      return '[PRUEBA · BAJO UMBRAL] ' + marketsAlertCardTitle_(event) +
        '\nMercado y plataforma: ' +
        [event.country || event.countryCode, event.platform].filter(Boolean).join(' · ') +
        '\nMovimiento real: ' + trimString_(event.detail) +
        '\nUmbral amarillo configurado: ' + marketsAlertNumberLabel_(event.threshold) + ' puntos' +
        '\nEn producción: no se enviaría esta alerta; solo se enviará al alcanzar o superar ese límite.';
    }
    return '[' + marketsAlertSeverityLabel_(event.severity).toUpperCase() + '] ' +
      marketsAlertCardTitle_(event) + '\nMercado y plataforma: ' +
      [event.country || event.countryCode, event.platform].filter(Boolean).join(' · ') + '\nQué ha cambiado: ' +
      trimString_(event.detail) + '\nUmbral configurado: ' + marketsAlertNumberLabel_(event.threshold) + ' puntos\n' +
      'Recomendación: ' + marketsAlertRecommendation_(event);
  });
  return 'MARKET PULSE\nBBVA Banca de Empresas e Instituciones\n\n' +
    (noSignals ? 'Sin alertas activas en el ámbito seleccionado\n' :
      marketsAlertPreviewIsBelowThreshold_(events) ? 'Prueba con movimientos reales bajo umbral\n' :
        'Alerta por cambios en ratings\n') +
    (isTest ? 'Vista previa construida exclusivamente con el último cierre real y la configuración vigente. No genera una alerta operativa.\n\n' : '\n') +
    lines.join('\n\n') + '\n\nDatos obtenidos: ' + marketsAlertDataDateLabel_(events) +
    '\nVersión: backend ' + MARKETS_CONFIG.codeVersion + ' / web ' + marketsAlertWebVersion_() +
    '\nGestiona tus alertas desde Market Pulse.\nAbrir con la cuenta corporativa: ' + loginUrl + '\nURL directa: ' + appUrl;
}

function renderMarketsAlertEmail_(events, recipient) {
  const isTest = marketsAlertIsTest_(events);
  const noSignals = marketsAlertPreviewHasNoSignals_(events);
  const critical = (events || []).filter(function (event) { return event.severity === 'CRITICAL'; }).length;
  const warning = (events || []).filter(function (event) { return event.severity === 'WARNING'; }).length;
  const positive = (events || []).filter(function (event) { return event.severity === 'POSITIVE'; }).length;
  const cards = (events || []).map(renderMarketsAlertEmailCard_).join('');
  const appUrl = marketsWebAppUrl_();
  const loginUrl = marketsWebAppLoginUrl_(recipient);
  const safeAppUrl = escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(appUrl);
  const safeLoginUrl = escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(loginUrl);
  const cta = '<table role="presentation" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0"><tr><td bgcolor="#001391" style="border-radius:8px;"><a href="' + safeLoginUrl + '" target="_blank" style="display:block;padding:13px 22px;border-radius:8px;color:#FFFFFF;text-decoration:none;font-weight:700;">Ir a Market Pulse</a></td></tr></table>' +
    '<p style="margin:12px 0 0;color:#46536D;font-size:11px;line-height:17px;">Si el botón no se abre, utiliza este <a href="' + safeAppUrl + '" target="_blank" style="color:#001391;font-weight:700;">acceso directo a Market Pulse</a>.</p>';
  const subtitle = noSignals ? 'El último cierre real del ámbito seleccionado no ha superado ninguna de las condiciones configuradas.' :
    marketsAlertPreviewIsBelowThreshold_(events) ?
      'Prueba construida con movimientos reales del ámbito guardado. En producción no se enviaría: ninguno alcanza el límite configurado.' :
    isTest ? 'Vista previa con el último cierre real disponible y la configuración vigente. No genera una alerta operativa.' :
    events.length + ' cambio' + (events.length === 1 ? ' ha' : 's han') + ' superado las condiciones configuradas. Contexto ejecutivo para valorar la situación.';
  const summary = isTest ? renderMarketsAlertTestSummary_(events) :
    '<table role="presentation" width="100%" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0"><tr>' +
    marketsAlertSummaryCell_('Críticas', critical, '#B92A45', '#F9E9ED') +
    marketsAlertSummaryCell_('Advertencias', warning, '#946200', '#FFF4CC') +
    marketsAlertSummaryCell_('Positivas', positive, '#277A49', '#E6F4EA') + '</tr></table>';
  return '<!doctype html><html><body style="margin:0;background:#F7F8F8;font-family:\'BBVABentonSans\',\'Benton Sans BBVA\',Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif;color:#000519;">' +
    '<table role="presentation" width="100%" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="background:#F7F8F8;"><tr><td align="center" style="padding:28px 12px;">' +
    '<table role="presentation" width="100%" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="width:100%;max-width:640px;background:#FFFFFF;border-radius:16px;overflow:hidden;">' +
    '<tr><td width="100%" style="padding:24px 28px;background:#001391;"><img src="cid:bbvaLogo" width="156" alt="BBVA Banca de Empresas e Instituciones" style="display:block;width:156px;height:auto;"><div style="margin-top:22px;color:#8BE1E9;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:1px;text-transform:uppercase;">MARKET PULSE · ' + (isTest ? 'PRUEBA DE CANAL' : 'ALERTAS DE RATINGS') + '</div><h1 style="margin:8px 0 0;color:#FFFFFF;font-size:28px;line-height:34px;">' + (noSignals ? 'Sin alertas activas' : marketsAlertPreviewIsBelowThreshold_(events) ? 'Prueba con datos reales' : 'Alerta por cambios en ratings') + '</h1><p style="margin:10px 0 0;color:#85C8FF;font-size:15px;line-height:22px;">' + escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(subtitle) + '</p></td></tr>' +
    '<tr><td width="100%" style="padding:22px 28px 8px;">' + summary + '</td></tr>' +
    '<tr><td width="100%" style="padding:14px 28px 8px;">' + cards + '</td></tr>' +
    '<tr><td width="100%" style="padding:18px 28px 30px;">' + cta + '<p style="margin:18px 0 0;color:#46536D;font-size:12px;line-height:18px;">Este correo es privado y contiene únicamente señales configuradas para tu cuenta. Las preferencias de otros usuarios nunca son visibles.</p><p style="margin:7px 0 0;color:#ADB8C2;font-size:10px;line-height:15px;">Datos obtenidos el ' + escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(marketsAlertDataDateLabel_(events)) + ' · backend ' + escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(MARKETS_CONFIG.codeVersion) + ' / web ' + escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(marketsAlertWebVersion_()) + '.</p></td></tr>' +
    '<tr><td width="100%" style="padding:16px 28px;background:#070E46;color:#85C8FF;font-size:11px;line-height:17px;"><strong style="color:#FFFFFF;">Market Pulse</strong><br>BBVA Banca de Empresas e Instituciones · Ratings y valoraciones públicas.</td></tr>' +
    '</table></td></tr></table></body></html>';
}

function marketsAlertIsTest_(events) {
  return Boolean((events || []).length) && (events || []).every(function (event) {
    return event.type === 'TEST' || Boolean(event.isTest);
  });
}

function marketsAlertPreviewHasNoSignals_(events) {
  return Boolean((events || []).length) && (events || []).every(function (event) {
    return event.type === 'TEST_SNAPSHOT';
  });
}

function marketsAlertPreviewIsBelowThreshold_(events) {
  return Boolean((events || []).length) && (events || []).every(function (event) {
    return Boolean(event.isBelowThresholdPreview);
  });
}

function marketsAlertWebVersion_() {
  return typeof MARKETS_WEB !== 'undefined' && trimString_(MARKETS_WEB.version) ?
    trimString_(MARKETS_WEB.version) : 'unknown';
}

function renderMarketsAlertTestSummary_(events) {
  if (marketsAlertPreviewHasNoSignals_(events)) {
    return '<div style="padding:16px 18px;border-radius:8px;background:#EAFBFA;color:#070E46;">' +
      '<strong style="display:block;font-size:17px;line-height:23px;">Sin alertas en el último cierre</strong>' +
      '<span style="display:block;margin-top:4px;color:#46536D;font-size:13px;line-height:19px;">Se han evaluado ' +
      events.length + ' series reales del ámbito guardado y ninguna ha superado los umbrales configurados.</span></div>';
  }
  if (marketsAlertPreviewIsBelowThreshold_(events)) {
    return '<div style="padding:16px 18px;border-radius:8px;background:#EAFBFA;color:#070E46;">' +
      '<strong style="display:block;font-size:17px;line-height:23px;">Prueba real · todavía bajo el umbral</strong>' +
      '<span style="display:block;margin-top:4px;color:#46536D;font-size:13px;line-height:19px;">Se muestran únicamente caídas observadas en los mercados y plataformas guardados. En una ejecución real no se enviaría ninguna hasta alcanzar el límite configurado.</span></div>';
  }
  return '<div style="padding:16px 18px;border-radius:8px;background:#EAFBFA;color:#070E46;">' +
    '<strong style="display:block;font-size:17px;line-height:23px;">Vista previa · datos reales</strong>' +
    '<span style="display:block;margin-top:4px;color:#46536D;font-size:13px;line-height:19px;">El contenido procede del último cierre disponible y aplica el ámbito y los umbrales guardados. Esta prueba no se registra como alerta operativa.</span></div>';
}

function marketsAlertSummaryCell_(label, value, color, background) {
  return '<td width="33.33%" style="padding:0 5px;"><div style="padding:14px;border-radius:8px;background:' + background + ';color:' + color + ';text-align:center;"><strong style="display:block;font-size:22px;line-height:26px;">' + value + '</strong><span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;">' + label + '</span></div></td>';
}

function renderMarketsAlertEmailCard_(event) {
  if (event.type === 'TEST_SNAPSHOT') return renderMarketsAlertSnapshotCard_(event);
  if (event.isBelowThresholdPreview) return renderMarketsBelowThresholdPreviewCard_(event);
  const palette = event.severity === 'CRITICAL' ? ['#B92A45', '#F9E9ED', 'Crítico'] :
    event.severity === 'WARNING' ? ['#946200', '#FFF4CC', 'Advertencia'] : ['#277A49', '#E6F4EA', 'Movimiento positivo'];
  const market = [event.country || event.countryCode, event.platform].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
  const title = marketsAlertCardTitle_(event);
  const metrics = marketsAlertMetricsHtml_(event, palette);
  return '<div style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;margin:0 0 12px;padding:18px;border-left:5px solid ' + palette[0] + ';border-radius:8px;background:' + palette[1] + ';">' +
    '<div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.7px;text-transform:uppercase;color:' + palette[0] + ';">' + palette[2] + ' · ' + escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(marketsAlertRuleLabel_(event.type)) + '</div>' +
    '<div style="margin-top:7px;color:#070E46;font-size:18px;font-weight:700;line-height:24px;">' + escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(title) + '</div>' +
    '<div style="margin-top:3px;color:#46536D;font-size:13px;line-height:19px;">' + escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(market) + '</div>' + metrics +
    '<div style="margin-top:12px;padding-top:11px;border-top:1px solid rgba(0,19,145,.12);color:#46536D;font-size:13px;line-height:19px;"><strong style="color:#070E46;">Qué significa:</strong> ' + escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(marketsAlertReason_(event)) + '<br><strong style="color:#070E46;">Siguiente paso:</strong> ' + escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(marketsAlertRecommendation_(event)) + '</div></div>';
}

function renderMarketsBelowThresholdPreviewCard_(event) {
  const market = [event.country || event.countryCode, event.platform].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
  return '<div style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;margin:0 0 12px;padding:18px;border-left:5px solid #001391;border-radius:8px;background:#EAFBFA;">' +
    '<div style="color:#001391;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.7px;text-transform:uppercase;">PRUEBA · MOVIMIENTO REAL BAJO UMBRAL · ' +
    escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(marketsAlertRuleLabel_(event.type)) + '</div>' +
    '<div style="margin-top:7px;color:#070E46;font-size:18px;font-weight:700;line-height:24px;">' +
    escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(marketsAlertCardTitle_(event)) + '</div>' +
    '<div style="margin-top:3px;color:#46536D;font-size:13px;line-height:19px;">' +
    escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(market) + '</div>' + marketsAlertMetricsHtml_(event, ['#001391', '#EAFBFA']) +
    '<div style="margin-top:12px;padding-top:11px;border-top:1px solid rgba(0,19,145,.12);color:#46536D;font-size:13px;line-height:19px;"><strong style="color:#070E46;">Qué significa:</strong> La caída real es de ' +
    escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(marketsAlertNumberLabel_(event.metricValue)) + ' puntos. El nivel amarillo está configurado en ' +
    escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(marketsAlertNumberLabel_(event.threshold)) +
    ' puntos.<br><strong style="color:#070E46;">En producción:</strong> este correo no se enviaría; la alerta solo se activa al alcanzar o superar el límite configurado.</div></div>';
}

function renderMarketsAlertSnapshotCard_(event) {
  const market = [event.country || event.countryCode, event.platform].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
  const count = event.ratingCount === null || event.ratingCount === undefined ? 'No disponible' :
    marketsAlertIntegerLabel_(event.ratingCount);
  return '<div style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;margin:0 0 12px;padding:18px;border-left:5px solid #001391;border-radius:8px;background:#EAFBFA;">' +
    '<div style="color:#001391;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.7px;text-transform:uppercase;">ÚLTIMO CIERRE REAL · SIN UMBRALES SUPERADOS</div>' +
    '<div style="margin-top:7px;color:#070E46;font-size:18px;font-weight:700;line-height:24px;">' +
    escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(market) + '</div><table role="presentation" width="100%" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="margin-top:13px;background:#FFFFFF;border-radius:8px;"><tr>' +
    marketsAlertMetricCell_('Rating actual', marketsAlertNumberLabel_(event.currentRating), marketsAlertDateLabel_(event.currentDate)) +
    marketsAlertMetricCell_('Valoraciones', count, 'acumuladas') +
    marketsAlertMetricCell_('Estado', 'Sin señal', 'según la configuración') +
    '</tr></table><div style="margin-top:12px;color:#46536D;font-size:13px;line-height:19px;">' +
    escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(event.detail) + '</div></div>';
}

function marketsAlertCardTitle_(event) {
  const app = trimString_(event.appName || 'BBVA Empresas');
  if (event.type === 'COMPETITIVE_CROSSOVER') {
    return event.direction === 'LOST' ? trimString_(event.competitorName) + ' ha superado a ' + app :
      app + ' ha recuperado posición frente a ' + trimString_(event.competitorName);
  }
  return 'BBVA ' + marketsAlertGeographyLabel_(event) + ' cae ' + marketsAlertNumberLabel_(event.metricValue) + ' puntos';
}

function marketsAlertGeographyLabel_(event) {
  return trimString_(event.country || event.countryCode || 'mercado');
}

function marketsAlertMetricsHtml_(event, palette) {
  if (event.type === 'COMPETITIVE_CROSSOVER') {
    return '<table role="presentation" width="100%" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="margin-top:13px;background:#FFFFFF;border-radius:8px;"><tr>' +
      marketsAlertMetricCell_('BBVA', marketsAlertNumberLabel_(event.bbvaRating), 'rating') +
      marketsAlertMetricCell_(trimString_(event.competitorName || 'Competidor'), marketsAlertNumberLabel_(event.competitorRating), 'rating') +
      marketsAlertMetricCell_('Brecha', marketsAlertSignedNumberLabel_(event.metricValue), 'puntos') + '</tr></table>';
  }
  return '<table role="presentation" width="100%" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="margin-top:13px;background:#FFFFFF;border-radius:8px;"><tr>' +
    marketsAlertMetricCell_('Rating anterior', marketsAlertNumberLabel_(event.baselineRating), marketsAlertDateLabel_(event.baselineDate)) +
    marketsAlertMetricCell_('Rating actual', marketsAlertNumberLabel_(event.currentRating), marketsAlertDateLabel_(event.currentDate)) +
    marketsAlertMetricCell_('Caída / límite', marketsAlertNumberLabel_(event.metricValue), 'límite ' + marketsAlertNumberLabel_(event.threshold)) + '</tr></table>';
}

function marketsAlertMetricCell_(label, value, note) {
  return '<td width="33.33%" style="padding:12px 9px;text-align:center;"><span style="display:block;color:#46536D;font-size:10px;text-transform:uppercase;">' + escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(label) + '</span><strong style="display:block;margin-top:3px;color:#070E46;font-size:18px;line-height:22px;">' + escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(value) + '</strong><small style="display:block;margin-top:2px;color:#46536D;font-size:10px;line-height:14px;">' + escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(note) + '</small></td>';
}

function marketsAlertSeverityLabel_(severity) {
  return severity === 'CRITICAL' ? 'Crítica' : severity === 'WARNING' ? 'Advertencia' : 'Movimiento positivo';
}

function marketsAlertReason_(event) {
  if (event.type === 'COMPETITIVE_CROSSOVER') {
    return event.direction === 'LOST' ? 'El competidor ha pasado de estar por debajo o empatado a situarse por encima de BBVA.' :
      'BBVA ha pasado de estar por debajo o empatado a situarse por encima del competidor.';
  }
  const limit = event.severity === 'CRITICAL' ? 'rojo' : 'amarillo';
  return 'La caída de ' + marketsAlertNumberLabel_(event.metricValue) +
    ' puntos supera el límite configurado para el nivel ' + limit +
    ' (' + marketsAlertNumberLabel_(event.threshold) + ' puntos).';
}

function marketsAlertRecommendation_(event) {
  if (event.type === 'COMPETITIVE_CROSSOVER') return 'Abre Market Pulse y compara la evolución de BBVA con el competidor en ese mercado.';
  return 'Abre Market Pulse, revisa la evolución de la aplicación y contrasta el movimiento con las valoraciones recientes.';
}

function marketsAlertNumberLabel_(value) {
  const number = Number(value);
  return isFinite(number) ? number.toFixed(2).replace('.', ',') : '—';
}

function marketsAlertIntegerLabel_(value) {
  const number = toFiniteNumber_(value);
  if (number === null) return 'N/D';
  return Math.round(number).toLocaleString('es-ES');
}

function marketsAlertSignedNumberLabel_(value) {
  const number = Number(value);
  return isFinite(number) ? (number > 0 ? '+' : '') + number.toFixed(2).replace('.', ',') : '—';
}

function marketsAlertDateLabel_(value) {
  if (!value) return 'fecha base';
  const date = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value);
  if (isNaN(date.getTime())) return trimString_(value);
  try { return Utilities.formatDate(date, MARKETS_CONFIG.timeZone, 'dd/MM/yyyy'); }
  catch (ignored) { return date.toISOString().slice(0, 10); }
}

function marketsAlertDataDateLabel_(events) {
  const dates = (events || []).map(function (event) {
    return event.currentDate || event.referenceDate || null;
  }).map(function (value) {
    if (!value) return null;
    const date = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value);
    return isNaN(date.getTime()) ? null : date;
  }).filter(Boolean).sort(function (a, b) { return b.getTime() - a.getTime(); });
  return dates.length ? marketsAlertDateLabel_(dates[0]) : 'no disponible';
}

function marketsAlertRuleLabel_(type) {
  return type === 'RATING_DROP_DAILY' ? 'Caída diaria del rating' :
    type === 'RATING_DROP_MTD' ? 'Caída del día 15 al día 15' :
    type === 'RATING_DROP_YTD' ? 'Caída desde inicio de año' :
    type === 'COMPETITIVE_CROSSOVER' ? 'Cruce de posición competitiva' :
    type === 'TEST' ? 'Verificación del canal' : type;
}

function buildMarketsAlertInlineImages_() {
  const html = HtmlService.createHtmlOutputFromFile('BrandIdentity').getContent();
  const match = html.match(/--asset-bbva-bei-logo:url\("data:image\/png;base64,([^"\)]+)"\)/);
  if (!match) throw new Error('No se ha encontrado el logotipo oficial de BBVA Banca de Empresas e Instituciones.');
  return { bbvaLogo: Utilities.newBlob(Utilities.base64Decode(match[1]), 'image/png', 'bbva-bei.png') };
}

function escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(value) {
  return trimString_(value).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
}


========================================
ARCHIVO: 55_AlertPreferences.gs
========================================

/** Preferencias privadas y autoservicio de alertas. */
var MARKETS_ALERT_DEFAULTS = Object.freeze({
  active: false,
  countries: Object.freeze(['ALL']),
  platforms: Object.freeze(['ALL']),
  reviewShareEnabled: false,
  reviewWarningPct: 15,
  reviewCriticalPct: 30,
  dailyDropEnabled: true,
  dailyWarningDrop: 0.10,
  dailyCriticalDrop: 0.15,
  mtdDropEnabled: true,
  mtdWarningDrop: 0.30,
  mtdCriticalDrop: 0.50,
  ytdDropEnabled: true,
  ytdWarningDrop: 0.30,
  ytdCriticalDrop: 0.50,
  repeatWhileBreached: false,
  crossoverEnabled: true,
  crossoverDirection: 'BOTH',
  deliveryMode: 'ALERTS_ONLY',
  newsletterEnabled: false,
  configVersion: '4'
});

function defaultMarketsAlertPreferences_(email) {
  return {
    email: normalizeMarketsAlertEmail_(email),
    active: MARKETS_ALERT_DEFAULTS.active,
    countries: MARKETS_ALERT_DEFAULTS.countries.slice(),
    platforms: MARKETS_ALERT_DEFAULTS.platforms.slice(),
    reviewShareEnabled: MARKETS_ALERT_DEFAULTS.reviewShareEnabled,
    reviewWarningPct: MARKETS_ALERT_DEFAULTS.reviewWarningPct,
    reviewCriticalPct: MARKETS_ALERT_DEFAULTS.reviewCriticalPct,
    dailyDropEnabled: MARKETS_ALERT_DEFAULTS.dailyDropEnabled,
    dailyWarningDrop: MARKETS_ALERT_DEFAULTS.dailyWarningDrop,
    dailyCriticalDrop: MARKETS_ALERT_DEFAULTS.dailyCriticalDrop,
    mtdDropEnabled: MARKETS_ALERT_DEFAULTS.mtdDropEnabled,
    mtdWarningDrop: MARKETS_ALERT_DEFAULTS.mtdWarningDrop,
    mtdCriticalDrop: MARKETS_ALERT_DEFAULTS.mtdCriticalDrop,
    ytdDropEnabled: MARKETS_ALERT_DEFAULTS.ytdDropEnabled,
    ytdWarningDrop: MARKETS_ALERT_DEFAULTS.ytdWarningDrop,
    ytdCriticalDrop: MARKETS_ALERT_DEFAULTS.ytdCriticalDrop,
    repeatWhileBreached: MARKETS_ALERT_DEFAULTS.repeatWhileBreached,
    crossoverEnabled: MARKETS_ALERT_DEFAULTS.crossoverEnabled,
    crossoverDirection: MARKETS_ALERT_DEFAULTS.crossoverDirection,
    deliveryMode: MARKETS_ALERT_DEFAULTS.deliveryMode,
    newsletterEnabled: MARKETS_ALERT_DEFAULTS.newsletterEnabled,
    configVersion: MARKETS_ALERT_DEFAULTS.configVersion,
    updatedAt: '', updatedBy: '', registeredAt: '', lastSeenAt: '', lastTestAt: '',
    lastDeliveryAt: '', status: 'NOT_CONFIGURED', persisted: false
  };
}

function normalizeMarketsAlertPreferences_(source, email) {
  const input = source || {};
  const defaults = defaultMarketsAlertPreferences_(email || input.email);
  const countries = normalizeMarketsAlertScope_(input.countries, MARKETS_CONFIG.countryCodes);
  const platforms = normalizeMarketsAlertScope_(input.platforms, ['Android', 'iOS', 'Huawei']);
  const reviewWarning = marketsAlertNumber_(input.reviewWarningPct, defaults.reviewWarningPct, 0, 100);
  const reviewCritical = marketsAlertNumber_(input.reviewCriticalPct, defaults.reviewCriticalPct, 0, 100);
  const dailyWarning = marketsAlertNumber_(input.dailyWarningDrop, defaults.dailyWarningDrop, 0.01, 5);
  const dailyCritical = marketsAlertNumber_(input.dailyCriticalDrop, defaults.dailyCriticalDrop, 0.01, 5);
  const mtdWarning = marketsAlertNumber_(input.mtdWarningDrop, defaults.mtdWarningDrop, 0.01, 5);
  const mtdCritical = marketsAlertNumber_(input.mtdCriticalDrop, defaults.mtdCriticalDrop, 0.01, 5);
  const ytdWarning = marketsAlertNumber_(input.ytdWarningDrop, defaults.ytdWarningDrop, 0.01, 5);
  const ytdCritical = marketsAlertNumber_(input.ytdCriticalDrop, defaults.ytdCriticalDrop, 0.01, 5);
  if (reviewCritical <= reviewWarning) throw new Error('El umbral critico de resenas debe ser mayor que el de advertencia.');
  validateMarketsAlertThresholds_('diaria', dailyWarning, dailyCritical);
  validateMarketsAlertThresholds_('mensual', mtdWarning, mtdCritical);
  validateMarketsAlertThresholds_('anual', ytdWarning, ytdCritical);
  const direction = trimString_(input.crossoverDirection || defaults.crossoverDirection).toUpperCase();
  if (['BOTH', 'LOST', 'GAINED'].indexOf(direction) < 0) throw new Error('Direccion de cruce competitivo no valida.');
  const storedDeliveryMode = trimString_(input.deliveryMode || defaults.deliveryMode).toUpperCase();
  const storedConfigVersion = trimString_(input.configVersion);
  const newsletterEnabled = input.newsletterEnabled === undefined ?
    (storedConfigVersion && Number(storedConfigVersion) < 4 ?
      parseBoolean_(input.active, false) : storedDeliveryMode !== 'ALERTS_ONLY') :
    parseBoolean_(input.newsletterEnabled, defaults.newsletterEnabled);
  const normalized = {
    email: defaults.email,
    active: parseBoolean_(input.active, defaults.active),
    countries: countries,
    platforms: platforms,
    reviewShareEnabled: parseBoolean_(input.reviewShareEnabled, defaults.reviewShareEnabled),
    reviewWarningPct: reviewWarning,
    reviewCriticalPct: reviewCritical,
    dailyDropEnabled: parseBoolean_(input.dailyDropEnabled, defaults.dailyDropEnabled),
    dailyWarningDrop: dailyWarning,
    dailyCriticalDrop: dailyCritical,
    mtdDropEnabled: parseBoolean_(input.mtdDropEnabled, defaults.mtdDropEnabled),
    mtdWarningDrop: mtdWarning,
    mtdCriticalDrop: mtdCritical,
    ytdDropEnabled: parseBoolean_(input.ytdDropEnabled, defaults.ytdDropEnabled),
    ytdWarningDrop: ytdWarning,
    ytdCriticalDrop: ytdCritical,
    repeatWhileBreached: parseBoolean_(input.repeatWhileBreached, defaults.repeatWhileBreached),
    crossoverEnabled: parseBoolean_(input.crossoverEnabled, defaults.crossoverEnabled),
    crossoverDirection: direction,
    newsletterEnabled: newsletterEnabled,
    deliveryMode: newsletterEnabled ? 'DAILY_DIGEST' : 'ALERTS_ONLY',
    configVersion: MARKETS_ALERT_DEFAULTS.configVersion,
    updatedAt: input.updatedAt || '',
    updatedBy: trimString_(input.updatedBy),
    registeredAt: input.registeredAt || '',
    lastSeenAt: input.lastSeenAt || '',
    lastTestAt: input.lastTestAt || '',
    lastDeliveryAt: input.lastDeliveryAt || '',
    persisted: Boolean(input.persisted)
  };
  normalized.status = normalized.active && normalized.newsletterEnabled ? 'ACTIVE' :
    normalized.active ? 'ALERTS_ONLY' :
      normalized.newsletterEnabled ? 'NEWSLETTER_ONLY' : 'PAUSED';
  return normalized;
}

function validateMarketsAlertThresholds_(label, warning, critical) {
  if (critical <= warning) throw new Error('El umbral critico de caida ' + label + ' debe ser mayor que el de advertencia.');
}

function normalizeMarketsAlertScope_(value, allowed) {
  const items = Array.isArray(value) ? value : trimString_(value).split(/[;,]/);
  const normalized = uniqueStrings_(items.map(function (item) { return trimString_(item); }));
  if (!normalized.length || normalized.indexOf('ALL') >= 0) return ['ALL'];
  const invalid = normalized.filter(function (item) { return allowed.indexOf(item) < 0; });
  if (invalid.length) throw new Error('Ambito de alerta no valido: ' + invalid.join(', '));
  return normalized;
}

function marketsAlertNumber_(value, fallback, minimum, maximum) {
  const parsed = toFiniteNumber_(value);
  const normalized = parsed === null ? fallback : Math.round(parsed * 100) / 100;
  if (normalized < minimum || normalized > maximum) {
    throw new Error('Umbral fuera de rango: ' + normalized);
  }
  return normalized;
}

function normalizeMarketsAlertEmail_(email) {
  const normalized = trimString_(email).toLowerCase();
  if (!/^[^\s@]+@bbva\.com$/.test(normalized)) throw new Error('Las alertas requieren una cuenta corporativa @bbva.com.');
  return normalized;
}

function currentMarketsUserEmail_() {
  const email = Session.getActiveUser().getEmail();
  if (!trimString_(email)) {
    throw new Error('No se ha podido identificar al usuario conectado. Revisa el modo de despliegue de la aplicacion web.');
  }
  return normalizeMarketsAlertEmail_(email);
}

function readMarketsAlertPreferences_(ss, email) {
  const normalizedEmail = normalizeMarketsAlertEmail_(email);
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.alertPreferences);
  if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() <= 1) return defaultMarketsAlertPreferences_(normalizedEmail);
  const headers = MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.alertPreferences;
  const indexes = indexHeaders_(headers);
  const rows = sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, headers.length).getValues();
  const row = rows.find(function (item) { return normalizeEmailForComparison_(item[indexes.user_email]) === normalizedEmail; });
  return row ? alertPreferencesFromRow_(row, indexes) : defaultMarketsAlertPreferences_(normalizedEmail);
}

function readAllMarketsAlertPreferences_(ss) {
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.alertPreferences);
  if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() <= 1) return [];
  const headers = MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.alertPreferences;
  const indexes = indexHeaders_(headers);
  return sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, headers.length).getValues()
    .filter(function (row) { return trimString_(row[indexes.user_email]); })
    .map(function (row) { return alertPreferencesFromRow_(row, indexes); });
}

function alertPreferencesFromRow_(row, indexes) {
  return normalizeMarketsAlertPreferences_({
    email: row[indexes.user_email], active: row[indexes.active],
    countries: row[indexes.countries], platforms: row[indexes.platforms],
    reviewShareEnabled: row[indexes.review_share_enabled],
    reviewWarningPct: row[indexes.review_warning_pct], reviewCriticalPct: row[indexes.review_critical_pct],
    dailyDropEnabled: row[indexes.daily_drop_enabled],
    dailyWarningDrop: row[indexes.daily_warning_drop], dailyCriticalDrop: row[indexes.daily_critical_drop],
    mtdDropEnabled: row[indexes.mtd_drop_enabled],
    mtdWarningDrop: row[indexes.mtd_warning_drop], mtdCriticalDrop: row[indexes.mtd_critical_drop],
    ytdDropEnabled: row[indexes.ytd_drop_enabled],
    ytdWarningDrop: row[indexes.ytd_warning_drop], ytdCriticalDrop: row[indexes.ytd_critical_drop],
    repeatWhileBreached: row[indexes.repeat_while_breached],
    crossoverEnabled: row[indexes.crossover_enabled], crossoverDirection: row[indexes.crossover_direction],
    deliveryMode: row[indexes.delivery_mode], updatedAt: row[indexes.updated_at],
    updatedBy: row[indexes.updated_by], registeredAt: row[indexes.registered_at],
    lastSeenAt: row[indexes.last_seen_at], lastTestAt: row[indexes.last_test_at],
    lastDeliveryAt: row[indexes.last_delivery_at], configVersion: row[indexes.config_version],
    persisted: true
  }, row[indexes.user_email]);
}

function marketsAlertPreferencesRow_(preferences, updatedBy) {
  const now = preferences.updatedAt instanceof Date ? preferences.updatedAt : new Date();
  return [
    preferences.email, preferences.active, preferences.countries.join(','), preferences.platforms.join(','),
    preferences.reviewShareEnabled, preferences.reviewWarningPct, preferences.reviewCriticalPct,
    false, 0.30, 0.50,
    preferences.crossoverEnabled, preferences.crossoverDirection, preferences.deliveryMode,
    now, updatedBy || preferences.email, preferences.configVersion, preferences.status,
    preferences.dailyDropEnabled, preferences.dailyWarningDrop, preferences.dailyCriticalDrop,
    preferences.mtdDropEnabled, preferences.mtdWarningDrop, preferences.mtdCriticalDrop,
    preferences.ytdDropEnabled, preferences.ytdWarningDrop, preferences.ytdCriticalDrop,
    preferences.repeatWhileBreached, preferences.registeredAt || now, preferences.lastSeenAt || '',
    preferences.lastTestAt || '', preferences.lastDeliveryAt || ''
  ];
}

function upsertMarketsAlertPreferences_(ss, preferences, updatedBy) {
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.alertPreferences);
  const previous = readMarketsAlertPreferences_(ss, preferences.email);
  const normalized = normalizeMarketsAlertPreferences_(preferences, preferences.email);
  normalized.updatedAt = new Date();
  normalized.updatedBy = updatedBy || normalized.email;
  normalized.registeredAt = preferences.registeredAt || previous.registeredAt || normalized.updatedAt;
  normalized.lastSeenAt = preferences.lastSeenAt || previous.lastSeenAt || '';
  normalized.lastTestAt = preferences.lastTestAt || previous.lastTestAt || '';
  normalized.lastDeliveryAt = preferences.lastDeliveryAt || previous.lastDeliveryAt || '';
  normalized.persisted = true;
  const existing = sheet.getLastRow() > 1 ? sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues() : [];
  const index = existing.findIndex(function (row) { return normalizeEmailForComparison_(row[0]) === normalized.email; });
  const row = marketsAlertPreferencesRow_(normalized, normalized.updatedBy);
  ensureSheetCapacity_(sheet, Math.max(2, sheet.getLastRow() + (index < 0 ? 1 : 0)), row.length);
  sheet.getRange(index < 0 ? sheet.getLastRow() + 1 : index + 2, 1, 1, row.length).setValues([row]);
  refreshFilter_(sheet, row.length);
  return normalized;
}

function registerOrTouchMarketsAlertUser_(ss, email) {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  if (!lock.tryLock(MARKETS_CONFIG.lockTimeoutMs)) {
    throw new Error('El registro de alertas esta siendo actualizado. Intentalo de nuevo.');
  }
  try {
    const preferences = readMarketsAlertPreferences_(ss, email);
    const now = new Date();
    if (!preferences.persisted) {
      preferences.registeredAt = now;
      preferences.lastSeenAt = now;
      return upsertMarketsAlertPreferences_(ss, preferences, 'SELF_SERVICE_REGISTRATION');
    }
    touchMarketsAlertTimestamp_(ss, email, 'LAST_SEEN_AT', now);
    preferences.lastSeenAt = now;
    return preferences;
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

function touchMarketsAlertTimestamp_(ss, email, header, value) {
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.alertPreferences);
  if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() <= 1) return;
  const emailColumn = sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues();
  const index = emailColumn.findIndex(function (row) {
    return normalizeEmailForComparison_(row[0]) === normalizeEmailForComparison_(email);
  });
  const column = indexHeaders_(MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.alertPreferences)[normalizeHeader_(header)];
  if (index >= 0 && column !== undefined) sheet.getRange(index + 2, column + 1).setValue(value || new Date());
}

function normalizeEmailForComparison_(email) {
  return trimString_(email).toLowerCase();
}

function getMarketsAlertPreferences() {
  const email = currentMarketsUserEmail_();
  const ss = openMarketsSpreadsheet_();
  requireCurrentSheetContract_(ss, MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.control, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.control);
  requireCurrentSheetContract_(ss, MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.alertPreferences, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.alertPreferences);
  requireCurrentSheetContract_(ss, MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.alerts, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.alerts);
  const control = readControl_(ss);
  const preferences = registerOrTouchMarketsAlertUser_(ss, email);
  return {
    user: { email: email }, preferences: alertPreferencesForClient_(preferences),
    defaults: alertPreferencesForClient_(defaultMarketsAlertPreferences_(email)),
    system: {
      emailDeliveryEnabled: parseBoolean_(control.SEND_ALERT_EMAILS, false),
      reviewDataAvailable: false,
      reviewDataMessage: 'La fuente actual publica rating y volumen agregados, no el reparto de estrellas 1 y 2 en las últimas 24 horas.',
      configWarning: trimString_(control.ALERT_CONFIG_WARNING),
      schedule: 'Resumen diario tras la actualización de las 07:05',
      privacy: 'Puedes modificar esta configuración desde Alertas aunque el alta la haya realizado un administrador. Administración conserva la gestión y la auditoría.',
      deliveryScope: 'Todos los usuarios activos · sin sesión',
      adminVisibility: 'Los administradores auditan altas y actividad en ALERT_SUBSCRIPTIONS.'
    },
    recentEvents: readRecentMarketsAlertEventsForUser_(ss, email, 6)
  };
}

function saveMarketsAlertPreferences(payload) {
  const email = currentMarketsUserEmail_();
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  if (!lock.tryLock(MARKETS_CONFIG.lockTimeoutMs)) throw new Error('La configuracion de alertas esta siendo actualizada. Intentalo de nuevo.');
  try {
    const ss = openMarketsSpreadsheet_();
    requireCurrentSheetContract_(ss, MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.alertPreferences, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.alertPreferences);
    return alertPreferencesForClient_(upsertMarketsAlertPreferences_(ss,
      normalizeMarketsAlertPreferences_(payload, email), email));
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

function resetMarketsAlertPreferences() {
  return saveMarketsAlertPreferences(defaultMarketsAlertPreferences_(currentMarketsUserEmail_()));
}

function sendMarketsAlertTestEmail() {
  const email = currentMarketsUserEmail_();
  const ss = openMarketsSpreadsheet_();
  requireCurrentSheetContract_(ss, MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.control, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.control);
  requireCurrentSheetContract_(ss, MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.alertPreferences, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.alertPreferences);
  requireCurrentSheetContract_(ss, MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.alerts, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.alerts);
  const control = readControl_(ss);
  if (!parseBoolean_(control.SEND_ALERT_EMAILS, false)) {
    throw new Error('El envio global esta desactivado. Activa SEND_ALERT_EMAILS en CONTROL antes de probar.');
  }
  const sentAt = new Date();
  const deliveryId = 'COM-' + Utilities.formatDate(sentAt, MARKETS_CONFIG.timeZone, 'yyyyMMdd-HHmmss') + '-' +
    Utilities.getUuid().slice(0, 8).toUpperCase();
  const preferences = readMarketsAlertPreferences_(ss, email);
  const ratings = readRatingObjects_(ss);
  const catalog = readAppCatalog_(ss);
  const events = validateMarketsRealAlertPreviewEvents_(
    buildMarketsRealAlertPreviewEvents_(ratings, catalog, preferences, deliveryId), preferences);
  const delivered = sendAlertEmails_(events, ss);
  if (delivered !== 1) throw new Error('Google no ha aceptado la prueba para el destinatario esperado.');
  let auditLogged = true;
  try {
    appendAlertEvents_(ss, [{
      timestamp: sentAt, eventKey: 'TEST|' + deliveryId + '|' + email,
      referenceDate: events[0].referenceDate || sentAt, platform: 'Configurada',
      countryCode: preferences.countries.join(','), type: 'TEST', severity: 'POSITIVE',
      detail: 'Vista previa con ' + events.length + ' señal(es) o cierre(s) reales según la configuración vigente.',
      recipients: email, userEmail: email, ruleId: deliveryId, metricValue: events.length,
      threshold: '', appKey: '', direction: 'TEST', runId: deliveryId,
      source: 'SELF_SERVICE_TEST_REAL_DATA'
    }]);
    touchMarketsAlertTimestamp_(ss, email, 'LAST_TEST_AT', sentAt);
  } catch (auditError) {
    auditLogged = false;
    console.error('Correo aceptado, pero no se pudo completar la auditoria ' + deliveryId + ': ' + errorToString_(auditError));
  }
  let effectiveSender = '';
  try { effectiveSender = normalizeEmailForComparison_(Session.getEffectiveUser().getEmail()); } catch (ignored) {}
  const delivery = events.deliveryResults && events.deliveryResults[0] || {};
  return {
    sent: true, accepted: true, recipient: email, sentAt: sentAt.toISOString(), deliveryId: deliveryId,
    effectiveSender: effectiveSender, senderName: delivery.senderName || MARKETS_ALERT_EMAIL.senderName,
    senderAddress: delivery.senderAddress || MARKETS_ALERT_EMAIL.senderAddress,
    senderMode: MARKETS_ALERT_EMAIL.senderMode,
    senderVerificationStatus: delivery.verificationStatus || '',
    messageId: delivery.messageId || '', threadId: delivery.threadId || '', auditLogged: auditLogged,
    realData: true, previewItems: events.length,
    scopeCountries: preferences.countries.slice(), scopePlatforms: preferences.platforms.slice(),
    previewCountries: uniqueStrings_(events.map(function (event) { return event.countryCode; })),
    codeVersion: MARKETS_CONFIG.codeVersion, webVersion: MARKETS_WEB.version
  };
}

function alertPreferencesForClient_(preferences) {
  const output = Object.assign({}, preferences);
  ['updatedAt', 'registeredAt', 'lastSeenAt', 'lastTestAt', 'lastDeliveryAt'].forEach(function (field) {
    if (output[field] instanceof Date) output[field] = output[field].toISOString();
  });
  return output;
}

function readRecentMarketsAlertEventsForUser_(ss, email, limit) {
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.alerts);
  if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() <= 1) return [];
  const headers = MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.alerts;
  const indexes = indexHeaders_(headers);
  const count = Math.min(500, sheet.getLastRow() - 1);
  const rows = sheet.getRange(sheet.getLastRow() - count + 1, 1, count, headers.length).getValues();
  return rows.reverse().filter(function (row) {
    return normalizeEmailForComparison_(row[indexes.user_email] || row[indexes.recipients]) === email;
  }).slice(0, limit).map(function (row) {
    const timestamp = row[indexes.timestamp];
    return {
      timestamp: timestamp instanceof Date ? timestamp.toISOString() : trimString_(timestamp),
      type: trimString_(row[indexes.type]), severity: trimString_(row[indexes.severity]),
      platform: trimString_(row[indexes.platform]), countryCode: trimString_(row[indexes.country_code]),
      detail: trimString_(row[indexes.detail]), direction: trimString_(row[indexes.direction])
    };
  });
}


========================================
ARCHIVO: 56_Newsletter.gs
========================================

/**
 * Newsletter mensual ejecutiva de Market Pulse.
 *
 * Se genera por destinatario y respeta sus mercados y plataformas. Solo usa
 * observaciones reales. Los volúmenes Android compartidos no se atribuyen a
 * una geografía y el efecto de nuevas valoraciones se oculta si el redondeo
 * impide una estimación defendible.
 */

function runMarketsMonthlyNewsletter() {
  const now = new Date();
  const day = Number(Utilities.formatDate(now, MARKETS_CONFIG.timeZone, 'd'));
  if (day !== 1) return { status: 'SKIPPED_NOT_MONTH_START', sent: 0 };
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  if (!lock.tryLock(MARKETS_CONFIG.lockTimeoutMs)) {
    throw new Error('Otra ejecución de comunicaciones sigue activa.');
  }
  try {
    const ss = openMarketsSpreadsheet_();
    ensureWorkbookSchema_(ss);
    const control = readControl_(ss);
    if (!marketsNewsletterDeliveryEnabled_(control)) {
      return { status: 'DISABLED', sent: 0 };
    }
    const period = marketsPreviousMonthKey_(now);
    return sendMarketsMonthlyNewsletter_(ss, period, false);
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

function sendMarketsMonthlyNewsletter_(ss, period, isTest) {
  const subscribers = readAllMarketsAlertPreferences_(ss).filter(function (item) {
    return item.newsletterEnabled;
  });
  const ratings = readRatingObjects_(ss);
  const catalog = readAppCatalog_(ss);
  const existing = readExistingAlertKeys_(ss);
  const sender = requireMarketsAlertSender_(ss);
  const images = buildMarketsAlertInlineImages_();
  const result = { status: 'NOT_REQUIRED', period: period, subscribers: subscribers.length,
    sent: 0, skipped: 0, failures: [] };
  const acceptedEvents = [];
  subscribers.forEach(function (preferences) {
    const eventKey = ['NEWSLETTER_MONTHLY', period, preferences.email].join('|');
    if (!isTest && existing[eventKey]) {
      result.skipped += 1;
      return;
    }
    try {
      const model = buildMarketsNewsletterModel_(ratings, catalog, preferences, period, {
        isTest: Boolean(isTest)
      });
      const message = marketsNewsletterEmailMessage_(preferences.email, model, images, sender);
      const accepted = Gmail.Users.Messages.send({ raw: message.raw }, 'me');
      if (!accepted || !trimString_(accepted.id)) throw new Error('Gmail no ha devuelto un identificador de mensaje.');
      result.sent += 1;
      acceptedEvents.push(marketsNewsletterAuditEvent_(preferences.email, model, eventKey,
        isTest ? 'NEWSLETTER_TEST' : 'MONTHLY_NEWSLETTER'));
      try { touchMarketsAlertTimestamp_(ss, preferences.email, 'LAST_DELIVERY_AT', new Date()); }
      catch (auditError) {
        console.error('Newsletter aceptada para ' + preferences.email +
          ', pero falló LAST_DELIVERY_AT: ' + errorToString_(auditError));
      }
    } catch (error) {
      result.failures.push({ recipient: preferences.email, error: errorToString_(error) });
    }
  });
  appendAlertEvents_(ss, acceptedEvents);
  result.status = result.failures.length ? (result.sent ? 'PARTIAL' : 'ERROR') :
    (result.sent ? 'DELIVERED' : 'NOT_REQUIRED');
  return result;
}

function sendMarketsAdminNewsletterTestEmail(request) {
  const access = requireMarketsAdministrator_();
  requireMarketsNewsletterContracts_(access.ss);
  const normalized = normalizeMarketsNewsletterPreviewRequest_(request);
  const target = normalizeMarketsAlertEmail_(normalized.targetEmail);
  if (!marketsNewsletterDeliveryEnabled_(access.control)) {
    throw new Error('El envío de newsletters está desactivado en CONTROL.');
  }
  const preferences = readMarketsAlertPreferences_(access.ss, target);
  if (!preferences.persisted) throw new Error('El destinatario seleccionado no está dado de alta.');
  return withMarketsNewsletterPreviewLock_(
    access.ss, access.email, preferences, target, normalized.requestId);
}

function normalizeMarketsNewsletterPreviewRequest_(request) {
  const input = typeof request === 'string' ? { targetEmail: request } : (request || {});
  const requestId = trimString_(input.requestId) ||
    ('NEWS-REQ-' + Utilities.getUuid());
  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{8,96}$/.test(requestId)) {
    throw new Error('El identificador de la prueba no es válido.');
  }
  return { targetEmail: input.targetEmail, requestId: requestId };
}

function withMarketsNewsletterPreviewLock_(ss, recipient, preferences, configuredFor, requestId) {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  if (!lock.tryLock(MARKETS_CONFIG.lockTimeoutMs)) {
    throw new Error('Otra comunicación sigue preparándose. Reintenta: la misma solicitud no se duplicará.');
  }
  try {
    return sendMarketsNewsletterPreview_(ss, recipient, preferences, configuredFor, requestId);
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

function requireMarketsNewsletterContracts_(ss) {
  [
    [MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.control, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.control],
    [MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.catalog, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.catalog],
    [MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.ratings, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.ratings],
    [MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.alerts, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.alerts],
    [MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.alertPreferences, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.alertPreferences]
  ].forEach(function (definition) {
    requireCurrentSheetContract_(ss, definition[0], definition[1]);
  });
}

function sendMarketsNewsletterPreview_(ss, recipient, preferences, configuredFor, requestId) {
  const deliveryId = 'NEWS-' + trimString_(requestId).replace(/^NEWS-REQ-/, '').slice(0, 72).toUpperCase();
  const eventKey = 'NEWSLETTER_TEST|' + deliveryId + '|' + recipient;
  if (readExistingAlertKeys_(ss)[eventKey]) {
    return {
      sent: true, accepted: true, alreadyAccepted: true, recipient: recipient,
      configuredFor: configuredFor, deliveryId: deliveryId, realData: true,
      auditLogged: true, codeVersion: MARKETS_CONFIG.codeVersion, webVersion: MARKETS_WEB.version
    };
  }
  const ratings = readRatingObjects_(ss);
  const catalog = readAppCatalog_(ss);
  const period = latestMarketsNewsletterPeriod_(ratings, preferences);
  if (!period) throw new Error('No hay cierres reales disponibles para el ámbito configurado.');
  const model = buildMarketsNewsletterModel_(ratings, catalog, preferences, period, {
    isTest: true, configuredFor: configuredFor
  });
  const sender = requireMarketsAlertSender_(ss);
  const message = marketsNewsletterEmailMessage_(recipient, model, buildMarketsAlertInlineImages_(), sender);
  const accepted = Gmail.Users.Messages.send({ raw: message.raw }, 'me');
  if (!accepted || !trimString_(accepted.id)) throw new Error('Gmail no ha devuelto un identificador de mensaje.');
  appendAlertEvents_(ss, [marketsNewsletterAuditEvent_(recipient, model,
    eventKey, 'NEWSLETTER_TEST')]);
  return {
    sent: true, accepted: true, alreadyAccepted: false, recipient: recipient, configuredFor: configuredFor,
    period: period, deliveryId: deliveryId, messageId: trimString_(accepted.id),
    threadId: trimString_(accepted.threadId), realData: true,
    scopeType: model.scope.type, auditLogged: true, senderName: sender.name,
    senderAddress: sender.address, codeVersion: MARKETS_CONFIG.codeVersion, webVersion: MARKETS_WEB.version
  };
}

function marketsNewsletterDeliveryEnabled_(control) {
  const configured = control && control.SEND_NEWSLETTER_EMAILS;
  return configured === undefined || configured === '' ?
    parseBoolean_(control && control.SEND_ALERT_EMAILS, false) : parseBoolean_(configured, false);
}

function marketsPreviousMonthKey_(date) {
  const local = new Date(date.getTime());
  local.setDate(1);
  local.setMonth(local.getMonth() - 1);
  return dateKey_(local).slice(0, 7);
}

function marketsPeriodBefore_(period) {
  const parts = String(period || '').split('-');
  const year = Number(parts[0]);
  const month = Number(parts[1]);
  if (!Number.isInteger(year) || month < 1 || month > 12) return '';
  return month === 1 ? (year - 1) + '-12' :
    year + '-' + String(month - 1).padStart(2, '0');
}

function latestMarketsNewsletterPeriod_(ratings, preferences) {
  return (ratings || []).filter(function (item) {
    return item.isBbva && item.observedDate instanceof Date && item.rating !== null &&
      marketsAlertScopeMatches_(preferences, item);
  }).map(function (item) {
    return dateKey_(item.observedDate).slice(0, 7);
  }).sort().pop() || '';
}

function buildMarketsNewsletterModel_(ratings, catalog, preferences, period, options) {
  if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(trimString_(period))) throw new Error('Periodo de newsletter no válido.');
  const scopedCatalog = (catalog || []).filter(function (app) {
    return app.isBbva && (app.marketSegment || 'BANK') === 'BANK' &&
      marketsAlertScopeMatches_(preferences, app);
  });
  const volumeMetadata = marketsNewsletterVolumeMetadata_(catalog);
  const year = period.slice(0, 4);
  const previousPeriod = marketsPeriodBefore_(period);
  const scopedRatings = (ratings || []).filter(function (item) {
    return item.observedDate instanceof Date && item.rating !== null &&
      marketsAlertScopeMatches_(preferences, item) &&
      (item.marketSegment || 'BANK') === 'BANK';
  });
  const byApp = scopedRatings.reduce(function (out, item) {
    (out[item.appKey] || (out[item.appKey] = [])).push(item);
    return out;
  }, {});
  const items = scopedCatalog.map(function (app) {
    const series = (byApp[app.appKey] || []).slice().sort(function (a, b) {
      return a.observedDate.getTime() - b.observedDate.getTime();
    });
    const monthRows = series.filter(function (item) {
      return dateKey_(item.observedDate).slice(0, 7) === period;
    });
    if (!monthRows.length) return null;
    const previousRows = series.filter(function (item) {
      return dateKey_(item.observedDate).slice(0, 7) === previousPeriod;
    });
    const monthStart = previousRows.length ? previousRows[previousRows.length - 1] : null;
    const end = monthRows[monthRows.length - 1];
    const yearRows = series.filter(function (item) {
      const key = dateKey_(item.observedDate);
      return key.slice(0, 4) === year && key <= dateKey_(end.observedDate);
    });
    const yearStart = yearRows[0] || null;
    const metadata = volumeMetadata[app.appKey] || { key: app.appKey, shared: false, markets: 1 };
    const position = marketsNewsletterPosition_(scopedRatings, catalog, app, period);
    const newReviews = monthStart ? marketsNewsletterCountDelta_(monthStart, end) : null;
    const volumeAvailable = !metadata.shared;
    return {
      appKey: app.appKey, countryCode: app.countryCode, country: app.country,
      platform: app.platform, monthStartRating: monthStart ? monthStart.rating : null, endRating: end.rating,
      yearStartRating: yearStart ? yearStart.rating : null,
      monthDelta: monthStart ? round2_(end.rating - monthStart.rating) : null,
      yearDelta: yearStart ? round2_(end.rating - yearStart.rating) : null,
      newReviews: volumeAvailable ? newReviews : null,
      volumeAvailable: volumeAvailable, volumeKey: metadata.key,
      sharedMarkets: metadata.markets, startCount: monthStart ? monthStart.ratingCount : null,
      endCount: end.ratingCount, startDate: monthStart ? monthStart.observedDate : null,
      endDate: end.observedDate, position: position,
      effect: volumeAvailable && monthStart ? marketsNewsletterReviewEffect_(monthStart, end) : null
    };
  }).filter(Boolean);
  if (!items.length) throw new Error('No hay cierres BBVA reales para ' + period + ' en el ámbito configurado.');
  const criticalCatalog = marketsCriticalItems_(scopedCatalog);
  const observedKeys = items.reduce(function (out, item) { out[item.appKey] = true; return out; }, {});
  const missing = criticalCatalog.filter(function (app) { return !observedKeys[app.appKey]; })
    .map(function (app) {
      return { appKey: app.appKey, country: app.country, countryCode: app.countryCode, platform: app.platform };
    });
  const countries = uniqueStrings_(items.map(function (item) { return item.countryCode; }));
  const allCountries = preferences.countries.indexOf('ALL') >= 0;
  const scopeType = allCountries ? 'ALL' : preferences.countries.length === 1 ? 'SINGLE' : 'MULTI';
  const reviewEffects = marketsNewsletterReviewEffects_(items, scopeType);
  const totals = marketsNewsletterTotals_(items, scopeType);
  totals.effects = reviewEffects.length;
  const movements = items.filter(function (item) { return item.monthDelta !== null; })
    .sort(function (a, b) { return b.monthDelta - a.monthDelta; });
  return {
    period: period, periodLabel: marketsNewsletterPeriodLabel_(period),
    generatedAt: new Date(), isTest: Boolean(options && options.isTest),
    configuredFor: trimString_(options && options.configuredFor) || preferences.email,
    scope: {
      type: scopeType, countries: countries,
      countryLabel: countries.length === 1 ? items[0].country : '',
      platformLabel: preferences.platforms.indexOf('ALL') >= 0 ?
        'Todas las plataformas' : preferences.platforms.join(', ')
    },
    items: items, reviewEffects: reviewEffects, totals: totals,
    coverage: {
      observed: criticalCatalog.filter(function (app) { return observedKeys[app.appKey]; }).length,
      expected: criticalCatalog.length,
      complete: missing.length === 0, missing: missing
    },
    strongest: movements.length ? movements[0] : null,
    weakest: movements.length ? movements[movements.length - 1] : null
  };
}

function marketsNewsletterVolumeMetadata_(catalog) {
  const marketsByKey = {};
  (catalog || []).filter(function (app) { return app.platform === 'Android' && app.isBbva; })
    .forEach(function (app) {
      const key = 'ANDROID|' + (trimString_(app.packageName).toLowerCase() || app.appKey);
      (marketsByKey[key] || (marketsByKey[key] = {}))[app.countryCode] = true;
    });
  return (catalog || []).reduce(function (out, app) {
    const key = app.platform === 'Android' ?
      'ANDROID|' + (trimString_(app.packageName).toLowerCase() || app.appKey) :
      [app.platform.toUpperCase(), app.countryCode, app.appId || app.appKey].join('|');
    const markets = app.platform === 'Android' ? Object.keys(marketsByKey[key] || {}).length : 1;
    out[app.appKey] = { key: key, shared: app.platform === 'Android' && markets > 1, markets: Math.max(1, markets) };
    return out;
  }, {});
}

function marketsNewsletterPosition_(ratings, catalog, app, period) {
  const expected = (catalog || []).filter(function (candidate) {
    return candidate.enabled !== false && (candidate.marketSegment || 'BANK') === 'BANK' &&
      candidate.countryCode === app.countryCode && candidate.platform === app.platform;
  });
  const latestByApp = {};
  (ratings || []).filter(function (item) {
    return item.countryCode === app.countryCode && item.platform === app.platform &&
      (item.marketSegment || 'BANK') === 'BANK' &&
      dateKey_(item.observedDate).slice(0, 7) === period;
  }).forEach(function (item) {
    const current = latestByApp[item.appKey];
    if (!current || item.observedDate.getTime() > current.observedDate.getTime()) latestByApp[item.appKey] = item;
  });
  const rows = expected.map(function (candidate) { return latestByApp[candidate.appKey] || null; })
    .filter(function (item) { return item && item.rating !== null; });
  const bbva = latestByApp[app.appKey];
  if (!bbva || rows.length !== expected.length) {
    return { available: false, observed: rows.length, expected: expected.length };
  }
  const rank = 1 + rows.filter(function (item) { return item.rating > bbva.rating; }).length;
  const leader = rows.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return b.rating - a.rating; })[0];
  return {
    available: true, rank: rank, total: rows.length,
    leader: leader ? leader.appName : '', gapToLeader: leader ? round2_(bbva.rating - leader.rating) : null
  };
}

function marketsNewsletterCountDelta_(start, end) {
  const first = toFiniteNumber_(start && start.ratingCount);
  const last = toFiniteNumber_(end && end.ratingCount);
  if (first === null || last === null || last < first) return null;
  return Math.round(last - first);
}

function marketsNewsletterReviewEffect_(start, end) {
  const startRating = toFiniteNumber_(start && start.rating);
  const endRating = toFiniteNumber_(end && end.rating);
  const startCount = toFiniteNumber_(start && start.ratingCount);
  const endCount = toFiniteNumber_(end && end.ratingCount);
  if ([startRating, endRating, startCount, endCount].some(function (value) { return value === null; })) return null;
  const newReviews = endCount - startCount;
  if (newReviews <= 0) return null;
  const rating = (endCount * endRating - startCount * startRating) / newReviews;
  const uncertainty = (endCount * marketsNewsletterRatingHalfStep_(endRating) +
    startCount * marketsNewsletterRatingHalfStep_(startRating)) / newReviews;
  if (rating < 1 || rating > 5 || uncertainty > 0.5) return null;
  return {
    newReviewRating: Math.round(rating * 100) / 100,
    gapToPreviousRating: round2_(rating - startRating),
    accumulatedRatingChange: round2_(endRating - startRating),
    uncertainty: Math.round(uncertainty * 100) / 100
  };
}

function marketsNewsletterRatingHalfStep_(rating) {
  return Math.abs(rating * 10 - Math.round(rating * 10)) < 0.000001 ? 0.05 : 0.005;
}

function marketsNewsletterTotals_(items, scopeType) {
  const comparable = (items || []).filter(function (item) {
    return toFiniteNumber_(item.monthDelta) !== null;
  });
  const sharedGroups = (items || []).filter(function (item) {
    return !item.volumeAvailable;
  }).reduce(function (out, item) {
    (out[item.volumeKey] || (out[item.volumeKey] = [])).push(item);
    return out;
  }, {});
  const uniqueVolumes = {};
  (items || []).forEach(function (item) {
    let reviews = item.newReviews;
    if (!item.volumeAvailable) {
      const group = sharedGroups[item.volumeKey] || [];
      const expectedMarkets = group.reduce(function (maximum, candidate) {
        return Math.max(maximum, Number(candidate.sharedMarkets) || 1);
      }, 1);
      if (scopeType === 'ALL' || group.length >= expectedMarkets) {
        reviews = marketsNewsletterRawCountDelta_(item.startCount, item.endCount);
      }
    }
    reviews = toFiniteNumber_(reviews);
    if (reviews === null) return;
    const current = uniqueVolumes[item.volumeKey];
    if (!current || reviews > current.value) {
      uniqueVolumes[item.volumeKey] = { value: reviews, platform: item.platform };
    }
  });
  const byPlatform = Object.keys(uniqueVolumes).reduce(function (out, key) {
    const item = uniqueVolumes[key];
    out[item.platform] = (out[item.platform] || 0) + item.value;
    return out;
  }, {});
  return {
    series: comparable.length,
    improving: comparable.filter(function (item) { return item.monthDelta > 0; }).length,
    declining: comparable.filter(function (item) { return item.monthDelta < 0; }).length,
    stable: comparable.filter(function (item) { return item.monthDelta === 0; }).length,
    reviewsByPlatform: byPlatform
  };
}

function marketsNewsletterReviewEffects_(items, scopeType) {
  const effects = (items || []).filter(function (item) {
    return item.volumeAvailable && Boolean(item.effect);
  }).map(function (item) {
    return {
      appKey: item.appKey, country: item.country, platform: item.platform,
      volumeKey: item.volumeKey, effect: item.effect, shared: false
    };
  });
  const shared = (items || []).filter(function (item) {
    return !item.volumeAvailable && item.platform === 'Android';
  }).reduce(function (out, item) {
    (out[item.volumeKey] || (out[item.volumeKey] = [])).push(item);
    return out;
  }, {});
  Object.keys(shared).forEach(function (volumeKey) {
    const group = shared[volumeKey];
    const expectedMarkets = group.reduce(function (maximum, item) {
      return Math.max(maximum, Number(item.sharedMarkets) || 1);
    }, 1);
    if (scopeType !== 'ALL' && group.length < expectedMarkets) return;
    const signatures = uniqueStrings_(group.map(function (item) {
      return [item.monthStartRating, item.startCount, item.endRating, item.endCount].join('|');
    }));
    if (signatures.length !== 1) return;
    const first = group[0];
    const effect = marketsNewsletterReviewEffect_(
      { rating: first.monthStartRating, ratingCount: first.startCount },
      { rating: first.endRating, ratingCount: first.endCount });
    if (!effect) return;
    effects.push({
      appKey: volumeKey, country: 'Ficha Android compartida',
      platform: 'Android', volumeKey: volumeKey, effect: effect, shared: true,
      markets: uniqueStrings_(group.map(function (item) { return item.country; })).sort()
    });
  });
  return effects.sort(function (a, b) {
    return compareMarketsPlatforms_(a, b) || trimString_(a.country).localeCompare(trimString_(b.country));
  });
}

function marketsNewsletterRawCountDelta_(startCount, endCount) {
  const first = toFiniteNumber_(startCount);
  const last = toFiniteNumber_(endCount);
  return first === null || last === null || last < first ? null : Math.round(last - first);
}

function marketsNewsletterEmailMessage_(recipient, model, inlineImages, sender) {
  const subject = (model.isTest ? '[PRUEBA] ' : '') + 'Market Pulse · Cierre ' +
    model.periodLabel + ' · ' + marketsNewsletterScopeTitle_(model);
  const plain = renderMarketsNewsletterPlainText_(model);
  const html = renderMarketsNewsletterHtml_(model);
  const image = inlineImages && inlineImages.bbvaLogo;
  if (!image || typeof image.getBytes !== 'function') throw new Error('No se ha podido incorporar el logotipo oficial.');
  const token = Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, '');
  const related = 'market_pulse_news_related_' + token;
  const alternative = 'market_pulse_news_alt_' + token;
  const crlf = '\r\n';
  const mime = [
    'From: ' + marketsAlertEncodedHeader_(sender.name) + ' <' + sender.address + '>',
    'To: ' + normalizeMarketsAlertEmail_(recipient),
    'Subject: ' + marketsAlertEncodedHeader_(subject),
    'Date: ' + new Date().toUTCString(),
    'Message-ID: <' + token + '@voc-commercial.bbva.com>',
    'MIME-Version: 1.0',
    'Content-Type: multipart/related; boundary="' + related + '"', '',
    '--' + related, 'Content-Type: multipart/alternative; boundary="' + alternative + '"', '',
    '--' + alternative, 'Content-Type: text/plain; charset="UTF-8"',
    'Content-Transfer-Encoding: base64', '', marketsAlertMimeText_(plain),
    '--' + alternative, 'Content-Type: text/html; charset="UTF-8"',
    'Content-Transfer-Encoding: base64', '', marketsAlertMimeText_(html),
    '--' + alternative + '--', '',
    '--' + related, 'Content-Type: image/png; name="bbva-bei.png"',
    'Content-Transfer-Encoding: base64', 'Content-ID: <bbvaLogo>',
    'Content-Disposition: inline; filename="bbva-bei.png"', '',
    marketsAlertMimeBytes_(image.getBytes()), '--' + related + '--', ''
  ].join(crlf);
  return { raw: Utilities.base64EncodeWebSafe(mime, Utilities.Charset.UTF_8).replace(/=+$/g, ''),
    subject: subject, recipient: recipient };
}

function renderMarketsNewsletterPlainText_(model) {
  const brief = marketsNewsletterExecutiveBrief_(model);
  const lines = model.items.map(function (item) {
    const volume = item.newReviews === null ? 'actividad N/D' :
      marketsAlertIntegerLabel_(item.newReviews) + ' nuevas valoraciones';
    const story = item.effect ?
      marketsNewsletterRatingLabel_(item.monthStartRating) + ' → voz nueva ' +
      marketsNewsletterRatingLabel_(item.effect.newReviewRating) + '/5 (' + volume +
      ') → cierre ' + marketsNewsletterRatingLabel_(item.endRating) + ' (' +
      marketsNewsletterSigned_(item.monthDelta) + ')' :
      marketsNewsletterRatingLabel_(item.monthStartRating) + ' → cierre ' +
      marketsNewsletterRatingLabel_(item.endRating) + ' (' +
      marketsNewsletterSigned_(item.monthDelta) + ') · ' + volume;
    return [item.country, item.platform].join(' · ') + ': ' + story +
      ' · año ' + marketsNewsletterSigned_(item.yearDelta) +
      ' · ' + marketsNewsletterPositionLabel_(item.position);
  });
  const effects = (model.reviewEffects || []).filter(function (item) {
    return item.shared;
  }).map(function (item) {
    return item.country + ' · ' + item.platform + ': ' +
      marketsNewsletterRatingLabel_(item.effect.newReviewRating) +
      '/5 en las nuevas valoraciones · ' +
      marketsNewsletterSigned_(item.effect.gapToPreviousRating) +
      ' frente al rating acumulado anterior · el acumulado se mueve ' +
      marketsNewsletterSigned_(item.effect.accumulatedRatingChange) + ' puntos' +
      (item.shared ? ' sobre la ficha completa, sin atribución geográfica' : '');
  });
  return 'MARKET PULSE\nBBVA Banca de Empresas e Instituciones\n\n' +
    (model.isTest ? 'VISTA PREVIA CON DATOS REALES — NO ES UN ENVÍO MENSUAL\n' : '') +
    'Cierre ejecutivo · ' + model.periodLabel + '\n' + marketsNewsletterScopeTitle_(model) + '\n\n' +
    brief.headline + '\n' + brief.summary +
    (model.coverage && !model.coverage.complete ? '\nCobertura de cierre: ' +
      model.coverage.observed + ' de ' + model.coverage.expected +
      ' series; se omiten las series sin dato real.' : '') +
    '\n\n' + lines.join('\n') +
    (effects.length ? '\n\nVOZ NUEVA DEL MES · POR FICHA Y PLATAFORMA\n' +
      effects.join('\n') : '') +
    '\n\n' + marketsWebAppUrl_();
}

function renderMarketsNewsletterHtml_(model) {
  const groups = marketsNewsletterGroups_(model.items);
  const scopeTitle = marketsNewsletterScopeTitle_(model);
  const brief = marketsNewsletterExecutiveBrief_(model);
  const preview = model.isTest ?
    '<tr><td style="padding:12px 24px;background:#FFE761;color:#070E46;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;text-align:center;">VISTA PREVIA · DATOS REALES · NO ES UN ENVÍO MENSUAL</td></tr>' : '';
  const groupHtml = groups.map(function (group) {
    const rows = group.items.map(marketsNewsletterRowHtml_).join('');
    const geographyBrief = model.scope.type === 'SINGLE' ? '' :
      '<span style="display:block;margin-top:4px;color:#85C8FF;font-size:10px;line-height:14px;">' +
      escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(marketsNewsletterGeographyBrief_(group.items)) + '</span>';
    return '<tr><td style="padding:8px 26px 20px;"><table role="presentation" width="100%" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="border:1px solid #CAD1D8;border-radius:10px;overflow:hidden;">' +
      '<tr><td colspan="2" style="padding:13px 16px;background:#070E46;color:#FFFFFF;">' +
      '<strong style="display:block;font-size:15px;">' + escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(group.country) +
      '</strong>' + geographyBrief + '</td></tr>' + rows + '</table></td></tr>';
  }).join('');
  const platformVolumes = Object.keys(model.totals.reviewsByPlatform || {})
    .sort(compareMarketsPlatforms_).map(function (platform) {
    return '<span style="display:inline-block;margin:0 6px 6px 0;padding:7px 10px;border-radius:999px;background:#EAFBFA;color:#070E46;font-size:11px;"><strong>' +
      escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(platform) + '</strong> · ' +
      escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(marketsAlertIntegerLabel_(model.totals.reviewsByPlatform[platform])) +
      '</span>';
  }).join('');
  const coverage = model.coverage && !model.coverage.complete ?
    '<tr><td style="padding:0 26px 18px;"><div style="padding:12px 14px;border-radius:8px;background:#FFF4CC;color:#6B4F00;font-size:12px;line-height:18px;"><strong>Cobertura de cierre: ' +
    escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(model.coverage.observed + ' de ' + model.coverage.expected) +
    ' series.</strong> Las series sin dato real se han omitido; no se han estimado.</div></td></tr>' : '';
  const effectItems = (model.reviewEffects || []).filter(function (item) { return item.shared; });
  const effects = effectItems.length ? '<tr><td style="padding:4px 26px 22px;"><div style="padding:18px;border-radius:10px;background:#EAFBFA;">' +
    '<div style="color:#001391;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.7px;text-transform:uppercase;">FICHA ANDROID COMPARTIDA · SIN ATRIBUCIÓN GEOGRÁFICA</div>' +
    effectItems.slice(0, 5).map(function (item) {
      return '<div style="padding-top:11px;color:#46536D;font-size:13px;line-height:19px;"><strong style="color:#070E46;">' +
        escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(item.country + ' · ' + item.platform) + ':</strong> ' +
        marketsNewsletterRatingLabel_(item.effect.newReviewRating) + '/5 en las nuevas valoraciones · ' +
        escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(marketsNewsletterSigned_(item.effect.gapToPreviousRating)) +
        ' frente al rating acumulado anterior · el acumulado se mueve ' +
        escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(marketsNewsletterSigned_(item.effect.accumulatedRatingChange)) + ' puntos' +
        (item.shared ? ' sobre la ficha compartida completa, sin atribución geográfica.' : '.') + '</div>';
    }).join('') + '</div></td></tr>' : '';
  const appUrl = escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(marketsWebAppLoginUrl_(model.configuredFor || ''));
  return '<!doctype html><html><body style="margin:0;background:#F7F8F8;font-family:\'BBVABentonSans\',\'Benton Sans BBVA\',Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif;color:#000519;">' +
    '<table role="presentation" width="100%" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="background:#F7F8F8;"><tr><td align="center" style="padding:28px 10px;">' +
    '<table role="presentation" width="100%" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="width:100%;max-width:720px;background:#FFFFFF;border-radius:16px;overflow:hidden;">' +
    preview +
    '<tr><td style="padding:26px 30px;background:#001391;color:#FFFFFF;"><img src="cid:bbvaLogo" width="168" alt="BBVA Banca de Empresas e Instituciones" style="display:block;width:168px;height:auto;">' +
    '<div style="margin-top:24px;color:#8BE1E9;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:1px;">MARKET PULSE · CIERRE MENSUAL</div>' +
    '<h1 style="margin:8px 0 0;font-family:Georgia,serif;font-size:31px;line-height:37px;">' +
    escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(model.periodLabel) + '</h1><p style="margin:9px 0 0;color:#85C8FF;font-size:15px;line-height:22px;">' +
    escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(scopeTitle) + '</p></td></tr>' +
    '<tr><td style="padding:24px 30px 12px;"><div style="color:#001391;font-size:10px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.8px;text-transform:uppercase;">LECTURA EJECUTIVA</div>' +
    '<div style="margin-top:7px;font-family:Georgia,serif;color:#070E46;font-size:24px;line-height:31px;">' +
    escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(brief.headline) + '</div><div style="margin-top:10px;color:#46536D;font-size:14px;line-height:21px;">' +
    escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(brief.summary) + '</div></td></tr>' +
    '<tr><td style="padding:10px 26px 22px;"><table role="presentation" width="100%" cellspacing="8" cellpadding="0"><tr>' +
    marketsNewsletterKpiHtml_('Mejoran', model.totals.improving, 'de ' + model.totals.series + ' series') +
    marketsNewsletterKpiHtml_('Retroceden', model.totals.declining, model.totals.stable + ' estables') +
    marketsNewsletterKpiHtml_('Voz nueva', model.totals.effects,
      'fichas con lectura') +
    '</tr></table>' + (model.scope.type !== 'SINGLE' && platformVolumes ? '<div style="padding:4px 8px 0;color:#46536D;font-size:10px;text-transform:uppercase;">Valoraciones por plataforma</div><div style="padding:8px 8px 0;">' +
      platformVolumes + '</div>' : '') + '</td></tr>' + coverage + groupHtml + effects +
    '<tr><td style="padding:4px 30px 28px;"><table role="presentation" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0"><tr><td bgcolor="#001391" style="border-radius:8px;"><a href="' +
    appUrl + '" style="display:block;padding:13px 22px;color:#FFFFFF;text-decoration:none;font-weight:700;">Abrir Market Pulse</a></td></tr></table>' +
    '<p style="margin:16px 0 0;color:#ADB8C2;font-size:10px;line-height:15px;">La lectura ejecutiva se genera exclusivamente a partir de las cifras mostradas. Ratings y volúmenes de Android, iOS y Huawei nunca se combinan. Solo se publican posiciones con cobertura competitiva completa; los volúmenes no atribuibles y los efectos no fiables se presentan como N/D o se omiten.</p></td></tr>' +
    '<tr><td style="padding:16px 30px;background:#070E46;color:#85C8FF;font-size:11px;line-height:17px;"><strong style="color:#FFFFFF;">Market Pulse</strong><br>BBVA Banca de Empresas e Instituciones · Uso interno.</td></tr>' +
    '</table></td></tr></table></body></html>';
}

function marketsNewsletterGroups_(items) {
  const groups = {};
  (items || []).forEach(function (item) {
    const group = groups[item.countryCode] || (groups[item.countryCode] = { country: item.country, items: [] });
    group.items.push(item);
  });
  return Object.keys(groups).sort().map(function (key) {
    groups[key].items.sort(compareMarketsPlatforms_);
    return groups[key];
  });
}

function marketsNewsletterGeographyBrief_(items) {
  const comparable = (items || []).filter(function (item) {
    return toFiniteNumber_(item.monthDelta) !== null;
  });
  if (!comparable.length) return 'Sin cierre anterior comparable';
  if (comparable.length === 1) {
    return comparable[0].platform + ' · ' +
      marketsNewsletterSigned_(comparable[0].monthDelta) + ' en el mes';
  }
  const improving = comparable.filter(function (item) { return item.monthDelta > 0; }).length;
  const declining = comparable.filter(function (item) { return item.monthDelta < 0; }).length;
  const stable = comparable.length - improving - declining;
  const parts = [];
  if (improving) parts.push(improving + (improving === 1 ? ' mejora' : ' mejoran'));
  if (declining) parts.push(declining + (declining === 1 ? ' retrocede' : ' retroceden'));
  if (stable) parts.push(stable + (stable === 1 ? ' estable' : ' estables'));
  return marketsNewsletterJoinFacts_(parts) + ' · ' + comparable.length + ' plataformas';
}

function marketsNewsletterRowHtml_(item) {
  const movementColor = item.monthDelta > 0 ? '#277A3E' : item.monthDelta < 0 ? '#B92A45' : '#46536D';
  const volume = item.newReviews === null ? (item.volumeAvailable ? 'N/D' : 'No atribuible') :
    marketsAlertIntegerLabel_(item.newReviews);
  const voice = item.effect || null;
  const voiceColor = !voice ? '#001391' : voice.gapToPreviousRating > 0.005 ?
    '#277A3E' : voice.gapToPreviousRating < -0.005 ? '#B92A45' : '#46536D';
  const middleLabel = voice ? 'Voz nueva' : 'Actividad del mes';
  const middleValue = voice ? marketsNewsletterRatingLabel_(voice.newReviewRating) + '/5' : volume;
  const middleNote = voice ? volume + ' nuevas valoraciones' :
    item.volumeAvailable ? 'nuevas valoraciones' : 'ficha compartida';
  const annual = item.yearStartRating === null || item.yearDelta === null ? '' :
    'Desde inicio de año: ' + marketsNewsletterSigned_(item.yearDelta) +
    ' (partía de ' + marketsNewsletterRatingLabel_(item.yearStartRating) + ').';
  const voiceGap = voice ? ' La voz nueva queda ' +
    marketsNewsletterSigned_(voice.gapToPreviousRating) +
    ' frente al rating acumulado anterior.' : '';
  return '<tr><td style="width:27%;padding:16px;border-top:1px solid #E2E6EA;vertical-align:top;"><strong style="display:block;color:#070E46;font-size:15px;">' +
    escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(item.platform) + '</strong><span style="display:block;margin-top:10px;color:#46536D;font-size:9px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.4px;text-transform:uppercase;">Posición competitiva</span>' +
    '<span style="display:block;margin-top:3px;color:#070E46;font-size:12px;line-height:17px;">' +
    escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(marketsNewsletterPositionLabel_(item.position)) + '</span></td>' +
    '<td style="padding:16px;border-top:1px solid #E2E6EA;"><table role="presentation" width="100%" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="border-radius:8px;background:#F7F8F8;"><tr>' +
    marketsNewsletterStoryCell_('Cierre anterior',
      marketsNewsletterRatingLabel_(item.monthStartRating), 'punto de partida', '#46536D') +
    marketsNewsletterStoryCell_(middleLabel, middleValue, middleNote, voiceColor) +
    marketsNewsletterStoryCell_('Cierre',
      marketsNewsletterRatingLabel_(item.endRating),
      marketsNewsletterSigned_(item.monthDelta) + ' en el mes', movementColor) +
    '</tr></table><div style="padding:8px 4px 0;color:#46536D;font-size:9px;line-height:14px;">' +
    escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(annual + voiceGap) + '</div></td></tr>';
}

function marketsNewsletterStoryCell_(label, value, note, color) {
  return '<td style="width:33.33%;padding:11px 10px;vertical-align:top;">' +
    '<span style="display:block;color:#46536D;font-size:8px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.25px;text-transform:uppercase;">' +
    escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(label) + '</span><strong style="display:block;margin-top:4px;color:' +
    color + ';font-size:16px;">' + escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(value) +
    '</strong><small style="display:block;margin-top:2px;color:#6B7280;font-size:8px;line-height:11px;">' +
    escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(note) + '</small></td>';
}

function marketsNewsletterKpiHtml_(label, value, note) {
  return '<td style="width:33.33%;padding:14px;border-radius:8px;background:#F7F8F8;text-align:center;"><span style="display:block;color:#46536D;font-size:10px;text-transform:uppercase;">' +
    escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(label) + '</span><strong style="display:block;margin-top:5px;color:#070E46;font-size:23px;">' +
    escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(value) + '</strong><small style="display:block;margin-top:3px;color:#46536D;font-size:10px;">' +
    escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(note) + '</small></td>';
}

function marketsNewsletterScopeTitle_(model) {
  if (model.scope.type === 'SINGLE') return model.scope.countryLabel + ' · ' + model.scope.platformLabel;
  if (model.scope.type === 'MULTI') return model.scope.countries.length + ' geografías seleccionadas · ' +
    model.scope.platformLabel;
  return 'Visión global · ' + model.scope.platformLabel;
}

function marketsNewsletterExecutiveNarrative_(model) {
  const brief = marketsNewsletterExecutiveBrief_(model);
  return [brief.headline, brief.summary].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
}

function marketsNewsletterExecutiveBrief_(model) {
  const totals = model.totals;
  const single = model.scope.type === 'SINGLE';
  const subject = single ? model.scope.countryLabel :
    model.scope.type === 'MULTI' ? 'El perímetro seleccionado' : 'La visión global de BBVA Empresas';
