  if (!totals.series) {
    return {
      headline: subject + ' no dispone de un cierre anterior comparable.',
      summary: 'El cierre actual se muestra con datos reales, pero no se calculan variaciones ni la nota de las nuevas valoraciones sin dos cierres mensuales consecutivos.'
    };
  }
  let headline = '';
  if (totals.series === 1 && model.items.length === 1) {
    const only = model.items[0];
    headline = only.platform + (only.monthDelta > 0 ? ' cierra el mes al alza en ' :
      only.monthDelta < 0 ? ' cierra el mes a la baja en ' : ' cierra el mes sin cambios en ') +
      only.country + '.';
  } else if (totals.declining === totals.series && totals.series > 0) {
    headline = single ? subject + ' cierra el mes con presión en todas las plataformas.' :
      'El mes cierra con presión en todo el perímetro analizado.';
  } else if (totals.improving === totals.series && totals.series > 0) {
    headline = single ? subject + ' avanza en todas las plataformas analizadas.' :
      'El mes cierra con avances en todo el perímetro analizado.';
  } else if (totals.stable === totals.series && totals.series > 0) {
    headline = single ? subject + ' cierra sin movimientos en sus ratings.' :
      'El mes cierra estable, sin movimientos en los ratings analizados.';
  } else if (totals.stable >= totals.improving && totals.stable >= totals.declining) {
    headline = totals.improving > totals.declining ?
      'La estabilidad marca el tono del mes, con un ligero sesgo positivo.' :
      totals.declining > totals.improving ?
        'La estabilidad marca el tono del mes, aunque aparecen señales de presión.' :
        'La estabilidad marca el tono de un mes equilibrado.';
  } else if (totals.declining > totals.improving) {
    headline = single ? subject + ' cierra con un balance de cautela.' :
      'El balance mensual exige atención, aunque la presión no es generalizada.';
  } else if (totals.improving > totals.declining) {
    headline = single ? subject + ' cierra con un balance favorable.' :
      'El balance mensual es favorable, aunque no homogéneo.';
  } else {
    headline = single ? subject + ' presenta un cierre mixto.' :
      'El mes deja un balance equilibrado, con movimientos en ambas direcciones.';
  }

  const facts = [];
  if (totals.series === 1 && model.items.length === 1) {
    // El titular ya resume la dirección; el detalle numérico vive una sola vez
    // en la secuencia Cierre anterior → Voz nueva → Cierre.
  } else {
    const strongest = model.strongest && model.strongest.monthDelta > 0 ? model.strongest : null;
    const weakest = model.weakest && model.weakest.monthDelta < 0 ? model.weakest : null;
    if (weakest) facts.push('El principal foco de atención está en ' +
      marketsNewsletterItemLabel_(weakest, model) + ' (' +
      marketsNewsletterSigned_(weakest.monthDelta) + ').');
    else if (strongest) facts.push('El avance más destacado se registra en ' +
      marketsNewsletterItemLabel_(strongest, model) + ' (' +
      marketsNewsletterSigned_(strongest.monthDelta) + ').');
  }

  const voiceContext = marketsNewsletterVoiceContext_(model);
  if (voiceContext) facts.push(voiceContext);
  const yearContext = marketsNewsletterYearContext_(model);
  if (yearContext) facts.push(yearContext);
  const positionContext = marketsNewsletterPositionContext_(model);
  if (positionContext) facts.push(positionContext);
  const volumeContext = marketsNewsletterVolumeContext_(model);
  if (!single && volumeContext) facts.push(volumeContext);
  return { headline: headline, summary: facts.slice(0, single ? 3 : 2).join(' ') };
}

function marketsNewsletterItemLabel_(item, model) {
  return model.scope.type === 'SINGLE' ? item.platform : item.country + ' · ' + item.platform;
}

function marketsNewsletterCountLabel_(count) {
  return ({ 2: 'dos', 3: 'tres', 4: 'cuatro', 5: 'cinco', 6: 'seis',
    7: 'siete', 8: 'ocho', 9: 'nueve', 10: 'diez' })[count] || String(count);
}

function marketsNewsletterJoinFacts_(parts) {
  if (parts.length < 2) return parts[0] || '';
  if (parts.length === 2) return parts[0] + ' y ' + parts[1].toLowerCase();
  return parts.slice(0, -1).join(', ') + ' y ' + parts[parts.length - 1].toLowerCase();
}

function marketsNewsletterYearContext_(model) {
  const item = (model.items || []).find(function (candidate) {
    return candidate.monthDelta < 0 && candidate.yearDelta > 0;
  });
  if (item) {
    return 'Pese al ajuste mensual, ' + marketsNewsletterItemLabel_(item, model) +
      ' se mantiene ' + marketsNewsletterSigned_(item.yearDelta) + ' sobre el inicio de año.';
  }
  const rebound = (model.items || []).find(function (candidate) {
    return candidate.monthDelta > 0 && candidate.yearDelta < 0;
  });
  return rebound ? 'El avance mensual de ' + marketsNewsletterItemLabel_(rebound, model) +
    ' todavía deja el rating ' + marketsNewsletterSigned_(rebound.yearDelta) +
    ' frente al inicio de año.' : '';
}

function marketsNewsletterPositionContext_(model) {
  const comparable = (model.items || []).filter(function (item) {
    return item.position && item.position.available;
  });
  if (!comparable.length) return '';
  const leaders = comparable.filter(function (item) { return item.position.rank === 1; });
  if (leaders.length) {
    if (comparable.length === 1) {
      return 'BBVA mantiene el liderazgo frente a la banca comparable.';
    }
    return 'BBVA lidera en ' + leaders.length + ' de ' + comparable.length +
      ' ' + (model.scope.type === 'SINGLE' ? 'plataformas comparables.' : 'series comparables.');
  }
  const best = comparable.slice().sort(function (a, b) {
    return a.position.rank - b.position.rank || b.endRating - a.endRating;
  })[0];
  return 'En ' + marketsNewsletterItemLabel_(best, model) + ', BBVA ocupa la ' +
    best.position.rank + '.ª posición entre ' + best.position.total + ' bancos comparados.';
}

function marketsNewsletterVolumeContext_(model) {
  const byPlatform = model.totals && model.totals.reviewsByPlatform || {};
  const platforms = Object.keys(byPlatform).filter(function (platform) {
    return toFiniteNumber_(byPlatform[platform]) !== null && byPlatform[platform] > 0;
  }).sort(compareMarketsPlatforms_);
  if (!platforms.length) return '';
  const facts = platforms.map(function (platform) {
    return platform + ' registra ' + marketsAlertIntegerLabel_(byPlatform[platform]) +
      ' nuevas valoraciones';
  });
  const joined = facts.length < 2 ? facts[0] :
    facts.length === 2 ? facts[0] + ' y ' + facts[1] :
      facts.slice(0, -1).join(', ') + ' y ' + facts[facts.length - 1];
  return 'Volumen separado por plataforma: ' + joined + '.';
}

function marketsNewsletterVoiceContext_(model) {
  const effects = (model.reviewEffects || []).filter(function (item) {
    return item.effect && toFiniteNumber_(item.effect.newReviewRating) !== null;
  });
  if (!effects.length) return '';
  if (effects.length === 1) {
    const item = effects[0];
    const label = model.scope.type === 'SINGLE' && !item.shared ?
      item.platform : item.country + ' · ' + item.platform;
    const gap = item.effect.gapToPreviousRating;
    const change = item.effect.accumulatedRatingChange;
    const quality = gap > 0.005 ? 'se sitúan por encima del rating previo' :
      gap < -0.005 ? 'quedan por debajo del rating previo' :
        'se mantienen alineadas con el rating previo';
    const consequence = change > 0.005 ? 'refuerzan el cierre' :
      change < -0.005 ? 'presionan el cierre' :
        'no alteran de forma visible el acumulado';
    return (model.scope.type === 'SINGLE' ? 'Las nuevas valoraciones ' :
      'En ' + label + ', las nuevas valoraciones ') + quality + ' y ' + consequence + '.';
  }
  const positive = effects.filter(function (item) {
    return item.effect.gapToPreviousRating > 0.005;
  }).length;
  const negative = effects.filter(function (item) {
    return item.effect.gapToPreviousRating < -0.005;
  }).length;
  const stable = effects.length - positive - negative;
  const notable = effects.slice().sort(function (a, b) {
    return Math.abs(b.effect.accumulatedRatingChange) -
      Math.abs(a.effect.accumulatedRatingChange);
  })[0];
  const notableLabel = model.scope.type === 'SINGLE' && !notable.shared ?
    notable.platform : notable.shared ? notable.country :
      notable.country + ' · ' + notable.platform;
  const count = marketsNewsletterCountLabel_(effects.length);
  const measurableScope = model.scope.type === 'SINGLE' ?
    'las ' + count + ' plataformas donde puede medirse' :
    'los ' + count + ' casos donde es posible medir su efecto';
  let reading = '';
  if (positive === effects.length) {
    reading = 'Las nuevas valoraciones aportan una señal favorable en ' +
      measurableScope + '.';
  } else if (negative === effects.length) {
    reading = 'Las nuevas valoraciones añaden presión en ' +
      measurableScope + '.';
  } else if (positive > negative) {
    reading = 'La voz del cliente ofrece un balance favorable en ' +
      measurableScope + '.';
  } else if (negative > positive) {
    reading = 'La voz del cliente aconseja cautela en ' +
      measurableScope + '.';
  } else if (stable === effects.length) {
    reading = 'Las nuevas valoraciones se mantienen alineadas con los ratings previos.';
  } else {
    reading = 'La voz del cliente presenta señales mixtas en ' +
      measurableScope + '.';
  }
  const change = notable.effect.accumulatedRatingChange;
  return reading + (Math.abs(change) < 0.005 ?
    ' No se observa un efecto relevante en los ratings acumulados.' :
    ' El mayor efecto en el cierre corresponde a ' + notableLabel +
      ' (' + marketsNewsletterSigned_(change) + ').');
}

function marketsNewsletterPositionLabel_(position) {
  if (!position || !position.available) return 'No disponible';
  if (position.rank === 1) return 'Líder entre ' + position.total +
    (position.total === 1 ? ' banco' : ' bancos');
  return position.rank + '.ª posición entre ' + position.total +
    (position.total === 1 ? ' banco' : ' bancos');
}

function marketsNewsletterRatingLabel_(value) {
  const number = toFiniteNumber_(value);
  return number === null ? 'N/D' : number.toFixed(2).replace('.', ',');
}

function marketsNewsletterSigned_(value) {
  const number = toFiniteNumber_(value);
  if (number === null) return 'N/D';
  if (Math.abs(number) < 0.005) return '0,00';
  return (number > 0 ? '+' : '') + number.toFixed(2).replace('.', ',');
}

function marketsNewsletterPeriodLabel_(period) {
  const months = ['enero', 'febrero', 'marzo', 'abril', 'mayo', 'junio',
    'julio', 'agosto', 'septiembre', 'octubre', 'noviembre', 'diciembre'];
  const parts = period.split('-');
  return months[Number(parts[1]) - 1] + ' ' + parts[0];
}

function marketsNewsletterAuditEvent_(recipient, model, eventKey, source) {
  return {
    timestamp: new Date(), eventKey: eventKey,
    referenceDate: dateFromKey_(model.period + '-01'),
    platform: model.scope.platformLabel, countryCode: model.scope.countries.join(','),
    type: source === 'NEWSLETTER_TEST' ? 'NEWSLETTER_TEST' : 'NEWSLETTER_MONTHLY',
    severity: 'POSITIVE', detail: marketsNewsletterExecutiveNarrative_(model),
    recipients: recipient, runId: (source === 'NEWSLETTER_TEST' ? 'NEWS-TEST-' : 'NEWS-') + model.period,
    source: source, userEmail: recipient, ruleId: 'MONTHLY_NEWSLETTER',
    metricValue: model.totals.series, threshold: '', appKey: '', direction: 'SUMMARY'
  };
}


========================================
ARCHIVO: 57_MonitorHealth.gs
========================================

/** Verifica que el proceso diario termino con datos utilizables y un canal de alertas operativo. */
function checkDailyRatingMonitorHealth() {
  const ss = openMarketsSpreadsheet_();
  ensureWorkbookSchema_(ss);
  const control = readControl_(ss);
  if (!parseBoolean_(control.MONITOR_HEALTH_ENABLED, true)) {
    return { healthy: true, status: 'DISABLED', notified: false };
  }
  const now = new Date();
  const dateKey = Utilities.formatDate(now, trimString_(control.TIME_ZONE) || MARKETS_CONFIG.timeZone, 'yyyy-MM-dd');
  const latest = readLatestRunForHealth_(ss, dateKey);
  const health = evaluateMarketsMonitorHealth_(latest, dateKey);
  health.notified = false;
  if (health.healthy) return health;
  const properties = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const notificationKey = 'MARKETS_MONITOR_HEALTH_ALERT_' + dateKey;
  if (properties.getProperty(notificationKey)) {
    health.notificationStatus = 'ALREADY_SENT';
    return health;
  }
  const recipient = normalizeMarketsAlertEmail_(trimString_(control.MONITOR_ADMIN_EMAIL) || MARKETS_ALERT_EMAIL.senderAddress);
  const delivery = sendMarketsMonitorHealthEmail_(recipient, health, now);
  properties.setProperty(notificationKey, JSON.stringify({
    sentAt: now.toISOString(), messageId: delivery.messageId, status: health.status
  }));
  health.notified = true;
  health.notificationStatus = 'SENT';
  health.recipient = recipient;
  health.messageId = delivery.messageId;
  return health;
}

function readLatestRunForHealth_(ss, expectedDateKey) {
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.runs);
  if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() <= 1) return null;
  const width = Math.min(sheet.getLastColumn(), MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.runs.length);
  const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, width).getValues()[0];
  const indexes = indexHeaders_(headers);
  const count = Math.min(20, sheet.getLastRow() - 1);
  const rows = sheet.getRange(sheet.getLastRow() - count + 1, 1, count, width).getValues();
  for (let index = rows.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
    const row = rows[index];
    const startedAt = row[indexes.started_at];
    if (!(startedAt instanceof Date)) continue;
    const dateKey = Utilities.formatDate(startedAt, MARKETS_CONFIG.timeZone, 'yyyy-MM-dd');
    if (dateKey !== expectedDateKey) continue;
    return {
      startedAt: startedAt,
      runId: trimString_(row[indexes.run_id]),
      status: trimString_(row[indexes.status]).toUpperCase(),
      appsTotal: toNonNegativeInteger_(row[indexes.apps_total]) || 0,
      appsOk: toNonNegativeInteger_(row[indexes.apps_ok]) || 0,
      rowsUpserted: toNonNegativeInteger_(row[indexes.rows_upserted]) || 0,
      detail: trimString_(row[indexes.detail]),
      alertDeliveryStatus: indexes.alert_delivery_status === undefined ? '' :
        trimString_(row[indexes.alert_delivery_status]).toUpperCase(),
      alertDeliveryDetail: indexes.alert_delivery_detail === undefined ? '' :
        trimString_(row[indexes.alert_delivery_detail])
    };
  }
  return null;
}

function evaluateMarketsMonitorHealth_(run, expectedDateKey) {
  if (!run) {
    return {
      healthy: false, status: 'MISSING', dateKey: expectedDateKey, runId: '',
      reason: 'No existe ninguna ejecucion registrada para la fecha esperada.'
    };
  }
  const dataHealthy = ['OK', 'PARTIAL'].indexOf(run.status) >= 0 &&
    run.appsTotal > 0 && run.appsOk > 0 && run.rowsUpserted > 0;
  const deliveryFailed = ['ERROR', 'PARTIAL'].indexOf(run.alertDeliveryStatus) >= 0;
  if (dataHealthy && !deliveryFailed) {
    return {
      healthy: true, status: 'HEALTHY', dateKey: expectedDateKey, runId: run.runId,
      runStatus: run.status, alertDeliveryStatus: run.alertDeliveryStatus || 'LEGACY'
    };
  }
  const reasons = [];
  if (!dataHealthy) {
    reasons.push('El proceso termino en ' + (run.status || 'estado desconocido') +
      ' con ' + run.appsOk + '/' + run.appsTotal + ' aplicaciones correctas y ' +
      run.rowsUpserted + ' filas actualizadas.');
  }
  if (deliveryFailed) {
    reasons.push('El canal de alertas termino en ' + run.alertDeliveryStatus +
      (run.alertDeliveryDetail ? ': ' + run.alertDeliveryDetail : '.'));
  }
  return {
    healthy: false, status: dataHealthy ? 'ALERT_DELIVERY_FAILED' : 'RUN_FAILED',
    dateKey: expectedDateKey, runId: run.runId, runStatus: run.status,
    alertDeliveryStatus: run.alertDeliveryStatus || 'UNKNOWN', reason: reasons.join(' '),
    detail: run.detail
  };
}

function sendMarketsMonitorHealthEmail_(recipient, health, sentAt) {
  const executor = normalizeEmailForComparison_(Session.getEffectiveUser().getEmail());
  if (!executor) throw new Error('No se puede identificar la cuenta propietaria del trigger de vigilancia.');
  const subject = '[ACCIÓN REQUERIDA] Market Pulse · Proceso diario · ' + health.dateKey;
  const reason = trimString_(health.reason || 'La ejecucion diaria necesita revision.');
  const detail = trimString_(health.detail);
  const runId = trimString_(health.runId) || 'sin ejecucion';
  const appUrl = marketsWebAppUrl_();
  const loginUrl = marketsWebAppLoginUrl_(recipient);
  const plain = 'MARKET PULSE\nBBVA Banca de Empresas e Instituciones\nMONITOR TÉCNICO\n\n' + reason + '\n\nFecha esperada: ' +
    health.dateKey + '\nRun: ' + runId + (detail ? '\nDetalle: ' + detail : '') +
    '\n\nEjecuta runNow() manualmente y revisa RUN_LOG.\n' + loginUrl + '\nURL directa: ' + appUrl;
  const html = '<!doctype html><html><body style="margin:0;background:#F7F8F8;font-family:\'BBVABentonSans\',\'Benton Sans BBVA\',Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif;color:#000519;">' +
    '<div style="max-width:620px;margin:24px auto;background:#FFFFFF;border-radius:12px;overflow:hidden;">' +
    '<div style="padding:22px 26px;background:#001391;color:#FFFFFF;"><img src="cid:bbvaLogo" width="156" alt="BBVA Banca de Empresas e Instituciones" style="display:block;width:156px;height:auto;"><div style="margin-top:20px;color:#8BE1E9;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.8px;">MARKET PULSE · MONITOR TÉCNICO</div><h1 style="margin:8px 0 0;font-size:24px;line-height:30px;">El proceso diario necesita revisión</h1></div>' +
    '<div style="padding:24px 26px;"><p style="margin:0 0 18px;line-height:22px;">' + escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(reason) + '</p>' +
    '<div style="padding:14px 16px;border-left:4px solid #B92A45;background:#F9E9ED;font-size:13px;line-height:20px;"><strong>Fecha esperada:</strong> ' + escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(health.dateKey) + '<br><strong>Run:</strong> ' + escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(runId) + (detail ? '<br><strong>Detalle:</strong> ' + escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(detail) : '') + '</div>' +
    '<p style="margin:20px 0 0;font-size:14px;line-height:21px;">Ejecuta <strong>runNow()</strong> manualmente y revisa <strong>RUN_LOG</strong>.</p>' +
    '<p style="margin:18px 0 0;"><a href="' + escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(loginUrl) + '" style="color:#001391;font-weight:700;">Ir a Market Pulse</a></p>' +
    '<p style="margin:18px 0 0;color:#ADB8C2;font-size:10px;line-height:15px;">Comprobación automática ' + escapeMarketsAlertHtml_(marketsAlertDateLabel_(sentAt)) + ' · aviso único por día.</p></div></div></body></html>';
  const token = Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, '');
  const relatedBoundary = 'bbva_health_related_' + token;
  const alternativeBoundary = 'bbva_health_alternative_' + token;
  const image = buildMarketsAlertInlineImages_().bbvaLogo;
  const crlf = '\r\n';
  const mime = [
    'From: ' + marketsAlertEncodedHeader_('BBVA Market Pulse · Monitor técnico') + ' <' + executor + '>',
    'To: ' + recipient,
    'Reply-To: ' + recipient,
    'Subject: ' + marketsAlertEncodedHeader_(subject),
    'Date: ' + sentAt.toUTCString(),
    'Message-ID: <' + token + '@voc-commercial.bbva.com>',
    'MIME-Version: 1.0',
    'Content-Type: multipart/related; boundary="' + relatedBoundary + '"', '',
    '--' + relatedBoundary,
    'Content-Type: multipart/alternative; boundary="' + alternativeBoundary + '"', '',
    '--' + alternativeBoundary, 'Content-Type: text/plain; charset="UTF-8"',
    'Content-Transfer-Encoding: base64', '', marketsAlertMimeText_(plain),
    '--' + alternativeBoundary, 'Content-Type: text/html; charset="UTF-8"',
    'Content-Transfer-Encoding: base64', '', marketsAlertMimeText_(html),
    '--' + alternativeBoundary + '--', '',
    '--' + relatedBoundary, 'Content-Type: image/png; name="bbva-bei.png"',
    'Content-Transfer-Encoding: base64', 'Content-ID: <bbvaLogo>',
    'Content-Disposition: inline; filename="bbva-bei.png"', '',
    marketsAlertMimeBytes_(image.getBytes()),
    '--' + relatedBoundary + '--', ''
  ].join(crlf);
  const accepted = Gmail.Users.Messages.send({
    raw: Utilities.base64EncodeWebSafe(mime, Utilities.Charset.UTF_8).replace(/=+$/g, '')
  }, 'me');
  if (!accepted || !trimString_(accepted.id)) {
    throw new Error('Gmail no ha aceptado el aviso operativo dirigido a ' + recipient + '.');
  }
  return { recipient: recipient, messageId: trimString_(accepted.id), threadId: trimString_(accepted.threadId) };
}

function sendMarketsMonitorHealthTestEmail() {
  const ss = openMarketsSpreadsheet_();
  ensureWorkbookSchema_(ss);
  const control = readControl_(ss);
  const now = new Date();
  const dateKey = Utilities.formatDate(now, MARKETS_CONFIG.timeZone, 'yyyy-MM-dd');
  const recipient = normalizeMarketsAlertEmail_(trimString_(control.MONITOR_ADMIN_EMAIL) || MARKETS_ALERT_EMAIL.senderAddress);
  const health = {
    healthy: false, status: 'TEST', dateKey: dateKey, runId: 'PRUEBA-MONITOR',
    reason: 'Prueba del canal de vigilancia. No existe una incidencia real.', detail: ''
  };
  return sendMarketsMonitorHealthEmail_(recipient, health, now);
}


========================================
ARCHIVO: 58_Admin.gs
========================================

/** Autorizacion y observabilidad privada para responsables de Market Pulse. */
function marketsAdministratorEmails_(control) {
  return uniqueStrings_(trimString_(control && control.ADMIN_EMAILS).split(/[;,\s]+/)
    .map(normalizeEmailForComparison_))
    .filter(function (email) { return /^[^\s@]+@bbva\.com$/.test(email); });
}

function isMarketsAdministrator_(email, control) {
  return marketsAdministratorEmails_(control).indexOf(normalizeEmailForComparison_(email)) >= 0;
}

function seedInitialMarketsAdministrator_(ss) {
  const control = readControl_(ss);
  if (marketsAdministratorEmails_(control).length) return control.ADMIN_EMAILS;
  const owner = normalizeEmailForComparison_(Session.getEffectiveUser().getEmail());
  if (!owner) throw new Error('No se ha podido identificar al propietario inicial para ADMIN_EMAILS.');
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.control);
  const rows = sheet.getRange(2, 1, Math.max(1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1), 1).getValues();
  const index = rows.findIndex(function (row) { return trimString_(row[0]) === 'ADMIN_EMAILS'; });
  if (index < 0) throw new Error('Falta ADMIN_EMAILS en CONTROL. Ejecuta setupProject() con el contrato vigente.');
  sheet.getRange(index + 2, 2).setValue(owner);
  return owner;
}

function getMarketsUserContext() {
  const email = normalizeEmailForComparison_(currentMarketsUserEmail_());
  const cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  const cacheKey = 'markets-web:administrator-emails:' + MARKETS_CONFIG.codeVersion;
  let administrators = null;
  try {
    const cached = cache.get(cacheKey);
    administrators = cached ? JSON.parse(cached) : null;
  } catch (ignored) {}
  if (!Array.isArray(administrators)) {
    const ss = openMarketsSpreadsheet_();
    requireCurrentSheetContract_(ss, MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.control, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.control);
    administrators = marketsAdministratorEmails_(readControl_(ss));
    try { cache.put(cacheKey, JSON.stringify(administrators), 300); } catch (ignored) {}
  }
  return { email: email, isAdmin: administrators.indexOf(email) >= 0 };
}

function requireMarketsAdministrator_() {
  const email = currentMarketsUserEmail_();
  const ss = openMarketsSpreadsheet_();
  requireCurrentSheetContract_(ss, MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.control, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.control);
  const control = readControl_(ss);
  if (!isMarketsAdministrator_(email, control)) {
    throw new Error('Acceso denegado. Esta cuenta no esta autorizada como administradora.');
  }
  return { email: email, ss: ss, control: control };
}

function getMarketsAdminDashboard() {
  const access = requireMarketsAdministrator_();
  const ss = access.ss;
  [
    [MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.runs, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.runs],
    [MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.fetchLog, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.fetchLog],
    [MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.alerts, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.alerts],
    [MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.alertPreferences, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.alertPreferences]
  ].forEach(function (definition) {
    requireCurrentSheetContract_(ss, definition[0], definition[1]);
  });
  let sender = { available: false, address: trimString_(access.control.ALERT_SENDER_ALIAS), verificationStatus: 'UNKNOWN' };
  try { sender = marketsAlertSenderStatus_(ss); } catch (error) { sender.error = errorToString_(error); }
  const runs = readRecentMarketsAdminRuns_(ss, 14);
  const subscribers = readAllMarketsAlertPreferences_(ss);
  const issues = readRecentMarketsAdminFetchIssues_(ss, 40);
  const alerts = readRecentMarketsAdminAlertEvents_(ss, 30);
  const usage = readMarketsUsageAnalytics_(ss,
    trimString_(access.control.TIME_ZONE) || MARKETS_CONFIG.timeZone);
  return {
    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    administrator: access.email,
    system: {
      codeVersion: MARKETS_CONFIG.codeVersion,
      webVersion: MARKETS_WEB.version,
      webAppUrl: marketsWebAppUrl_(),
      timeZone: trimString_(access.control.TIME_ZONE) || MARKETS_CONFIG.timeZone,
      dailySchedule: String(MARKETS_CONFIG.runHour).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(MARKETS_CONFIG.runMinute).padStart(2, '0'),
      healthSchedule: String(MARKETS_CONFIG.healthCheckHour).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(MARKETS_CONFIG.healthCheckMinute).padStart(2, '0'),
      newsletterSchedule: 'Día 1 · ' + String(MARKETS_CONFIG.newsletterHour).padStart(2, '0') + ':' +
        String(MARKETS_CONFIG.newsletterMinute).padStart(2, '0'),
      emailDeliveryEnabled: parseBoolean_(access.control.SEND_ALERT_EMAILS, false),
      newsletterDeliveryEnabled: marketsNewsletterDeliveryEnabled_(access.control),
      healthMonitorEnabled: parseBoolean_(access.control.MONITOR_HEALTH_ENABLED, true),
      healthRecipient: trimString_(access.control.MONITOR_ADMIN_EMAIL),
      administratorEmails: marketsAdministratorEmails_(access.control),
      senderAddress: sender.address,
      senderAvailable: Boolean(sender.available),
      senderVerificationStatus: trimString_(sender.verificationStatus),
      senderError: trimString_(sender.error)
    },
    summary: {
      activeSubscribers: subscribers.filter(function (item) {
        return item.active || item.newsletterEnabled;
      }).length,
      pausedSubscribers: subscribers.filter(function (item) {
        return !item.active && !item.newsletterEnabled;
      }).length,
      recentRuns: runs.length,
      recentIssues: issues.length,
      recentAlertEvents: alerts.length,
      usersThisMonth: usage.kpis.usersMonth
    },
    recentRuns: runs,
    fetchIssues: issues,
    subscribers: subscribers.map(function (item) {
      const client = alertPreferencesForClient_(item);
      client.lastSeenAt = adminDateTime_(item.lastSeenAt);
      client.lastTestAt = adminDateTime_(item.lastTestAt);
      client.lastDeliveryAt = adminDateTime_(item.lastDeliveryAt);
      client.updatedAt = adminDateTime_(item.updatedAt);
      client.registeredAt = adminDateTime_(item.registeredAt);
      return client;
    }),
    recentAlerts: alerts,
    usage: usage
  };
}

/**
 * Alta y mantenimiento administrativo de suscriptores. La identidad que se
 * modifica procede del argumento separado, nunca de la sesion ni del payload.
 */
function saveMarketsAdminSubscriber(email, payload) {
  const access = requireMarketsAdministrator_();
  const targetEmail = normalizeMarketsAlertEmail_(email);
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  if (!lock.tryLock(MARKETS_CONFIG.lockTimeoutMs)) {
    throw new Error('Las suscripciones estan siendo actualizadas. Intentalo de nuevo.');
  }
  try {
    [
      [MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.alertPreferences, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.alertPreferences],
      [MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.ratings, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.ratings],
      [MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.alerts, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.alerts]
    ].forEach(function (definition) {
      requireCurrentSheetContract_(access.ss, definition[0], definition[1]);
    });
    const previous = readMarketsAlertPreferences_(access.ss, targetEmail);
    const source = Object.assign({}, payload || {}, {
      email: targetEmail,
      registeredAt: previous.registeredAt || new Date(),
      lastSeenAt: previous.lastSeenAt || '',
      lastTestAt: previous.lastTestAt || '',
      lastDeliveryAt: previous.lastDeliveryAt || ''
    });
    const saved = upsertMarketsAlertPreferences_(access.ss,
      normalizeMarketsAlertPreferences_(source, targetEmail), access.email);
    let acknowledgements = [];
    if (!previous.persisted && saved.active && saved.ytdDropEnabled &&
        !isMarketsAdministrator_(targetEmail, access.control)) {
      const ratings = readRatingObjects_(access.ss);
      const referenceDate = latestMarketsRatingDate_(ratings, saved);
      if (referenceDate) {
        acknowledgements = buildMarketsEnrollmentYtdAcknowledgements_(ratings, referenceDate, saved);
        appendAlertEvents_(access.ss, acknowledgements);
      }
    }
    const client = alertPreferencesForClient_(saved);
    client.enrollmentYtdAcknowledged = acknowledgements.length;
    return client;
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

/**
 * Ejecuta una recuperacion completa bajo demanda. runDailyRatingMonitor deja
 * la auditoria en RUN_LOG y conserva el mismo bloqueo que la carga programada.
 */
function requestMarketsAdminRatingRecovery() {
  const access = requireMarketsAdministrator_();
  const result = runDailyRatingMonitor();
  return Object.assign({}, result, {
    requestedBy: access.email,
    requestedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    mode: 'MANUAL_ADMIN_RECOVERY'
  });
}

function readRecentMarketsAdminRuns_(ss, limit) {
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.runs);
  if (sheet.getLastRow() <= 1) return [];
  const headers = MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.runs;
  const indexes = indexHeaders_(headers);
  const count = Math.min(limit, sheet.getLastRow() - 1);
  return sheet.getRange(sheet.getLastRow() - count + 1, 1, count, headers.length).getValues().reverse()
    .filter(function (row) { return trimString_(row[indexes.run_id]); })
    .map(function (row) {
      return {
        startedAt: adminDateTime_(row[indexes.started_at]), runId: trimString_(row[indexes.run_id]),
        status: trimString_(row[indexes.status]), appsTotal: adminNumber_(row[indexes.apps_total]),
        appsOk: adminNumber_(row[indexes.apps_ok]), appsNoRating: adminNumber_(row[indexes.apps_no_rating]),
        appsError: adminNumber_(row[indexes.apps_error]), rowsUpserted: adminNumber_(row[indexes.rows_upserted]),
        durationMs: adminNumber_(row[indexes.duration_ms]), subscribers: adminNumber_(row[indexes.alert_subscribers]),
        emailsSent: adminNumber_(row[indexes.emails_sent]), deliveryStatus: trimString_(row[indexes.alert_delivery_status]),
        detail: trimString_(row[indexes.detail]), deliveryDetail: trimString_(row[indexes.alert_delivery_detail])
      };
    });
}

function readRecentMarketsAdminFetchIssues_(ss, limit) {
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.fetchLog);
  if (sheet.getLastRow() <= 1) return [];
  const headers = MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.fetchLog;
  const indexes = indexHeaders_(headers);
  const count = Math.min(600, sheet.getLastRow() - 1);
  return sheet.getRange(sheet.getLastRow() - count + 1, 1, count, headers.length).getValues().reverse()
    .filter(function (row) {
      return isMarketsCriticalPlatform_(row[indexes.platform]) && trimString_(row[indexes.status]) !== 'OK';
    })
    .slice(0, limit).map(function (row) {
      return {
        fetchedAt: adminDateTime_(row[indexes.fetched_at]), runId: trimString_(row[indexes.run_id]),
        appKey: trimString_(row[indexes.app_key]), platform: trimString_(row[indexes.platform]),
        countryCode: trimString_(row[indexes.country_code]), status: trimString_(row[indexes.status]),
        httpStatus: adminNumber_(row[indexes.http_status]), detail: trimString_(row[indexes.detail])
      };
    });
}

function readRecentMarketsAdminAlertEvents_(ss, limit) {
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.alerts);
  if (sheet.getLastRow() <= 1) return [];
  const headers = MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.alerts;
  const indexes = indexHeaders_(headers);
  const count = Math.min(limit, sheet.getLastRow() - 1);
  return sheet.getRange(sheet.getLastRow() - count + 1, 1, count, headers.length).getValues().reverse()
    .filter(function (row) { return trimString_(row[indexes.event_key]); })
    .map(function (row) {
      return {
        timestamp: adminDateTime_(row[indexes.timestamp]), type: trimString_(row[indexes.type]),
        severity: trimString_(row[indexes.severity]), platform: trimString_(row[indexes.platform]),
        countryCode: trimString_(row[indexes.country_code]), userEmail: normalizeEmailForComparison_(row[indexes.user_email] || row[indexes.recipients]),
        detail: trimString_(row[indexes.detail]), runId: trimString_(row[indexes.run_id])
      };
    });
}

function adminDateTime_(value) {
  return value instanceof Date ? value.toISOString() : trimString_(value);
}

function adminNumber_(value) {
  const parsed = toFiniteNumber_(value);
  return parsed === null ? null : parsed;
}


========================================
ARCHIVO: 59_UsageAnalytics.gs
========================================

/** Telemetria funcional minima para adopcion y experiencia de usuario. */
var MARKETS_USAGE = Object.freeze({
  maxEventsPerBatch: 25,
  maxAnalyticsRows: 200000,
  views: Object.freeze(['overview', 'benchmark', 'evolution', 'alerts', 'admin']),
  eventTypes: Object.freeze(['SESSION_START', 'VIEW', 'FILTER', 'ACTION', 'ERROR']),
  devices: Object.freeze(['DESKTOP', 'MOBILE', 'TABLET', 'UNKNOWN'])
});

function ensureMarketsUsageSheet_(ss) {
  let sheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.usage);
  const created = !sheet;
  sheet = sheet || ss.insertSheet(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.usage);
  ensureCurrentSheetContract_(sheet, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.usage);
  if (created) applyTableStyle_(sheet, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.usage.length);
  return sheet;
}

function recordMarketsUsageEvents(events) {
  const email = currentMarketsUserEmail_();
  const source = Array.isArray(events) ? events.slice(0, MARKETS_USAGE.maxEventsPerBatch) : [];
  const rows = source.map(function (event) {
    return normalizeMarketsUsageEvent_(event, email);
  }).filter(Boolean);
  if (!rows.length) return { recorded: 0 };
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  if (!lock.tryLock(MARKETS_CONFIG.lockTimeoutMs)) return { recorded: 0, deferred: true };
  try {
    const ss = openMarketsSpreadsheet_();
    const sheet = ensureMarketsUsageSheet_(ss);
    const start = sheet.getLastRow() + 1;
    ensureSheetCapacity_(sheet, start + rows.length - 1, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.usage.length);
    sheet.getRange(start, 1, rows.length, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.usage.length).setValues(rows);
    CacheService.getScriptCache().remove(usageAnalyticsCacheKey_(MARKETS_CONFIG.timeZone));
    return { recorded: rows.length };
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

function normalizeMarketsUsageEvent_(event, email) {
  const input = event || {};
  const eventType = usageToken_(input.eventType, 32);
  if (MARKETS_USAGE.eventTypes.indexOf(eventType) < 0) return null;
  const view = trimString_(input.view).toLowerCase();
  const safeView = MARKETS_USAGE.views.indexOf(view) >= 0 ? view : '';
  const sessionId = usageToken_(input.sessionId, 80);
  if (!sessionId) return null;
  const device = usageToken_(input.device, 16);
  return [
    new Date(), normalizeMarketsAlertEmail_(email), sessionId, eventType, safeView,
    usageToken_(input.action, 64), usageDetail_(input.detail, 240),
    usageDetail_(input.webVersion, 32),
    MARKETS_USAGE.devices.indexOf(device) >= 0 ? device : 'UNKNOWN'
  ];
}

function usageToken_(value, maximum) {
  return trimString_(value).toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9_:\-|]/g, '').slice(0, maximum);
}

function usageDetail_(value, maximum) {
  return trimString_(value).replace(/[\u0000-\u001f\u007f]+/g, ' ').slice(0, maximum);
}

function readMarketsUsageAnalytics_(ss, timeZone, now) {
  const zone = timeZone || MARKETS_CONFIG.timeZone;
  const cache = !now ? CacheService.getScriptCache() : null;
  const cacheKey = usageAnalyticsCacheKey_(zone);
  if (cache) {
    const cached = cache.get(cacheKey);
    if (cached) {
      try { return JSON.parse(cached); } catch (ignored) {}
    }
  }
  const sheet = ensureMarketsUsageSheet_(ss);
  const totalRows = Math.max(0, sheet.getLastRow() - 1);
  const rowCount = Math.min(MARKETS_USAGE.maxAnalyticsRows, totalRows);
  const truncated = totalRows > rowCount;
  const rows = rowCount ? sheet.getRange(sheet.getLastRow() - rowCount + 1, 1, rowCount,
    MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.usage.length).getValues() : [];
  const analytics = buildMarketsUsageAnalytics_(rows, zone, now || new Date(), {
    totalRows: totalRows, rowsAnalyzed: rowCount, truncated: truncated
  });
  if (cache) cache.put(cacheKey, JSON.stringify(analytics), 300);
  return analytics;
}

function usageAnalyticsCacheKey_(timeZone) {
  return 'markets:usage:analytics:' + MARKETS_CONFIG.codeVersion + ':' + trimString_(timeZone);
}

function buildMarketsUsageAnalytics_(rows, timeZone, now, tracking) {
  const indexes = indexHeaders_(MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.usage);
  const today = usageDateKey_(now, timeZone);
  const month = today.slice(0, 7);
  const year = today.slice(0, 4);
  const currentWeek = usageWeekKey_(today);
  const previousWeek = usageOffsetDateKey_(currentWeek, -7);
  const previousComparableEnd = usageOffsetDateKey_(today, -7);
  const daily = {};
  const users = {};
  const views = {};
  const actions = {};
  const devices = {};
  const unique = { day: {}, month: {}, year: {}, week: {}, previousWeek: {} };
  const sessions = { day: {}, month: {}, year: {}, week: {}, previousWeek: {} };
  const incidents = [];
  let eventsToday = 0;
  let errorsMonth = 0;

  (rows || []).forEach(function (row) {
    const timestamp = row[indexes.timestamp];
    if (!(timestamp instanceof Date) || isNaN(timestamp.getTime())) return;
    const dateKey = usageDateKey_(timestamp, timeZone);
    const email = normalizeEmailForComparison_(row[indexes.user_email]);
    if (!email) return;
    const sessionId = trimString_(row[indexes.session_id]);
    if (!sessionId) return;
    if (dateKey >= currentWeek && dateKey <= today) {
      unique.week[email] = true; sessions.week[sessionId] = true;
    } else if (dateKey >= previousWeek && dateKey <= previousComparableEnd) {
      unique.previousWeek[email] = true; sessions.previousWeek[sessionId] = true;
    }
    if (dateKey.slice(0, 4) !== year) return;
    const type = trimString_(row[indexes.event_type]);
    const view = trimString_(row[indexes.view]).toLowerCase();
    const action = trimString_(row[indexes.action]);
    const device = trimString_(row[indexes.device]) || 'UNKNOWN';
    const item = users[email] || (users[email] = {
      email: email, sessions: {}, activeDays: {}, views: 0, actions: 0, errors: 0,
      firstSeenAt: timestamp, lastSeenAt: timestamp
    });
    item.sessions[sessionId] = true;
    item.activeDays[dateKey] = true;
    item.firstSeenAt = timestamp < item.firstSeenAt ? timestamp : item.firstSeenAt;
    item.lastSeenAt = timestamp > item.lastSeenAt ? timestamp : item.lastSeenAt;
    unique.year[email] = true;
    sessions.year[sessionId] = true;
    if (dateKey === today) {
      unique.day[email] = true; sessions.day[sessionId] = true; eventsToday += 1;
    }
    if (dateKey.slice(0, 7) === month) {
      unique.month[email] = true; sessions.month[sessionId] = true;
    }
    const day = daily[dateKey] || (daily[dateKey] = { users: {}, sessions: {}, events: 0, errors: 0 });
    day.users[email] = true; day.sessions[sessionId] = true; day.events += 1;
    if (type === 'VIEW' && view) {
      item.views += 1;
      views[view] = (views[view] || 0) + 1;
    } else if (type === 'ACTION' || type === 'FILTER') {
      item.actions += 1;
      if (action) actions[action] = (actions[action] || 0) + 1;
    } else if (type === 'ERROR') {
      item.errors += 1; day.errors += 1;
      if (dateKey.slice(0, 7) === month) errorsMonth += 1;
      incidents.push({
        timestamp: timestamp.toISOString(), email: email, view: view,
        action: action, detail: usageDetail_(row[indexes.detail], 240)
      });
    }
    devices[device] = (devices[device] || 0) + 1;
  });

  const ranking = Object.keys(users).map(function (email) {
    const item = users[email];
    return {
      email: email, sessions: Object.keys(item.sessions).length,
      activeDays: Object.keys(item.activeDays).length, views: item.views,
      actions: item.actions, errors: item.errors,
      firstSeenAt: item.firstSeenAt.toISOString(), lastSeenAt: item.lastSeenAt.toISOString()
    };
  }).sort(function (a, b) {
    return b.activeDays - a.activeDays || b.sessions - a.sessions || b.views - a.views ||
      a.email.localeCompare(b.email);
  });
  const leastActive = ranking.slice().sort(function (a, b) {
    return a.activeDays - b.activeDays || a.sessions - b.sessions || a.views - b.views ||
      a.lastSeenAt.localeCompare(b.lastSeenAt);
  });
  const dailyTrend = [];
  for (let offset = -13; offset <= 0; offset += 1) {
    const key = usageOffsetDateKey_(today, offset);
    const item = daily[key] || { users: {}, sessions: {}, events: 0, errors: 0 };
    dailyTrend.push({
      date: key, users: Object.keys(item.users).length, sessions: Object.keys(item.sessions).length,
      events: item.events, errors: item.errors
    });
  }
  const currentWeekUsers = Object.keys(unique.week).length;
  const previousWeekUsers = Object.keys(unique.previousWeek).length;
  const currentWeekSessions = Object.keys(sessions.week).length;
  const previousWeekSessions = Object.keys(sessions.previousWeek).length;
  return {
    generatedAt: now.toISOString(),
    kpis: {
      usersToday: Object.keys(unique.day).length,
      usersMonth: Object.keys(unique.month).length,
      usersYear: Object.keys(unique.year).length,
      sessionsToday: Object.keys(sessions.day).length,
      sessionsMonth: Object.keys(sessions.month).length,
      sessionsYear: Object.keys(sessions.year).length,
      eventsToday: eventsToday,
      errorsMonth: errorsMonth
    },
    weekOverWeek: {
      users: currentWeekUsers, previousUsers: previousWeekUsers,
      usersChangePct: usageChangePct_(currentWeekUsers, previousWeekUsers),
      sessions: currentWeekSessions, previousSessions: previousWeekSessions,
      sessionsChangePct: usageChangePct_(currentWeekSessions, previousWeekSessions)
    },
    dailyTrend: dailyTrend,
    topViews: usageCountRanking_(views),
    topActions: usageCountRanking_(actions),
    devices: usageCountRanking_(devices),
    mostActive: ranking.slice(0, 10),
    leastActive: leastActive.slice(0, 10),
    recentIncidents: incidents.sort(function (a, b) {
      return b.timestamp.localeCompare(a.timestamp);
    }).slice(0, 20),
    tracking: Object.assign({}, tracking || {}, {
      coverageStart: rows && rows.length && rows[0][indexes.timestamp] instanceof Date ?
        rows[0][indexes.timestamp].toISOString() : ''
    })
  };
}

function usageCountRanking_(counts) {
  return Object.keys(counts || {}).map(function (key) {
    return { key: key, count: counts[key] };
  }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.count - a.count || a.key.localeCompare(b.key); });
}

function usageChangePct_(current, previous) {
  if (!previous) return current ? null : 0;
  return Math.round((current - previous) / previous * 1000) / 10;
}

function usageDateKey_(value, timeZone) {
  return Utilities.formatDate(value, timeZone, 'yyyy-MM-dd');
}

function usageOffsetDateKey_(dateKey, offset) {
  const date = dateFromKey_(dateKey);
  date.setDate(date.getDate() + offset);
  return dateKey_(date);
}

function usageWeekKey_(dateKey) {
  const date = dateFromKey_(dateKey);
  const day = date.getDay() || 7;
  date.setDate(date.getDate() - day + 1);
  return dateKey_(date);
}

ARCHIVO: 60_WebApp.gs

/** Web app de lectura para el dashboard de ratings. */
var MARKETS_WEB = Object.freeze({
  version: '2026.07.27.50',
  // Solo debe cambiar si cambia el formato serializado. Un cambio visual no
  // puede obligar al primer usuario a reconstruir la cache desde Sheets.
  cacheSchemaVersion: '2026.07.26.40',
  cacheSeconds: 21600,
  maxCacheCharacters: 95000,
  defaultTrendMonths: 18,
  maxTrendMonths: 36,
  latestSnapshotTailRows: 500,
  persistentChunkCharacters: 7500,
  persistentMaxCharacters: 220000,
  countryCodes: Object.freeze(['AR', 'CO', 'ES', 'MX', 'PE', 'TR', 'UY', 'VE'])
});

/** Publica el dashboard bajo el control de acceso del despliegue de Apps Script. */
function doGet() {
  return HtmlService.createTemplateFromFile('Index').evaluate()
    .setTitle('Market Pulse · BBVA Banca de Empresas e Instituciones')
    .addMetaTag('viewport', 'width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover');
}

/** Compone los modulos de presentacion sin introducir peticiones HTTP adicionales. */
function includeMarketsWebPartial_(name) {
  return HtmlService.createHtmlOutputFromFile(name).getContent();
}

/** RPC inicial consumido por Index.html. */
function getMarketsDashboardBootstrap() {
  const cacheKey = marketsBootstrapCacheKey_();
  const data = readMarketsWebCache_(cacheKey, function () {
    const persistent = readMarketsPersistentCache_('bootstrap');
    if (persistent) return persistent;
    const ss = openMarketsSpreadsheet_();
    let allTrends = readMarketsPersistentCache_('trends');
    if (!allTrends) allTrends = buildAllDashboardTrendsForWeb_(ss, MARKETS_WEB.maxTrendMonths);
    const bootstrap = buildMarketsDashboardBootstrap_(ss, allTrends);
    tryWriteMarketsPersistentCache_('bootstrap', bootstrap, bootstrap.meta.dataDate);
    tryWriteMarketsPersistentCache_('trends', allTrends, bootstrap.meta.dataDate);
    return bootstrap;
  });
  // La identidad nunca se almacena en la cache compartida.
  data.meta = data.meta || {};
  data.meta.codeVersion = MARKETS_CONFIG.codeVersion;
  data.meta.webVersion = MARKETS_WEB.version;
  data.userContext = getMarketsUserContext();
  return data;
}

/** Incluye la tendencia inicial para evitar una segunda RPC durante el arranque. */
function buildMarketsDashboardBootstrap_(ss, allTrends, catalog) {
  const latestDate = readLatestCompetitionDateForWeb_(ss);
  if (!latestDate) throw new Error('DASHBOARD_COMPETITION no contiene fechas publicables.');
  const apps = catalog || readAppCatalog_(ss);
  const storeMetadata = buildStoreMetadataByAppKeyForWeb_(apps);
  const observed = readCompetitionSnapshotForWeb_(ss, latestDate, storeMetadata);
  const snapshot = completeCompetitionSnapshotForWeb_(observed, apps, storeMetadata);
  return {
    meta: buildMarketsWebMeta_(ss, snapshot, latestDate),
    snapshot: snapshot,
    trends: filterDashboardTrendsForWeb_(allTrends || [],
      normalizeDashboardTrendRequest_(defaultMarketsTrendRequest_())),
    consolidated: buildConsolidatedSnapshotForWeb_(snapshot, apps)
  };
}

/** RPC para series mensuales filtradas. */
function getMarketsDashboardTrends(request) {
  const normalized = normalizeDashboardTrendRequest_(request);
  const cacheKey = marketsTrendCacheKey_(normalized);
  return readMarketsWebCache_(cacheKey, function () {
    let allTrends = readMarketsPersistentCache_('trends');
    if (!allTrends) {
      const ss = openMarketsSpreadsheet_();
      allTrends = buildAllDashboardTrendsForWeb_(ss, MARKETS_WEB.maxTrendMonths);
      tryWriteMarketsPersistentCache_('trends', allTrends, readLatestCompetitionDateForWeb_(ss));
    }
    return filterDashboardTrendsForWeb_(allTrends, normalized);
  });
}

function normalizeDashboardTrendRequest_(request) {
  const source = request || {};
  const countryCode = trimString_(source.countryCode).toUpperCase();
  const platform = trimString_(source.platform);
  const segment = trimString_(source.segment).toUpperCase() || 'BANK';
  const months = Math.min(MARKETS_WEB.maxTrendMonths,
    Math.max(1, toNonNegativeInteger_(source.months) || MARKETS_WEB.defaultTrendMonths));
  if (countryCode && !/^[A-Z]{2}$/.test(countryCode)) throw new Error('Pais no valido: ' + countryCode);
  if (platform && ['Android', 'iOS', 'Huawei'].indexOf(platform) < 0) {
    throw new Error('Plataforma no valida: ' + platform);
  }
  if (['BANK', 'FINTECH', 'ALL'].indexOf(segment) < 0) throw new Error('Segmento no valido: ' + segment);
  return {
    countryCode: countryCode,
    platform: platform,
    segment: segment,
    months: months,
    bbvaOnly: parseBoolean_(source.bbvaOnly, false)
  };
}

function readLatestCompetitionDateForWeb_(ss) {
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.competition);
  return !sheet || sheet.getLastRow() <= 1 ? '' : webDateKey_(sheet.getRange(sheet.getLastRow(), 1).getValue());
}

function readCompetitionSnapshotForWeb_(ss, dateKey, storeMetadataByAppKey) {
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.competition);
  if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() <= 1) return [];
  const bounds = findLatestCompetitionBoundsForWeb_(sheet, dateKey);
  if (!bounds) return [];
  const width = sheet.getLastColumn();
  const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, width).getValues()[0];
  const indexes = indexHeaders_(headers);
  const rows = sheet.getRange(bounds.firstRow, 1, bounds.lastRow - bounds.firstRow + 1, width).getValues();
  return rows.filter(function (row) {
    return trimString_(row[indexes.app_key]) && webDateKey_(row[indexes.observed_date]) === dateKey;
  }).map(function (row) {
    const appKey = trimString_(row[indexes.app_key]);
    return mapCompetitionRowForWeb_(row, indexes, (storeMetadataByAppKey || {})[appKey]);
  });
}

/**
 * Google Play publica el volumen por package, no por el parametro gl. Si un
 * package Android aparece en varios mercados, todos representan una sola ficha.
 * iOS y Huawei conservan alcance de mercado porque sus fuentes son territoriales.
 */
function buildStoreMetadataByAppKeyForWeb_(catalog) {
  const androidMarketsByKey = {};
  (catalog || []).forEach(function (app) {
    if (app.platform !== 'Android') return;
    const key = storeVolumeKeyForWeb_(app);
    const markets = androidMarketsByKey[key] || (androidMarketsByKey[key] = {});
    markets[app.countryCode] = true;
  });
  return (catalog || []).reduce(function (out, app) {
    const volumeKey = storeVolumeKeyForWeb_(app);
    const volumeMarkets = app.platform === 'Android'
      ? Object.keys(androidMarketsByKey[volumeKey] || {}).length : 1;
    out[app.appKey] = {
      storeUrl: sanitizeStoreWebUrl_(buildStoreWebUrl_(app), app.platform),
      volumeKey: volumeKey,
      volumeScope: app.platform === 'Android' && volumeMarkets > 1 ? 'SHARED_LISTING' : 'MARKET',
      volumeMarkets: Math.max(1, volumeMarkets)
    };
    return out;
  }, {});
}

function storeVolumeKeyForWeb_(app) {
  const platform = trimString_(app.platform).toUpperCase();
  const country = trimString_(app.countryCode).toUpperCase();
  if (platform === 'ANDROID') {
    return 'ANDROID|' + (trimString_(app.packageName).toLowerCase() || trimString_(app.appKey));
  }
  const storeId = trimString_(app.appId || app.packageName || app.appKey);
  return [platform, country, storeId].join('|');
}

/** Añade placeholders de catalogo para medir cobertura contra el universo esperado. */
function completeCompetitionSnapshotForWeb_(snapshot, catalog, storeMetadataByAppKey) {
  const observedByKey = (snapshot || []).reduce(function (out, item) {
    out[item.appKey] = item;
    return out;
  }, {});
  return (catalog || []).map(function (app) {
    if (observedByKey[app.appKey]) return observedByKey[app.appKey];
    const metadata = (storeMetadataByAppKey || {})[app.appKey] || {};
    return {
      platform: app.platform,
      countryCode: app.countryCode,
      country: app.country,
      segment: app.marketSegment || 'BANK',
      appKey: app.appKey,
      appName: app.appName,
      isBbva: Boolean(app.isBbva),
      displayOrder: app.displayOrder,
      rating: null,
      ratingCount: null,
      rankingComplete: false,
      delta30d: null,
      fresh: false,
      ratingCountDelta30d: null,
      storeUrl: metadata.storeUrl || '',
      volumeKey: metadata.volumeKey || app.appKey,
      volumeScope: metadata.volumeScope || 'MARKET',
      volumeMarkets: metadata.volumeMarkets || 1
    };
  });
}

/** La fecha mas reciente se resuelve con una sola lectura del final de la tabla. */
function findLatestCompetitionBoundsForWeb_(sheet, dateKey) {
  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow <= 1) return null;
  const count = Math.min(MARKETS_WEB.latestSnapshotTailRows, lastRow - 1);
  const firstTailRow = lastRow - count + 1;
  const dates = sheet.getRange(firstTailRow, 1, count, 1).getValues().map(function (row) {
    return webDateKey_(row[0]);
  });
  const first = dates.indexOf(dateKey);
  const last = dates.lastIndexOf(dateKey);
  if (first > 0 || firstTailRow === 2) {
    return first < 0 ? null : { firstRow: firstTailRow + first, lastRow: firstTailRow + last };
  }
  return binarySearchCompetitionDateBoundsForWeb_(sheet, dateKey, lastRow);
}

/** Fallback O(log n) si la ultima foto supera la ventana de lectura rapida. */
function binarySearchCompetitionDateBoundsForWeb_(sheet, dateKey, lastRow) {
  let low = 2;
  let high = lastRow + 1;
  while (low < high) {
    const middle = Math.floor((low + high) / 2);
    const middleKey = webDateKey_(sheet.getRange(middle, 1).getValue());
    if (middleKey < dateKey) low = middle + 1;
    else high = middle;
  }
  const firstRow = low;
  if (firstRow > lastRow || webDateKey_(sheet.getRange(firstRow, 1).getValue()) !== dateKey) return null;
  low = firstRow;
  high = lastRow + 1;
  while (low < high) {
    const middle = Math.floor((low + high) / 2);
    const middleKey = webDateKey_(sheet.getRange(middle, 1).getValue());
    if (middleKey <= dateKey) low = middle + 1;
    else high = middle;
  }
  return { firstRow: firstRow, lastRow: low - 1 };
}

function mapCompetitionRowForWeb_(row, indexes, catalogStoreMetadata) {
  const metadata = typeof catalogStoreMetadata === 'string'
    ? { storeUrl: catalogStoreMetadata } : (catalogStoreMetadata || {});
  const materializedStoreUrl = indexes.store_web_url === undefined ? '' : row[indexes.store_web_url];
  const platform = trimString_(row[indexes.platform]);
  const appKey = trimString_(row[indexes.app_key]);
  return {
    platform: platform,
    countryCode: trimString_(row[indexes.country_code]),
    country: trimString_(row[indexes.country]),
    segment: trimString_(row[indexes.market_segment]) || 'BANK',
    appKey: appKey,
    appName: trimString_(row[indexes.app_name]),
    isBbva: parseBoolean_(row[indexes.is_bbva], false),
    displayOrder: webNumber_(row[indexes.display_order]),
    rating: webNumber_(row[indexes.rating]),
    ratingCount: webNumber_(row[indexes.rating_count]),
    rankingComplete: parseBoolean_(row[indexes.ranking_is_complete], false),
    delta30d: webNumber_(row[indexes.delta_30d]),
    fresh: parseBoolean_(row[indexes.is_fresh_this_run], false),
    ratingCountDelta30d: webNumber_(row[indexes.rating_count_delta_30d]),
    storeUrl: sanitizeStoreWebUrl_(metadata.storeUrl || materializedStoreUrl, platform),
    volumeKey: trimString_(metadata.volumeKey) || appKey,
    volumeScope: metadata.volumeScope === 'SHARED_LISTING' ? 'SHARED_LISTING' : 'MARKET',
    volumeMarkets: Math.max(1, toNonNegativeInteger_(metadata.volumeMarkets) || 1)
  };
}

/** Limita los enlaces publicados a fichas HTTPS de las tres stores soportadas. */
function sanitizeStoreWebUrl_(value, platform) {
  const url = trimString_(value);
  const patterns = {
    Android: /^https:\/\/play\.google\.com\/store\/apps\/details\?/i,
    iOS: /^https:\/\/apps\.apple\.com\/[a-z]{2}\/app\/id\d+(?:[/?#]|$)/i,
    Huawei: /^https:\/\/appgallery\.huawei\.com\/app\/C\d+(?:[/?#]|$)/i
  };
  return patterns[platform] && patterns[platform].test(url) ? url : '';
}

function buildAllDashboardTrendsForWeb_(ss, months) {
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.trends);
  if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() <= 1) return [];
  const width = sheet.getLastColumn();
  const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, width).getValues()[0];
  assertCurrentSheetContract_(sheet, MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.trends, headers.map(trimString_));
  const indexes = indexHeaders_(headers);
  const rows = sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, width).getValues();
  const monthKeys = uniqueStrings_(rows.map(function (row) { return webMonthKey_(row[indexes.period_month]); }))
    .sort();
  const cutoff = monthKeys[Math.max(0, monthKeys.length - months)] || '';
  return rows.filter(function (row) {
    const month = webMonthKey_(row[indexes.period_month]);
    return month && month >= cutoff && trimString_(row[indexes.app_key]);
  }).map(function (row) { return mapTrendRowForWeb_(row, indexes); });
}

function filterDashboardTrendsForWeb_(allTrends, normalized) {
  const monthKeys = uniqueStrings_((allTrends || []).map(function (row) { return row.month; })).sort();
  const cutoff = monthKeys[Math.max(0, monthKeys.length - normalized.months)] || '';
  return (allTrends || []).filter(function (row) {
    return row.month >= cutoff &&
      (!normalized.countryCode || row.countryCode === normalized.countryCode) &&
      (!normalized.platform || row.platform === normalized.platform) &&
      matchesMarketsSegment_(row, normalized.segment) &&
      (!normalized.bbvaOnly || row.isBbva);
  });
}

function matchesMarketsSegment_(row, segment) {
  return segment === 'ALL' || row.segment === segment || (segment === 'FINTECH' && row.isBbva);
}

function mapTrendRowForWeb_(row, indexes) {
  return {
    month: webMonthKey_(row[indexes.period_month]),
    platform: trimString_(row[indexes.platform]),
    countryCode: trimString_(row[indexes.country_code]),
    country: trimString_(row[indexes.country]),
    appKey: trimString_(row[indexes.app_key]),
    appName: trimString_(row[indexes.app_name]),
    isBbva: parseBoolean_(row[indexes.is_bbva], false),
    ratingEom: webNumber_(row[indexes.rating_eom]),
    segment: trimString_(row[indexes.market_segment]) || 'BANK',
    ratingCountEom: webNumber_(row[indexes.rating_count_eom])
  };
}

function buildConsolidatedSnapshotForWeb_(snapshot, catalog) {
  const bbva = (catalog || []).filter(function (app) {
    return app.isBbva && (app.marketSegment || 'BANK') === 'BANK';
  });
  const itemsByAppKey = (snapshot || []).reduce(function (out, item) {
    if (item.isBbva && item.segment === 'BANK') out[item.appKey] = item;
    return out;
  }, {});
  const groupRating = weightedMetric_(bbva, itemsByAppKey, 'rating', 'groupUserWeight');
  const groupDelta = weightedMetric_(bbva, itemsByAppKey, 'delta30d', 'groupUserWeight');
  const countries = {};
  uniqueStrings_(bbva.map(function (app) { return app.countryCode; })).forEach(function (countryCode) {
    const configs = bbva.filter(function (app) { return app.countryCode === countryCode; });
    const rating = weightedMetric_(configs, itemsByAppKey, 'rating', 'countryPlatformWeight');
    const delta = weightedMetric_(configs, itemsByAppKey, 'delta30d', 'countryPlatformWeight');
    countries[countryCode] = {
      rating: webNumber_(rating.value),
      delta30d: webNumber_(delta.value),
      coverage: webNumber_(rating.coverage)
    };
  });
  const rating = webNumber_(groupRating.value);
  const delta = webNumber_(groupDelta.value);
  const coverage = webNumber_(groupRating.coverage);
  return {
    group: rating === null && delta === null && coverage === null ? null :
      { rating: rating, delta30d: delta, coverage: coverage },
    countries: countries
  };
}

function buildMarketsWebMeta_(ss, snapshot, latestDate) {
  const countriesByCode = {};
  (snapshot || []).forEach(function (row) {
    if (row.countryCode) countriesByCode[row.countryCode] = row.country || row.countryCode;
  });
  const countries = Object.keys(countriesByCode).sort().map(function (code) {
    return { code: code, name: countriesByCode[code] };
  });
  return {
    codeVersion: MARKETS_CONFIG.codeVersion,
    webVersion: MARKETS_WEB.version,
    dataDate: latestDate,
    countries: countries,
    logos: readMarketsPersistentCache_('logos') || {},
    latestRun: readLatestRunForWeb_(ss)
  };
}

/** Conserva iconos oficiales obtenidos en las mismas respuestas de las stores. */
function updateMarketsLogoCache_(results) {
  const logos = readMarketsPersistentCache_('logos') || {};
  let changed = false;
  (results || []).forEach(function (result) {
    const appKey = trimString_(result && result.app && result.app.appKey);
    const logoUrl = trimString_(result && result.logoUrl);
    if (!appKey || !/^https:\/\//i.test(logoUrl) || logos[appKey] === logoUrl) return;
    logos[appKey] = logoUrl;
    changed = true;
  });
  if (changed) writeMarketsPersistentCache_('logos', logos, new Date().toISOString());
  return logos;
}

function readLatestRunForWeb_(ss) {
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.runs);
  if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() <= 1) return null;
  const width = Math.min(sheet.getLastColumn(), MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.runs.length);
  const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, width).getValues()[0];
  const indexes = indexHeaders_(headers);
  const row = sheet.getRange(sheet.getLastRow(), 1, 1, width).getValues()[0];
  const runId = trimString_(row[indexes.run_id]);
  const fetchSummary = readRunFetchSummaryForWeb_(ss, runId,
    Math.max(1, webNumber_(row[indexes.apps_total]) || 1));
  const deliveryStatus = indexes.alert_delivery_status === undefined ? '' :
    trimString_(row[indexes.alert_delivery_status]).toUpperCase();
  let status = trimString_(row[indexes.status]).toUpperCase();
  if (status === 'PARTIAL' && fetchSummary.total > 0 && !fetchSummary.incidents.length &&
      ['ERROR', 'PARTIAL'].indexOf(deliveryStatus) < 0) status = 'OK';
  return {
    startedAt: webDateTime_(row[indexes.started_at]),
    status: status,
    appsTotal: fetchSummary.total,
    appsOk: fetchSummary.ok,
    incidents: fetchSummary.incidents
  };
}

function readRunFetchSummaryForWeb_(ss, runId, appsTotal) {
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.fetchLog);
  if (!runId || !sheet || sheet.getLastRow() <= 1) return { total: 0, ok: 0, incidents: [] };
  const count = Math.min(Math.max(20, (appsTotal || 1) * 2), sheet.getLastRow() - 1);
  const headers = MARKETS_CONFIG.headers.fetchLog;
  const indexes = indexHeaders_(headers);
  const rows = sheet.getRange(sheet.getLastRow() - count + 1, 1, count, headers.length).getValues();
  const critical = rows.filter(function (item) {
    return trimString_(item[indexes.run_id]) === runId &&
      isMarketsCriticalPlatform_(item[indexes.platform]);
  });
  const incidents = critical.filter(function (item) {
    return trimString_(item[indexes.status]) !== 'OK';
  }).map(function (item) {
    return {
      appKey: trimString_(item[indexes.app_key]),
      countryCode: trimString_(item[indexes.country_code]),
      platform: trimString_(item[indexes.platform]),
      status: trimString_(item[indexes.status]),
      detail: trimString_(item[indexes.detail])
    };
  });
  return {
    total: critical.length,
    ok: critical.filter(function (item) { return trimString_(item[indexes.status]) === 'OK'; }).length,
    incidents: incidents
  };
}

function readMarketsWebCache_(key, builder) {
  const cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  const cached = cache.get(key);
  if (cached) {
    try { return JSON.parse(cached); } catch (ignored) {}
  }
  const value = builder();
  writeMarketsWebCache_(cache, key, value);
  return value;
}

function writeMarketsWebCache_(cache, key, value) {
  const serialized = JSON.stringify(value);
  if (serialized.length <= MARKETS_WEB.maxCacheCharacters) {
    try { cache.put(key, serialized, MARKETS_WEB.cacheSeconds); } catch (ignored) {}
  }
}

function marketsBootstrapCacheKey_() {
  return 'markets-web:bootstrap:' + MARKETS_WEB.cacheSchemaVersion;
}

function marketsTrendCacheKey_(request) {
  const normalized = normalizeDashboardTrendRequest_(request);
  const keyParts = [normalized.countryCode || 'ALL', normalized.platform || 'ALL', normalized.segment,
    normalized.bbvaOnly ? 'BBVA' : 'ALL', normalized.months];
  return 'markets-web:trends:' + MARKETS_WEB.cacheSchemaVersion + ':' + keyParts.join(':');
}

function defaultMarketsTrendRequest_() {
  return { segment: 'ALL', bbvaOnly: true, months: MARKETS_WEB.defaultTrendMonths };
}

function marketsPersistentMetaKey_(name) {
  return 'MARKETS_WEB_CACHE_' + trimString_(name).toUpperCase() + '_META';
}

function marketsPersistentChunkKey_(name, generation, index) {
  return 'MARKETS_WEB_CACHE_' + trimString_(name).toUpperCase() + '_' + generation + '_' + index;
}

/** Nivel L2 sin caducidad: JSON comprimido y dividido para respetar 9 KB por property. */
function writeMarketsPersistentCache_(name, value, revision) {
  const serialized = JSON.stringify(value);
  const compressed = Utilities.gzip(Utilities.newBlob(serialized, 'application/json')).getBytes();
  const encoded = Utilities.base64Encode(compressed);
  if (encoded.length > MARKETS_WEB.persistentMaxCharacters) {
    throw new Error('Cache persistente demasiado grande para ' + name + ': ' + encoded.length + ' caracteres.');
  }
  const properties = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const metaKey = marketsPersistentMetaKey_(name);
  const oldMeta = safeJsonParse_(properties.getProperty(metaKey));
  const generation = new Date().getTime().toString(36) + '-' + Utilities.getUuid().slice(0, 8);
  const parts = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(encoded.length / MARKETS_WEB.persistentChunkCharacters));
  const chunks = {};
  for (let index = 0; index < parts; index += 1) {
    chunks[marketsPersistentChunkKey_(name, generation, index)] = encoded.slice(
      index * MARKETS_WEB.persistentChunkCharacters, (index + 1) * MARKETS_WEB.persistentChunkCharacters);
  }
  properties.setProperties(chunks, false);
  const meta = {
    version: MARKETS_WEB.cacheSchemaVersion,
    generation: generation,
    parts: parts,
    revision: trimString_(revision),
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    jsonCharacters: serialized.length,
    encodedCharacters: encoded.length
  };
  properties.setProperty(metaKey, JSON.stringify(meta));
  deleteMarketsPersistentGeneration_(properties, name, oldMeta);
  return meta;
}

function tryWriteMarketsPersistentCache_(name, value, revision) {
  try {
    return { ok: true, meta: writeMarketsPersistentCache_(name, value, revision), error: '' };
  } catch (error) {
    const detail = errorToString_(error);
    console.warn('La cache persistente ' + name + ' no se ha actualizado: ' + detail);
    return { ok: false, meta: null, error: detail };
  }
}

function readMarketsPersistentCache_(name) {
  try {
    const properties = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
    const values = typeof properties.getProperties === 'function' ? properties.getProperties() : null;
    const metaKey = marketsPersistentMetaKey_(name);
    const meta = safeJsonParse_(values ? values[metaKey] : properties.getProperty(metaKey));
    if (!meta || (name !== 'logos' && meta.version !== MARKETS_WEB.cacheSchemaVersion) ||
        !meta.generation || !meta.parts) return null;
    let encoded = '';
    for (let index = 0; index < meta.parts; index += 1) {
      const chunkKey = marketsPersistentChunkKey_(name, meta.generation, index);
      const chunk = values ? values[chunkKey] : properties.getProperty(chunkKey);
      if (!chunk) return null;
      encoded += chunk;
    }
    const blob = Utilities.newBlob(Utilities.base64Decode(encoded), 'application/gzip');
    return JSON.parse(Utilities.ungzip(blob).getDataAsString('UTF-8'));
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn('No se pudo leer la cache persistente ' + name + ': ' + errorToString_(error));
    return null;
  }
}

function deleteMarketsPersistentGeneration_(properties, name, meta) {
  if (!meta || !meta.generation || !meta.parts) return;
  const keys = [];
  for (let index = 0; index < meta.parts; index += 1) {
    keys.push(marketsPersistentChunkKey_(name, meta.generation, index));
  }
  keys.forEach(function(key) {
    properties.deleteProperty(key);
  });
}

function deleteMarketsPersistentCache_(name) {
  const properties = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const metaKey = marketsPersistentMetaKey_(name);
  const meta = safeJsonParse_(properties.getProperty(metaKey));
  deleteMarketsPersistentGeneration_(properties, name, meta);
  properties.deleteProperty(metaKey);
}

/**
 * Invalida y precalienta las dos respuestas del primer render. Se invoca al
 * terminar el RUN diario y tambien puede ejecutarse manualmente tras desplegar.
 */
function refreshMarketsDashboardCache_(ss, catalog) {
  const cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  try { cache.remove(marketsBootstrapCacheKey_()); } catch (ignored) {}
  const allTrends = buildAllDashboardTrendsForWeb_(ss, MARKETS_WEB.maxTrendMonths);
  const bootstrap = buildMarketsDashboardBootstrap_(ss, allTrends, catalog);
  const revision = bootstrap.meta.dataDate;
  const bootstrapPersistence = tryWriteMarketsPersistentCache_('bootstrap', bootstrap, revision);
  const trendPersistence = tryWriteMarketsPersistentCache_('trends', allTrends, revision);
  try { cache.removeAll(marketsInteractiveTrendCacheKeys_(bootstrap.snapshot)); } catch (ignored) {}
  writeMarketsWebCache_(cache, marketsBootstrapCacheKey_(), bootstrap);
  const trends = filterDashboardTrendsForWeb_(allTrends,
    normalizeDashboardTrendRequest_(defaultMarketsTrendRequest_()));
  writeMarketsWebCache_(cache, marketsTrendCacheKey_(defaultMarketsTrendRequest_()), trends);
  return {
    date: bootstrap.meta.dataDate,
    snapshotRows: bootstrap.snapshot.length,
    trendRows: trends.length,
    persistedTrendRows: allTrends.length,
    revision: trimString_(revision),
    cacheSeconds: MARKETS_WEB.cacheSeconds,
    persistentCacheReady: bootstrapPersistence.ok && trendPersistence.ok,
    persistentCacheDetail: [bootstrapPersistence.error, trendPersistence.error].filter(Boolean).join(' | ')
  };
}

function marketsInteractiveTrendCacheKeys_(snapshot) {
  const snapshotCountries = uniqueStrings_((snapshot || []).map(function (row) { return row.countryCode; }));
  const countries = [''].concat(snapshotCountries.length ? snapshotCountries : MARKETS_WEB.countryCodes);
  const platforms = ['', 'Android', 'iOS', 'Huawei'];
  const segments = ['BANK', 'FINTECH', 'ALL'];
  const keys = [];
  countries.forEach(function (countryCode) {
    platforms.forEach(function (platform) {
      segments.forEach(function (segment) {
        keys.push(marketsTrendCacheKey_({
          countryCode: countryCode,
          platform: platform,
          segment: segment,
          months: MARKETS_WEB.defaultTrendMonths,
          bbvaOnly: !countryCode && segment === 'BANK'
        }));
      });
    });
  });
  return uniqueStrings_(keys);
}

/** Fuerza fallback a Sheets si el RUN no pudo materializar una revision nueva. */
function invalidateMarketsDashboardCache_() {
  const keys = [marketsBootstrapCacheKey_()].concat(marketsInteractiveTrendCacheKeys_([]));
  try { CacheService.getScriptCache().removeAll(uniqueStrings_(keys)); } catch (ignored) {}
  deleteMarketsPersistentCache_('bootstrap');
  deleteMarketsPersistentCache_('trends');
}

function warmMarketsDashboardCache() {
  return refreshMarketsDashboardCache_(openMarketsSpreadsheet_());
}

function webNumber_(value) {
  if (value === '' || value === null || value === undefined) return null;
  return toFiniteNumber_(value);
}

function webDateKey_(value) {
  if (value instanceof Date && !isNaN(value.getTime())) {
    return Utilities.formatDate(value, MARKETS_CONFIG.timeZone, 'yyyy-MM-dd');
  }
  const text = trimString_(value);
  return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(text) ? text.slice(0, 10) : '';
}

function webMonthKey_(value) {
  const key = webDateKey_(value);
  return key ? key.slice(0, 7) : '';
}

function webDateTime_(value) {
  if (value instanceof Date && !isNaN(value.getTime())) {
    return Utilities.formatDate(value, MARKETS_CONFIG.timeZone, "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ssXXX");
  }
  return trimString_(value);
}
