function setupProject() {
  ScriptApp.requireAllScopes(ScriptApp.AuthMode.FULL);
  const ss = openMarketsSpreadsheet_();
  initializeWorkbook_(ss);
  seedInitialMarketsAdministrator_(ss);
  installDailyTrigger_();
  const validation = validateProject();
  console.log('Market Pulse preparado. Abra exclusivamente la Web App: ' + validation.webAppUrl);
  console.log('Tipo requerido: Aplicacion web. Nunca use una URL /macros/library/d/.');
  return validation;
}

function validateProject() {
  const ss = openMarketsSpreadsheet_();
  ensureWorkbookSchema_(ss);
  const control = readControl_(ss);
  const catalog = readAppCatalog_(ss);
  const alertUsers = readAllMarketsAlertPreferences_(ss);
  const alertSender = marketsAlertSenderStatus_(ss);
  const counts = catalog.reduce(function (out, app) {
    out[app.platform] = (out[app.platform] || 0) + 1;
    return out;
  }, {});
  const geography = catalog.reduce(function (out, app) {
    out[app.countryCode] = out[app.countryCode] || {};
    const key = app.platform + '|' + app.marketSegment;
    out[app.countryCode][key] = (out[app.countryCode][key] || 0) + 1;
    return out;
  }, {});
  return {
    spreadsheetId: ss.getId(),
    codeVersion: MARKETS_CONFIG.codeVersion,
    webAppUrl: marketsWebAppUrl_(),
    deployment: marketsWebDeploymentStatus_(),
    enabledApps: catalog.length,
    platforms: counts,
    geographies: geography,
    weightConfiguration: validateWeightConfiguration_(catalog),
    alerting: {
      emailDeliveryEnabled: parseBoolean_(control.SEND_ALERT_EMAILS, false),
      registeredUsers: alertUsers.length,
      subscribers: alertUsers.filter(function (item) { return item.active; }).length,
      pausedUsers: alertUsers.filter(function (item) { return !item.active; }).length,
      dailyDropSubscribers: alertUsers.filter(function (item) { return item.active && item.dailyDropEnabled; }).length,
      monthDropSubscribers: alertUsers.filter(function (item) { return item.active && item.mtdDropEnabled; }).length,
      yearDropSubscribers: alertUsers.filter(function (item) { return item.active && item.ytdDropEnabled; }).length,
      repeatingSubscribers: alertUsers.filter(function (item) { return item.active && item.repeatWhileBreached; }).length,
      senderAddress: alertSender.address,
      senderAvailable: alertSender.available,
      senderVerificationStatus: alertSender.verificationStatus,
      healthMonitorEnabled: parseBoolean_(control.MONITOR_HEALTH_ENABLED, true),
      healthMonitorRecipient: trimString_(control.MONITOR_ADMIN_EMAIL) || MARKETS_ALERT_EMAIL.senderAddress,
      newsletterEnabled: marketsNewsletterDeliveryEnabled_(control),
      newsletterSchedule: 'Día 1 · ' + String(MARKETS_CONFIG.newsletterHour).padStart(2, '0') + ':' +
        String(MARKETS_CONFIG.newsletterMinute).padStart(2, '0'),
      adminSheet: MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets.alertPreferences,
      warning: trimString_(control.ALERT_CONFIG_WARNING)
    },
    sheets: Object.keys(MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets).map(function (key) { return MARKETS_CONFIG.sheets[key]; })
  };
}

function marketsWebDeploymentStatus_() {
  let serviceUrl = '';
  try {
    const service = ScriptApp.getService();
    serviceUrl = service ? trimString_(service.getUrl()) : '';
  } catch (ignored) {}
  const configuredUrl = marketsWebAppUrl_();
  const runtimeType = marketsDeploymentUrlType_(serviceUrl);
  return {
    requiredType: 'Aplicacion web',
    configuredUrl: configuredUrl,
    configuredType: marketsDeploymentUrlType_(configuredUrl),
    runtimeUrl: serviceUrl,
    runtimeType: runtimeType,
    ready: runtimeType === 'WEB_APP',
    expectedPattern: 'https://script.google.com/a/macros/bbva.com/s/.../exec',
    forbiddenPattern: 'https://script.google.com/macros/library/d/...'
  };
}

function rebuildAllMetrics() {
  const ss = openMarketsSpreadsheet_();
  ensureWorkbookSchema_(ss);
  const control = readControl_(ss);
  const catalog = readAppCatalog_(ss);
  rebuildRatingMetrics_(ss, toNonNegativeInteger_(control.RATING_LOOKBACK_DAYS) || 30, catalog);
  const dashboard = rebuildDashboardDatasets_(ss, catalog, readRatingObjects_(ss));
  refreshMarketsDashboardCache_(ss, catalog);
  return dashboard;
}

function installDailyTrigger_() {
  removeManagedTriggers_();
  ScriptApp.newTrigger('runDailyRatingMonitor')
    .timeBased()
    .atHour(MARKETS_CONFIG.runHour)
    .nearMinute(MARKETS_CONFIG.runMinute)
    .everyDays(1)
    .inTimezone(MARKETS_CONFIG.timeZone)
    .create();
  ScriptApp.newTrigger('checkDailyRatingMonitorHealth')
    .timeBased()
    .atHour(MARKETS_CONFIG.healthCheckHour)
    .nearMinute(MARKETS_CONFIG.healthCheckMinute)
    .everyDays(1)
    .inTimezone(MARKETS_CONFIG.timeZone)
    .create();
  ScriptApp.newTrigger('runMarketsMonthlyNewsletter')
    .timeBased()
    .onMonthDay(1)
    .atHour(MARKETS_CONFIG.newsletterHour)
    .nearMinute(MARKETS_CONFIG.newsletterMinute)
    .inTimezone(MARKETS_CONFIG.timeZone)
    .create();
}

function removeManagedTriggers_() {
  ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(function (trigger) {
    if (['runDailyRatingMonitor', 'checkDailyRatingMonitorHealth',
        'runMarketsMonthlyNewsletter'].indexOf(trigger.getHandlerFunction()) >= 0) {
      ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(trigger);
    }
  });
}

function authorizeAndInstallMarketsAutomation() {
  ScriptApp.requireAllScopes(ScriptApp.AuthMode.FULL);
  const ss = openMarketsSpreadsheet_();
  ensureWorkbookSchema_(ss);
  const sender = requireMarketsAlertSender_(ss);
  installDailyTrigger_();
  return {
    authorized: true, sender: sender.address, verificationStatus: sender.verificationStatus,
    dailySchedule: String(MARKETS_CONFIG.runHour).padStart(2, '0') + ':' +
      String(MARKETS_CONFIG.runMinute).padStart(2, '0') + ' ' + MARKETS_CONFIG.timeZone,
    healthSchedule: String(MARKETS_CONFIG.healthCheckHour).padStart(2, '0') + ':' +
      String(MARKETS_CONFIG.healthCheckMinute).padStart(2, '0') + ' ' + MARKETS_CONFIG.timeZone,
    newsletterSchedule: 'Día 1 · ' + String(MARKETS_CONFIG.newsletterHour).padStart(2, '0') + ':' +
      String(MARKETS_CONFIG.newsletterMinute).padStart(2, '0') + ' ' + MARKETS_CONFIG.timeZone
  };
}

function onOpen() {
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().createMenu('Market Pulse')
    .addItem('Validar proyecto', 'validateProject')
    .addItem('Mostrar URL de la aplicacion', 'showMarketsWebAppLink')
    .addItem('Ejecutar ahora', 'runNow')
    .addItem('Recalcular metricas', 'rebuildAllMetrics')
    .addSeparator()
    .addItem('Autorizar e instalar automatizacion', 'authorizeAndInstallMarketsAutomation')
    .addItem('Comprobar salud ahora', 'checkDailyRatingMonitorHealth')
    .addItem('Probar aviso tecnico', 'sendMarketsMonitorHealthTestEmail')
    .addToUi();
}

function showMarketsWebAppLink() {
  const url = marketsWebAppUrl_();
  const html = HtmlService.createHtmlOutput(
    '<div style="font:14px Arial,sans-serif;padding:18px;color:#070E46;">' +
    '<h2 style="margin:0 0 12px;">Market Pulse</h2>' +
    '<p>Esta es la unica URL que debe abrirse y compartirse:</p>' +
    '<p><a href="' + url + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" ' +
    'style="color:#001391;font-weight:700;">Abrir aplicacion web</a></p>' +
    '<p style="margin-top:16px;color:#46536D;font-size:12px;">La URL debe terminar en <strong>/exec</strong>. ' +
    'No utilice enlaces que contengan <strong>/macros/library/</strong>.</p></div>'
  ).setWidth(480).setHeight(220);
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().showModalDialog(html, 'URL oficial de Market Pulse');
}


========================================
ARCHIVO: 99_Core.gs
========================================

function trimString_(value) {
  return value === null || value === undefined ? '' : String(value).trim();
}

/** URL corporativa unica para web, correos y diagnostico. */
function marketsWebAppUrl_() {
  const url = trimString_(MARKETS_CONFIG.webAppUrl);
  const type = marketsDeploymentUrlType_(url);
  if (type !== 'WEB_APP') {
    const detail = type === 'LIBRARY' ?
      'La URL pertenece a una biblioteca de Apps Script, no a una aplicacion web.' :
      'La URL debe ser la direccion corporativa /s/.../exec completa.';
    throw new Error('MARKETS_CONFIG.webAppUrl no es una aplicacion web valida. ' + detail);
  }
  return url;
}

function marketsDeploymentUrlType_(value) {
  const url = trimString_(value);
  if (/^https:\/\/script\.google\.com\/macros\/library\/d\/[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\/\d+(?:[/?#]|$)/.test(url)) {
    return 'LIBRARY';
  }
  if (/^https:\/\/script\.google\.com\/a\/macros\/bbva\.com\/s\/[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\/exec(?:[?#].*)?$/.test(url) ||
      /^https:\/\/script\.google\.com\/macros\/s\/[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\/exec(?:[?#].*)?$/.test(url)) {
    return 'WEB_APP';
  }
  if (/\/dev(?:[?#].*)?$/.test(url)) return 'DEVELOPMENT';
  return url ? 'UNKNOWN' : 'MISSING';
}

/** Fuerza la seleccion de la cuenta Workspace antes de entrar desde un correo. */
function marketsWebAppLoginUrl_(email) {
  const account = trimString_(email).toLowerCase();
  const query = [];
  if (/^[^\s@]+@bbva\.com$/.test(account)) query.push('Email=' + encodeURIComponent(account));
  query.push('continue=' + encodeURIComponent(marketsWebAppUrl_()));
  return 'https://accounts.google.com/AccountChooser?' + query.join('&');
}

function normalizeHeader_(value) {
  return trimString_(value).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '_').replace(/^_|_$/g, '');
}

function indexHeaders_(headers) {
  return (headers || []).reduce(function (out, header, index) {
    out[normalizeHeader_(header)] = index;
    return out;
  }, {});
}

function parseBoolean_(value, defaultValue) {
  if (typeof value === 'boolean') return value;
  const normalized = trimString_(value).toLowerCase();
  if (['true', '1', 'si', 'sí', 'yes'].indexOf(normalized) >= 0) return true;
  if (['false', '0', 'no'].indexOf(normalized) >= 0) return false;
  return Boolean(defaultValue);
}

function toFiniteNumber_(value) {
  if (typeof value === 'number') return isFinite(value) ? value : null;
  const normalized = trimString_(value).replace(/\s/g, '').replace(',', '.');
  if (!normalized) return null;
  const parsed = Number(normalized);
  return isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : null;
}

function toNonNegativeInteger_(value) {
  const parsed = toFiniteNumber_(value);
  return parsed !== null && parsed >= 0 ? Math.round(parsed) : null;
}

function nonNegativeNumberOrZero_(value) {
  const parsed = toFiniteNumber_(value);
  return parsed !== null && parsed >= 0 ? parsed : 0;
}

function normalizeRating_(value) {
  const parsed = toFiniteNumber_(value);
  // Conserva la precision publicada por la fuente. La interfaz decide cuantos
  // decimales mostrar; redondear aqui destruye informacion necesaria para
  // inferir la nota de las opiniones nuevas a partir de acumulados grandes.
  return parsed !== null && parsed >= 1 && parsed <= 5 ? Math.round(parsed * 1000000) / 1000000 : null;
}

function dateKey_(value) {
  if (!(value instanceof Date) || isNaN(value.getTime())) return '';
  const year = value.getFullYear();
  const month = String(value.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const day = String(value.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  return year + '-' + month + '-' + day;
}

function dateFromKey_(key) {
  const parts = trimString_(key).split('-').map(Number);
  if (parts.length !== 3 || parts.some(function (part) { return !isFinite(part); })) return null;
  return new Date(parts[0], parts[1] - 1, parts[2], 12, 0, 0, 0);
}

function round2_(value) {
  return Math.round(Number(value) * 100) / 100;
}

function makeRatingKey_(date, appKey) {
  return dateKey_(date) + '|' + trimString_(appKey);
}

/** Ranking de competicion: 5, 5, 4 produce 1, 1, 3. */
function rankSnapshot_(items) {
  const ratings = (items || []).map(function (item) { return normalizeRating_(item.rating); });
  return ratings.map(function (rating) {
    if (rating === null) return null;
    return 1 + ratings.filter(function (other) { return other !== null && other > rating; }).length;
  });
}

/** Devuelve el valor de la ultima observacion anterior al corte. */
function findPreviousValue_(series, targetMillis, field) {
  let low = 0;
  let high = (series || []).length - 1;
  let found = null;
  while (low <= high) {
    const middle = Math.floor((low + high) / 2);
    if (series[middle].dateMillis <= targetMillis) {
      found = series[middle][field];
      low = middle + 1;
    } else {
      high = middle - 1;
    }
  }
  return found;
}

function findPreviousRating_(series, targetMillis) {
  return findPreviousValue_(series, targetMillis, 'rating');
}

function retryableStatus_(status) {
  return status === 408 || status === 429 || status >= 500;
}

function errorToString_(error) {
  if (!error) return 'Error desconocido';
  return trimString_(error.message || error.stack || error);
}

function safeJsonParse_(text) {
  try { return JSON.parse(text); } catch (ignored) { return null; }
}

function uniqueStrings_(values) {
  const seen = {};
  return (values || []).map(trimString_).filter(function (value) {
    if (!value || seen[value]) return false;
    seen[value] = true;
    return true;
  });
}

/**
 * Android e iOS sostienen la cobertura operativa. Huawei se conserva como
 * fuente complementaria: se captura y publica cuando existe, pero su ausencia
 * nunca degrada una ejecución, un agregado ni una alerta técnica.
 */
function isMarketsCriticalPlatform_(platform) {
  return ['Android', 'iOS'].indexOf(trimString_(platform)) >= 0;
}

function marketsPlatformRank_(value) {
  const platform = trimString_(value && typeof value === 'object' ? value.platform : value);
  const index = ['Android', 'iOS', 'Huawei'].indexOf(platform);
  return index < 0 ? 3 : index;
}

function compareMarketsPlatforms_(a, b) {
  const first = trimString_(a && typeof a === 'object' ? a.platform : a);
  const second = trimString_(b && typeof b === 'object' ? b.platform : b);
  return marketsPlatformRank_(first) - marketsPlatformRank_(second) ||
    first.localeCompare(second);
}

function marketsCriticalItems_(items) {
  return (items || []).filter(function (item) {
    const source = item && item.app ? item.app : item;
    const explicit = trimString_(source && source.platform);
    const prefix = trimString_(source && source.appKey).split('|')[0].toUpperCase();
    const platform = explicit || (prefix === 'ANDROID' ? 'Android' : prefix === 'IOS' ? 'iOS' : prefix === 'HUAWEI' ? 'Huawei' : '');
    return isMarketsCriticalPlatform_(platform);
  });
}
