    "use strict";
    function lineChart(series,options){
      const ui=window.MarketsUI,theme=window.MarketsTheme.chart,finite=window.MarketsDomain.finite;
      const visible=(series||[]).slice(0,12).map(item=>({...item,points:(item.points||[]).filter(point=>!options.period||point.month<=options.period)})).filter(item=>item.points.length);
      if(!visible.length)return{svg:ui.empty("Sin datos para esta selección","No existen observaciones con los filtros y el periodo elegidos."),legend:"",series:[]};
      const months=[...new Set(visible.flatMap(item=>item.points.map(point=>point.month)))].sort();
      const monthIndex=new Map(months.map((month,index)=>[month,index]));
      const values=visible.flatMap(item=>item.points.map(point=>point.value)).filter(finite);
      let min=Math.max(1,Math.floor((Math.min(...values)-.12)*10)/10),max=Math.min(5,Math.ceil((Math.max(...values)+.12)*10)/10);if(max-min<.4){min=Math.max(1,min-.2);max=Math.min(5,max+.2)}
      const width=theme.width,height=theme.height,left=theme.left,right=theme.right,top=theme.top,bottom=theme.bottom,plotW=width-left-right,plotH=height-top-bottom;
      const x=month=>left+(months.length===1?plotW/2:monthIndex.get(month)/(months.length-1)*plotW);const y=value=>top+(max-value)/(max-min)*plotH;
      const ticks=Array.from({length:5},(_,index)=>min+(max-min)*index/4);const step=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(months.length/8));
      const grid=ticks.map(value=>`<g><line x1="${left}" y1="${y(value)}" x2="${width-right}" y2="${y(value)}" stroke="${theme.grid}"/><text x="${left-12}" y="${y(value)+4}" text-anchor="end" fill="${theme.axis}" font-size="${theme.fontSize}">${options.format(value,1)}</text></g>`).join("");
      const labels=months.map((month,index)=>index%step===0||index===months.length-1?`<text x="${x(month)}" y="${height-16}" text-anchor="middle" fill="${theme.axis}" font-size="${theme.fontSize}">${ui.esc(options.monthLabel(month))}</text>`:"").join("");
      const lines=visible.map((item,index)=>{const color=theme.palette[index%theme.palette.length];const points=item.points.filter(point=>finite(point.value)).sort((a,b)=>a.month.localeCompare(b.month));const path=points.map((point,position)=>`${position?"L":"M"}${x(point.month).toFixed(1)},${y(point.value).toFixed(1)}`).join(" ");const circles=points.map(point=>`<circle class="chart-point" tabindex="0" cx="${x(point.month)}" cy="${y(point.value)}" r="${theme.pointRadius}" fill="${color}" stroke="${theme.surface}" stroke-width="${theme.pointStroke}" data-label="${ui.esc(item.label)}" data-month="${ui.esc(options.monthLabel(point.month))}" data-value="${options.format(point.value,2)}"></circle>`).join("");return`<path d="${path}" fill="none" stroke="${color}" stroke-width="${item.isBbva?theme.focusLineWidth:theme.lineWidth}" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-linecap="round" opacity="${item.isBbva?1:.82}"/>${circles}`}).join("");
      const scope=options.allMarkets?"BBVA Empresas en todos los mercados":options.context,idPrefix=options.idPrefix||"markets";
      const svg=`<svg viewBox="0 0 ${width} ${height}" role="img" aria-labelledby="${idPrefix}-chart-title ${idPrefix}-chart-desc"><title id="${idPrefix}-chart-title">Evolución mensual de ratings</title><desc id="${idPrefix}-chart-desc">${ui.esc(scope)}. Periodo hasta ${ui.esc(options.monthLabel(months.at(-1)))}</desc>${grid}<line x1="${left}" y1="${top}" x2="${left}" y2="${height-bottom}" stroke="${theme.axis}"/>${labels}${lines}</svg>`;
      return{svg,legend:ui.legend(visible,theme.palette),series:visible};
    }
    function ratingBars(groups,options){
      const ui=window.MarketsUI,finite=window.MarketsDomain.finite;
      const boards=(groups||[]).map(group=>`<section class="rating-board"><header><strong>${ui.esc(group.platform)}</strong><span>${group.rows.length} ${group.rows.length===1?"referencia":"referencias"}</span></header><ol>${group.rows.map(row=>{const value=finite(row.rating)?row.rating:0;return`<li><span class="rating-bar-label" title="${ui.esc(options.label(row))}">${ui.esc(options.label(row))}</span><span class="rating-bar-metric"><span class="rating-bar-track"><i style="width:${Math.max(0,Math.min(100,value/5*100))}%"></i></span><b aria-label="Rating ${ui.esc(options.format(value,1))} sobre 5">${ui.esc(options.format(value,1))}</b></span></li>`}).join("")}</ol></section>`).join("");
      return boards?`<div class="rating-comparison-grid">${boards}</div>`:ui.empty("Sin ratings comparables","Prueba otro mercado o plataforma.");
    }
    function monthlyMatrix(model,options){
      const ui=window.MarketsUI,finite=window.MarketsDomain.finite;
      if(!model?.periods?.length||!model?.rows?.length)return ui.empty("Sin cierres mensuales","Aún no hay histórico suficiente para esta selección.");
      const header=model.periods.map((month,index)=>`<th scope="col" class="${index===model.periods.length-1?"latest":""}"><span>${ui.esc(options.monthLabel(month))}</span>${index===model.periods.length-1?'<small><i></i>Actual</small>':""}</th>`).join("");
      const body=model.rows.map(row=>`<tr><th scope="row"><span class="monthly-country-flag">${options.flag(row.countryCode)}</span><strong>${ui.esc(row.country)}</strong></th>${row.cells.map((cell,index)=>{const hasDelta=finite(cell.delta)&&Math.abs(cell.delta)>=.005,tone=cell.delta>0?"delta-up":"delta-down";const reviews=cell.platforms.map(item=>{const detail=item.unavailable?"N/D: Google Play no permite desagregar esta ficha por geografía.":`${options.integer(item.value)} opiniones`;return`<span class="monthly-review${item.unavailable?" unavailable":""}" title="${ui.esc(detail)}"><span>${ui.esc(item.platform)}</span><b>${item.unavailable?"N/D":options.compactInteger(item.value)}</b></span>`}).join("");return`<td class="${index===row.cells.length-1?"latest":""}"><div class="monthly-rating"><strong>${options.format(cell.rating,2)}</strong>${hasDelta?`<em class="${tone}">${ui.esc(options.signed(cell.delta))}</em>`:""}</div><div class="monthly-reviews">${reviews}</div></td>`}).join("")}</tr>`).join("");
      return`<div class="monthly-matrix-scroll"><table class="monthly-matrix"><caption>Evolución mensual del rating y volumen de opiniones de BBVA Empresas</caption><thead><tr><th scope="col"><span>Geografía</span><small>Rating y opiniones</small></th>${header}</tr></thead><tbody>${body}</tbody></table></div>`;
    }
    function newReviewImpact(model,options){
      const ui=window.MarketsUI,finite=window.MarketsDomain.finite,summary=model?.summary||{};
      if(!model?.previousPeriod||!model?.rows?.length)return ui.empty("Aún no hay base mensual","Se necesitan dos cierres consecutivos con rating y volumen acumulado.");
      const status=impact=>!finite(impact)?{label:"Sin estimación",tone:"neutral"}:impact>.005?{label:"Impulsa el rating",tone:"positive"}:impact<-.005?{label:"Presiona el rating",tone:"negative"}:{label:"Mantiene el nivel",tone:"neutral"};
      const reason=row=>row.reasons.includes("SHARED_LISTING")?"Ficha Android compartida: no admite atribución a este mercado":row.reasons.includes("SHARED_CONFLICT")?"La ficha compartida no presenta cierres consistentes":row.reasons.includes("ROUNDING")?"El redondeo del rating acumulado impide una estimación fiable":row.reasons.includes("NO_NEW_REVIEWS")?"Sin nuevas opiniones en el mes":"Falta uno de los dos cierres comparables";
      const platformModels=summary.platforms||[],validPlatforms=platformModels.filter(item=>finite(item.rating)),unavailablePlatforms=platformModels.filter(item=>!finite(item.rating));
      const platformCards=validPlatforms.map(item=>{const signal=status(item.impact);return`<article class="new-review-platform-card ${signal.tone}"><header><strong>${ui.esc(item.platform)}</strong><span>${item.availableSeries}/${item.totalSeries} fichas</span></header><div><strong>${options.format(item.rating,2)}<small> / 5</small></strong><b class="${signal.tone}">${ui.esc(signal.label)} · ${ui.esc(options.signed(item.impact))}</b></div><p>${options.integer(item.newReviews)} opiniones utilizables · cálculo exclusivo de ${ui.esc(item.platform)}</p></article>`}).join("");
      const unavailableNote=unavailablePlatforms.length?`<div class="new-review-unavailable"><strong>Sin estimación agregada:</strong><span>${unavailablePlatforms.map(item=>`${ui.esc(item.platform)} (${item.availableSeries}/${item.totalSeries} fichas calculables)`).join(" · ")}</span></div>`:"";
      const rows=model.rows.map(row=>{const signal=status(row.impact),scope=row.scopeLabel||row.country;return`<tr><th scope="row"><strong>${ui.esc(row.country)}</strong><small>${ui.esc(scope===row.country?row.countryCode:scope)}</small></th><td><strong>${ui.esc(row.platform)}</strong><small>${row.volumeScope==="SHARED_LISTING"?"Una única ficha multipaís":"Ficha independiente"}</small></td><td><strong>${finite(row.rating)?options.format(row.rating,2):"N/D"}</strong>${finite(row.rating)?" / 5":""}<small>${finite(row.rating)?`Estimación ${row.confidence==="HIGH"?"estable":row.confidence==="MEDIUM"?"orientativa":"sensible al redondeo"}`:ui.esc(reason(row))}</small></td><td><strong>${finite(row.newReviews)?options.integer(row.newReviews):"N/D"}</strong><small>opiniones nuevas de esta ficha</small></td><td><strong class="${signal.tone}">${ui.esc(options.signed(row.impact))}</strong><small>vs. rating previo de la misma ficha</small></td><td><span class="new-review-status ${signal.tone}">${ui.esc(signal.label)}</span></td></tr>`}).join("");
      const coverage=summary.totalSeries?summary.availableSeries/summary.totalSeries:null;
      return`<div class="new-review-overview"><div class="new-review-platform-grid">${platformCards||'<article class="new-review-platform-card neutral"><header><strong>Sin estimaciones publicables</strong></header><p>El detalle inferior explica qué impide calcular cada ficha.</p></article>'}</div><div class="new-review-mini"><article><span>Cobertura</span><strong>${options.integer(summary.availableSeries)}/${options.integer(summary.totalSeries)}</strong><small>${finite(coverage)?options.format(coverage*100,0)+"% de fichas únicas":"Sin base"}</small></article><article><span>Balance</span><strong>${options.integer(summary.improvingSeries)} / ${options.integer(summary.pressuringSeries)}</strong><small>impulsan / presionan</small></article><article><span>Estables</span><strong>${options.integer(summary.stableSeries)}</strong><small>sin diferencia relevante</small></article></div></div>${unavailableNote}<div class="new-review-table-scroll"><table class="new-review-table"><caption>Rating estimado de las opiniones nuevas por ficha y plataforma</caption><thead><tr><th>Mercado / ficha</th><th>Plataforma</th><th>Nota del mes</th><th>Volumen</th><th>Efecto</th><th>Lectura</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table></div>`;
    }
    window.MarketsCharts=Object.freeze({lineChart,ratingBars,monthlyMatrix,newReviewImpact});
  })();
</script>

<script>
  (()=>{
    "use strict";
    const esc=value=>String(value??"").replace(/[&<>"']/g,char=>({"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;","'":"&#39;"}[char]));
    const paths=Object.freeze({close:'M13.131 12L21.365 20.234L20.234 21.365L12 13.131L3.76603 21.365L2.63503 20.234L10.869 12L2.63403 3.76603L3.76503 2.63503L11.999 10.869L20.234 2.63403L21.365 3.76503L13.131 11.999V12Z',check:'M9.19994 18.1309L4.03394 12.9659L5.16594 11.8339L9.19994 15.8689L18.8339 6.23389L19.9659 7.36589L9.19994 18.1309Z',right:'M16.0239 12.781C16.5243 12.3807 16.5243 11.6196 16.0239 11.2192L8.6247 5.29987C7.96993 4.77606 7 5.24223 7 6.08073V17.9195C7 18.758 7.96993 19.2242 8.6247 18.7004L16.0239 12.781Z',arrows:'M8.79999 9.13212V16.0001H7.19999V6.13112L4.36599 8.96512L3.23499 7.83412L8.00099 3.06812L12.767 7.83412L11.636 8.96512L8.80199 6.13112V9.13212H8.79999ZM19.634 15.0341L16.8 17.8681V7.99911H15.2V17.8681L12.366 15.0341L11.235 16.1651L16.001 20.9311L20.767 16.1651L19.636 15.0341H19.634Z'});
    const icon=name=>`<span class="icon" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="${paths[name]||paths.check}"></path></svg></span>`;
    const metric=card=>`<article class="metric-card ${card.accent?"accent":""}"><div class="metric-label">${esc(card.label)}</div><div class="metric-value ${esc(card.valueClass||"")}">${esc(card.value)} <small>${esc(card.suffix||"")}</small></div><div class="metric-foot">${esc(card.foot||"")}</div></article>`;
    const metrics=cards=>(cards||[]).map(metric).join("");
    const empty=(title,copy)=>`<div class="empty-state"><div><strong>${esc(title)}</strong>${esc(copy)}</div></div>`;
    const loading=(title="Cargando datos de evolución",copy="La vista ejecutiva ya está disponible.")=>`<div class="loading-state"><div><strong>${esc(title)}</strong><span>${esc(copy)}</span><div class="loading-line"></div></div></div>`;
    const legend=(items,palette)=>(items||[]).map((item,index)=>`<span class="legend-item"><i class="legend-swatch" style="background:${palette[index%palette.length]}"></i>${esc(item.label)}</span>`).join("");
    const option=(value,label,selected=false)=>`<option value="${esc(value)}"${selected?' selected':''}>${esc(label)}</option>`;
    window.MarketsUI=Object.freeze({esc,icon,metric,metrics,empty,loading,legend,option});
  })();
</script>

  .filter-wrap{position:relative;z-index:4;max-width:calc(var(--grid-max) + var(--grid-margin) * 2);margin:calc(var(--space-4) * -1) auto var(--space-0);padding:var(--space-0) var(--grid-margin)}.filter-bar{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(192px,.8fr) minmax(320px,1.4fr);gap:var(--grid-gutter);padding:var(--space-2);border-radius:var(--radius-container);background:var(--color-surface);box-shadow:var(--elevation-2)}.filter-bar.benchmark-mode{grid-template-columns:minmax(192px,.8fr) minmax(320px,1.4fr) minmax(224px,.8fr)}.control{display:grid;gap:var(--space-1)}.select-control{width:100%;height:var(--control-height);padding:var(--space-0) var(--space-5) var(--space-0) var(--space-2);color:var(--color-primary-darkest);border:1px solid var(--color-border);border-radius:var(--radius-component);background:var(--color-surface);cursor:pointer}.platform-switch{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);min-height:var(--control-height);padding:var(--space-05);border:1px solid var(--color-border);border-radius:var(--radius-component);background:var(--color-surface-muted)}.platform-button{border:0;border-radius:var(--radius-component);background:transparent;cursor:pointer;color:var(--color-text-secondary);font-size:var(--font-size-xs);font-weight:var(--weight-bold)}.platform-button.active{color:var(--color-surface);background:var(--color-primary-medium)}

<script>
  (()=>{
    "use strict";
    const config=Object.freeze({
      flags:Object.freeze({AR:"🇦🇷",CO:"🇨🇴",ES:"🇪🇸",MX:"🇲🇽",PE:"🇵🇪",TR:"🇹🇷",UY:"🇺🇾",VE:"🇻🇪"}),
      countries:Object.freeze({AR:"Argentina",CO:"Colombia",ES:"España",MX:"México",PE:"Perú",TR:"Turquía",UY:"Uruguay",VE:"Venezuela"}),
      platforms:Object.freeze(["Android","iOS","Huawei"]),
      criticalPlatforms:Object.freeze(["Android","iOS"]),
      platformLabels:Object.freeze({ALL:"Todas",Android:"Android",iOS:"iOS",Huawei:"Huawei"}),
      storeLabels:Object.freeze({Android:"Google Play",iOS:"App Store",Huawei:"AppGallery"}),
      segmentLabels:Object.freeze({BANK:"Banca empresas",FINTECH:"Fintech y Neobancos",ALL:"Todo el mercado"})
    });
    const finite=value=>typeof value==="number"&&Number.isFinite(value);
    const sum=values=>values.filter(finite).reduce((total,value)=>total+value,0);
    const sumOrNull=values=>{const clean=values.filter(finite);return clean.length?sum(clean):null};
    const mean=values=>{const clean=values.filter(finite);return clean.length?sum(clean)/clean.length:null};
    const groupBy=(rows,selector)=>rows.reduce((out,row)=>{const key=selector(row);(out[key]||(out[key]=[])).push(row);return out},{});
    const appName=row=>row.isBbva?"BBVA Empresas":row.appName;
    const isCriticalPlatform=platform=>config.criticalPlatforms.includes(platform);
    const selectionRows=(snapshot,selection,segment="BANK",includeBbva=false)=>(snapshot||[]).filter(row=>(selection.country==="ALL"||row.countryCode===selection.country)&&(selection.platform==="ALL"||row.platform===selection.platform)&&(segment==="ALL"||row.segment===segment||(includeBbva&&segment==="FINTECH"&&row.isBbva)));
    const coverageRows=(rows,selection)=>(selection?.platform||"ALL")==="ALL"?(rows||[]).filter(row=>isCriticalPlatform(row.platform)):(rows||[]);
    const trendRows=(trends,selection)=>(trends||[]).filter(row=>(selection.country==="ALL"||row.countryCode===selection.country)&&(selection.platform==="ALL"||row.platform===selection.platform)&&(selection.country!=="ALL"||row.isBbva)&&(!selection.bbvaOnly||row.isBbva));
    function uniqueStoreRows(rows){
      const byKey=new Map();
      (rows||[]).forEach(row=>{const key=row.volumeKey||row.appKey;const current=byKey.get(key);if(!current||quality(row)>quality(current)||(quality(row)===quality(current)&&finite(row.ratingCount)&&(!finite(current.ratingCount)||row.ratingCount>current.ratingCount)))byKey.set(key,row)});
      return[...byKey.values()];
    }
    const quality=row=>(finite(row.rating)?8:0)+(finite(row.ratingCount)?4:0)+(finite(row.ratingCountDelta30d)?2:0)+(row.fresh?1:0);
    function volumeSummary(rows){
      const bbvaOnly=uniqueStoreRows((rows||[]).filter(row=>row.isBbva));
      const counted=bbvaOnly.filter(row=>finite(row.ratingCount));
      const comparable=bbvaOnly.filter(row=>finite(row.ratingCountDelta30d));
      return{accumulated:sumOrNull(counted.map(row=>row.ratingCount)),period:sumOrNull(comparable.map(row=>Math.max(0,row.ratingCountDelta30d))),counted:counted.length,comparable:comparable.length,shared:bbvaOnly.filter(row=>row.volumeScope==="SHARED_LISTING").length};
    }
    function rankRows(rows){
      const sorted=[...(rows||[])].sort((a,b)=>b.rating-a.rating||(a.displayOrder||0)-(b.displayOrder||0)||appName(a).localeCompare(appName(b)));
      return sorted.map(row=>({...row,computedRank:1+sorted.filter(other=>other.rating>row.rating).length}));
    }
    function countBbvaLeaders(rows){
      const groups=groupBy(rows||[],row=>`${row.countryCode}|${row.platform}`);
      let value=0,comparable=0,sole=0,shared=0;
      Object.values(groups).forEach(group=>{
        const ranked=rankRows(group.filter(row=>finite(row.rating)));
        if(ranked.length<2||ranked.some(row=>!row.rankingComplete))return;
        const bbva=ranked.find(row=>row.isBbva);
        if(!bbva)return;
        comparable++;
        if(bbva.computedRank!==1)return;
        value++;
        if(ranked.filter(row=>row.rating===bbva.rating).length===1)sole++;
        else shared++;
      });
      return{value,comparable,sole,shared};
    }
    function availablePeriods(rows){return[...new Set((rows||[]).filter(row=>finite(row.ratingEom)).map(row=>row.month))].sort()}
    function periodModel(rows,preferredYear,preferredMonth){
      const periods=availablePeriods(rows);const latest=periods.at(-1)||"";
      const years=[...new Set(periods.map(period=>period.slice(0,4)))].sort().reverse();
      const year=years.includes(String(preferredYear))?String(preferredYear):(latest.slice(0,4)||"");
      const months=periods.filter(period=>period.startsWith(`${year}-`)).map(period=>period.slice(5)).sort().reverse();
      const month=months.includes(String(preferredMonth).padStart(2,"0"))?String(preferredMonth).padStart(2,"0"):(months[0]||"");
      return{period:year&&month?`${year}-${month}`:"",year,month,years,months,periods};
    }
    function ratingAt(rows,period){const values=(rows||[]).filter(row=>row.month===period&&row.isBbva&&finite(row.ratingEom)).map(row=>row.ratingEom);return mean(values)}
    function previousAvailable(periods,target){return[...(periods||[])].filter(period=>period<target).sort().at(-1)||""}
    function previousYearClose(periods,period){const priorYear=String(Number(period.slice(0,4))-1);return[...(periods||[])].filter(item=>item<=`${priorYear}-12`).sort().at(-1)||""}
    function evolutionKpis(rows,snapshot,selection,period){
      const bbva=(rows||[]).filter(row=>row.isBbva&&row.month<=period);const periods=availablePeriods(bbva);const currentPeriod=periods.includes(period)?period:previousAvailable(periods,`${period}-z`);
      const monthPeriod=previousAvailable(periods,currentPeriod);const yearPeriod=previousYearClose(periods,currentPeriod);
      const current=ratingAt(bbva,currentPeriod),monthClose=ratingAt(bbva,monthPeriod),yearClose=ratingAt(bbva,yearPeriod);
      const expectedRows=coverageRows(selectionRows(snapshot,selection,"BANK").filter(row=>row.isBbva),selection);
      const observedRows=coverageRows(bbva,selection);
      const expected=new Set(expectedRows.map(row=>row.appKey)).size;
      const observed=new Set(observedRows.filter(row=>row.month===currentPeriod&&finite(row.ratingEom)).map(row=>row.appKey)).size;
      return{currentPeriod,yearPeriod,monthPeriod,current,yearClose,monthClose,annual:finite(current)&&finite(yearClose)?current-yearClose:null,monthly:finite(current)&&finite(monthClose)?current-monthClose:null,coverage:expected?observed/expected:null,observed,expected};
    }
    function prepareSeries(rows,selection){
      const buckets=new Map();
      (rows||[]).forEach(row=>{let key,label;if(selection.country==="ALL"){key=row.countryCode;label=`BBVA Empresas ${config.countries[row.countryCode]||row.country||row.countryCode}`}else if(selection.platform==="ALL"){key=appName(row);label=appName(row)}else{key=row.appKey;label=appName(row)}const bucketKey=`${key}|${row.month}`;const bucket=buckets.get(bucketKey)||{key,label,month:row.month,values:[],isBbva:row.isBbva};bucket.values.push(row.ratingEom);bucket.isBbva=bucket.isBbva||row.isBbva;buckets.set(bucketKey,bucket)});
      const grouped=new Map();buckets.forEach(bucket=>{const item=grouped.get(bucket.key)||{key:bucket.key,label:bucket.label,isBbva:bucket.isBbva,points:[]};item.points.push({month:bucket.month,value:mean(bucket.values)});item.isBbva=item.isBbva||bucket.isBbva;grouped.set(bucket.key,item)});
      return[...grouped.values()].sort((a,b)=>Number(b.isBbva)-Number(a.isBbva)||latestValue(b)-latestValue(a)||a.label.localeCompare(b.label));
    }
    const latestValue=series=>[...(series.points||[])].sort((a,b)=>a.month.localeCompare(b.month)).at(-1)?.value||0;
    function ratingComparison(snapshot,selection){
      const platforms=selection.platform==="ALL"?config.platforms:[selection.platform];
      return platforms.map(platform=>{
        const rows=selectionRows(snapshot,{country:selection.country,platform},"BANK")
          .filter(row=>finite(row.rating)&&(selection.country!=="ALL"||row.isBbva));
        return{platform,rows:rankRows(rows)};
      }).filter(group=>group.rows.length);
    }
    function monthlyRatingMatrix(trends,snapshot,selection,endPeriod,limit=8){
      const rows=(trends||[]).filter(row=>row.isBbva&&finite(row.ratingEom)&&(!endPeriod||row.month<=endPeriod)&&(selection.country==="ALL"||row.countryCode===selection.country)&&(selection.platform==="ALL"||row.platform===selection.platform));
      const periods=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>row.month))].sort().slice(-Math.max(1,limit));
      const platforms=selection.platform==="ALL"?config.platforms.filter(platform=>isCriticalPlatform(platform)||rows.some(row=>row.platform===platform&&finite(row.ratingEom))):[selection.platform];
      const metadata=new Map((snapshot||[]).filter(row=>row.isBbva).map(row=>[row.appKey,row]));
      const countryCodes=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>row.countryCode))].sort((a,b)=>(config.countries[a]||a).localeCompare(config.countries[b]||b,"es"));
      const matrixRows=countryCodes.map(countryCode=>{
        const cells=periods.map(month=>{
          const platformVolumes=platforms.map(platform=>{
            const matches=rows.filter(row=>row.countryCode===countryCode&&row.month===month&&row.platform===platform);
            const unavailable=platform==="Android"&&matches.some(row=>metadata.get(row.appKey)?.volumeScope==="SHARED_LISTING");
            return{platform,rating:mean(matches.map(row=>row.ratingEom)),value:unavailable?null:sumOrNull(matches.map(row=>row.ratingCountEom)),unavailable};
          });
          return{month,rating:mean(platformVolumes.map(item=>item.rating)),platforms:platformVolumes};
        });
        cells.forEach((cell,index)=>{const previous=index?cells[index-1].rating:null;cell.delta=finite(cell.rating)&&finite(previous)?cell.rating-previous:null});
        const countryRow=rows.find(row=>row.countryCode===countryCode);
        return{countryCode,country:config.countries[countryCode]||countryRow?.country||countryCode,cells};
      });
      return{periods,platforms,rows:matrixRows,hasSharedAndroid:matrixRows.some(row=>row.cells.some(cell=>cell.platforms.some(item=>item.unavailable)))};
    }
    function previousCalendarMonth(period){
      if(!/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(period||""))return"";
      const date=new Date(`${period}-15T12:00:00`);date.setMonth(date.getMonth()-1);
      return`${date.getFullYear()}-${String(date.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0")}`;
    }
    function ratingRoundingHalfStep(value,platform){
      if(!finite(value))return null;
      const text=Math.abs(value).toFixed(6).replace(/0+$/,"").replace(/\.$/,"");
      const observedDecimals=(text.split(".")[1]||"").length;
      // El historico anterior a la preservacion de precision se guardaba a dos
      // decimales. Google Play puede publicar solo uno; en ese caso se respeta
      // la resolucion realmente observada en vez de asumir centesimas falsas.
      const minimumDecimals=platform==="Android"?1:2;
      return .5*Math.pow(10,-Math.max(minimumDecimals,observedDecimals));
    }
    function monthlyNewReviewImpact(trends,snapshot,selection,period){
      const previousPeriod=previousCalendarMonth(period),metadata=new Map((snapshot||[]).filter(row=>row.isBbva).map(row=>[row.appKey,row]));
      const scoped=(trends||[]).filter(row=>row.isBbva&&(selection.country==="ALL"||row.countryCode===selection.country)&&(selection.platform==="ALL"||row.platform===selection.platform));
      const byApp=groupBy(scoped,row=>row.appKey),rawItems=[];
      Object.values(byApp).forEach(appRows=>{
        const current=appRows.find(row=>row.month===period),previous=appRows.find(row=>row.month===previousPeriod);
        if(!current)return;
        const meta=metadata.get(current.appKey)||{},base={appKey:current.appKey,volumeKey:meta.volumeKey||current.appKey,volumeScope:meta.volumeScope||"MARKET",countryCode:current.countryCode,country:config.countries[current.countryCode]||current.country||current.countryCode,platform:current.platform,previousRating:previous?.ratingEom,currentRating:current.ratingEom,rating:null,newReviews:null,impact:null,confidence:"NONE"};
        if(meta.volumeScope==="SHARED_LISTING"&&selection.country!=="ALL"){rawItems.push({...base,available:false,reason:"SHARED_LISTING"});return}
        if(!previous||![current.ratingEom,current.ratingCountEom,previous.ratingEom,previous.ratingCountEom].every(finite)){rawItems.push({...base,available:false,reason:"MISSING_BASE"});return}
        const newReviews=current.ratingCountEom-previous.ratingCountEom;
        const inputSignature=[previous.ratingEom,previous.ratingCountEom,current.ratingEom,current.ratingCountEom].join("|");
        if(!(newReviews>0)){rawItems.push({...base,inputSignature,available:false,reason:"NO_NEW_REVIEWS"});return}
        const rating=(current.ratingCountEom*current.ratingEom-previous.ratingCountEom*previous.ratingEom)/newReviews;
        const currentHalfStep=ratingRoundingHalfStep(current.ratingEom,current.platform),previousHalfStep=ratingRoundingHalfStep(previous.ratingEom,previous.platform);
        const roundingUncertainty=(current.ratingCountEom*currentHalfStep+previous.ratingCountEom*previousHalfStep)/newReviews;
        if(!finite(rating)||rating<1||rating>5||roundingUncertainty>.5){rawItems.push({...base,inputSignature,newReviews,available:false,reason:"ROUNDING"});return}
        rawItems.push({...base,inputSignature,newReviews,rating,impact:rating-previous.ratingEom,available:true,confidence:roundingUncertainty<=.1?"HIGH":roundingUncertainty<=.25?"MEDIUM":"INDICATIVE"});
      });
      let items=rawItems;
      if(selection.country==="ALL"){
        const independent=rawItems.filter(item=>item.volumeScope!=="SHARED_LISTING"),shared=groupBy(rawItems.filter(item=>item.volumeScope==="SHARED_LISTING"),item=>item.volumeKey);
        items=independent.concat(Object.values(shared).map(group=>{
          const first=group[0],countries=[...new Set(group.map(item=>item.country))].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,"es")),consistent=new Set(group.map(item=>item.inputSignature)).size===1;
          return{...first,appKey:first.volumeKey,countryCode:"SHARED",country:"Ficha compartida multipaís",scopeLabel:countries.join(" · "),sharedMarkets:countries,available:consistent&&first.available,reason:consistent?first.reason:"SHARED_CONFLICT"};
        }));
      }
      const rows=items.filter(item=>isCriticalPlatform(item.platform)||item.available).map(item=>({...item,reasons:item.available?[]:[item.reason]})).sort((a,b)=>{
        const countryOrder=a.country.localeCompare(b.country,"es");return countryOrder||config.platforms.indexOf(a.platform)-config.platforms.indexOf(b.platform)||a.appKey.localeCompare(b.appKey);
      });
      const platformSummaries=config.platforms.map(platform=>{
        const platformRows=rows.filter(item=>item.platform===platform),valid=platformRows.filter(item=>item.available),newReviews=sum(valid.map(item=>item.newReviews)),rating=newReviews?sum(valid.map(item=>item.rating*item.newReviews))/newReviews:null,referenceRating=newReviews?sum(valid.map(item=>item.previousRating*item.newReviews))/newReviews:null;
        return{platform,rating,newReviews,impact:finite(rating)&&finite(referenceRating)?rating-referenceRating:null,availableSeries:valid.length,totalSeries:platformRows.length};
      }).filter(item=>item.totalSeries);
      const validItems=rows.filter(item=>item.available);
      return{period,previousPeriod,rows,summary:{platforms:platformSummaries,improvingSeries:validItems.filter(row=>row.impact>.005).length,pressuringSeries:validItems.filter(row=>row.impact<-.005).length,stableSeries:validItems.filter(row=>Math.abs(row.impact)<=.005).length,availableSeries:validItems.length,totalSeries:rows.length}};
    }
    function exclusiveScopeSelection(values,changedValue,checked){
      const selected=[...new Set((values||[]).filter(Boolean))];
      if(changedValue==="ALL"&&checked)return["ALL"];
      const specific=selected.filter(value=>value!=="ALL");
      return specific.length?specific:["ALL"];
    }
    window.MarketsDomain=Object.freeze({config,finite,sum,sumOrNull,mean,groupBy,appName,isCriticalPlatform,selectionRows,coverageRows,trendRows,uniqueStoreRows,volumeSummary,rankRows,countBbvaLeaders,availablePeriods,periodModel,evolutionKpis,prepareSeries,latestValue,ratingComparison,monthlyRatingMatrix,ratingRoundingHalfStep,monthlyNewReviewImpact,exclusiveScopeSelection});
  })();
</script>

        <section class="section panel monthly-pulse-panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><h3>Pulso mensual de BBVA Empresas</h3><span>Histórico hasta el cierre seleccionado · rating y opiniones por plataforma</span></div><span id="monthly-matrix-meta" class="panel-meta">Últimos cierres</span></div><div id="monthly-matrix" class="monthly-matrix-wrap"></div><div class="monthly-matrix-note"><span><b>Lectura:</b> la variación solo aparece cuando existe movimiento real.</span><span id="monthly-shared-note" class="hidden"><b>N/D:</b> listing Android compartido sin desglose fiable por geografía.</span></div></section>

Si falta volumen, continuidad o precisión, se muestra N/D.</span></div></section>

            <div><span class="alert-profile-kicker">Tu canal de señales</span><h3 id="alert-user-email">—</h3><p id="alert-system-copy">Solo tú puedes ver y modificar esta configuración.</p></div>

            </div>
          </section>

          <div class="alert-status-grid">
            <article class="alert-status-card"><span class="status-orb" id="alert-mail-orb"></span><div><small>Canal de correo</small><strong id="alert-mail-status">Comprobando</strong></div></article>
            <article class="alert-status-card"><span class="status-orb aqua"></span><div><small>Frecuencia</small><strong id="alert-schedule">Resumen diario</strong></div></article>

            <article class="alert-status-card"><span class="status-orb private"></span><div><small>Privacidad</small><strong>Configuración individual</strong></div></article>
          </div>
          <div id="alert-config-warning" class="alert-inline-message warning hidden"></div>

          <form id="alert-form">
            <section class="alert-config-section">
              <div class="alert-section-heading"><div><span class="alert-step">01</span><div><h3>Ámbito que quieres vigilar</h3><p>Combina mercados y plataformas. “Todos” mantiene automáticamente las nuevas geografías.</p></div></div></div>
              <div class="scope-layout"><div><strong class="scope-label">Mercados</strong><div id="alert-country-scope" class="scope-chips"></div><small id="alert-country-scope-summary" class="scope-summary"></small></div><div><strong class="scope-label">Plataformas</strong><div id="alert-platform-scope" class="scope-chips"></div><small id="alert-platform-scope-summary" class="scope-summary"></small></div></div>
            </section>

            <section class="alert-config-section">
              <div class="alert-section-heading"><div><span class="alert-step">02</span><div><h3>Señales que quieres recibir</h3><p>Activa solo las reglas que sean útiles para ti y personaliza sus límites.</p></div></div></div>

ARCHIVO: Report.html

<script>
  (()=>{
    "use strict";
    const D=window.MarketsDomain,U=window.MarketsUI,C=window.MarketsCharts;
    const finite=D.finite;
    const esc=value=>U.esc(value==null?"":String(value));
    const fmt=(value,digits=1)=>finite(value)?value.toLocaleString("es-ES",{minimumFractionDigits:digits,maximumFractionDigits:digits}):"—";
    const integer=value=>finite(value)?Math.round(value).toLocaleString("es-ES"):"—";
    const signed=value=>{if(!finite(value))return"—";const normalized=Math.abs(value)<.005?0:value;return`${normalized>0?"+":""}${fmt(normalized,2)}`};
    const pct=value=>finite(value)?`${fmt(value*100,0)}%`:"—";
    const dateLabel=value=>{if(!value)return"—";const date=/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(value))?new Date(`${value}T12:00:00`):new Date(value);return Number.isNaN(date.getTime())?String(value):new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-ES",{day:"2-digit",month:"long",year:"numeric"}).format(date)};
    const dateTimeLabel=value=>{const date=new Date(value);return Number.isNaN(date.getTime())?"—":new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-ES",{day:"2-digit",month:"short",year:"numeric",hour:"2-digit",minute:"2-digit"}).format(date)};
    const monthLabel=value=>value?new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-ES",{month:"short",year:"2-digit"}).format(new Date(`${value}-15T12:00:00`)):"—";
    const countryName=(code,meta)=>D.config.countries[code]||meta?.countries?.find(item=>item.code===code)?.name||code;
    const platformOrder=platform=>D.config.platforms.indexOf(platform);
    const tone=value=>!finite(value)?"neutral":value>.005?"positive":value<-.005?"negative":"neutral";

    function render(input){
      const target=input?.target;
      if(!target)throw new Error("No se ha encontrado el contenedor del informe.");
      const meta=input.meta||{},snapshot=input.snapshot||[],trends=input.trends||[],segment=input.benchmarkSegment||"BANK",generatedAt=input.generatedAt||new Date();
      if(!["overview","benchmark","evolution"].includes(input.view))throw new Error("La vista actual no admite descarga de informe.");
      const view=input.view;
      const selection={country:input.selection?.country||"ALL",platform:input.selection?.platform||"ALL",bbvaOnly:view==="evolution"&&input.selection?.bbvaOnly===true};
      const bbva=D.selectionRows(snapshot,selection,"BANK").filter(row=>row.isBbva);
      const platforms=selection.platform==="ALL"?D.config.platforms.filter(platform=>D.isCriticalPlatform(platform)||bbva.some(row=>row.platform===platform&&finite(row.rating))):[selection.platform];
      const coverageRows=D.coverageRows(bbva,selection);
      const observed=coverageRows.filter(row=>finite(row.rating));
      const competitive=D.selectionRows(snapshot,selection,view==="benchmark"?segment:"BANK",true).filter(row=>finite(row.rating));
      const volume=D.volumeSummary(bbva);
      const leaders=D.countBbvaLeaders(competitive);
      const average=D.mean(observed.map(row=>row.rating));
      const expected=coverageRows.length;
      const fresh=coverageRows.filter(row=>row.fresh&&finite(row.rating)).length;
      const coverage=expected?fresh/expected:null;
      const trendRows=D.trendRows(trends,selection);
      const period=D.periodModel(trendRows,view==="evolution"?input.periodYear||"":"",view==="evolution"?input.periodMonth||"":"").period;
      const evolution=D.evolutionKpis(trendRows,snapshot,selection,period);
      const series=D.prepareSeries(trendRows,selection);
      const displaySeries=series.map(item=>({...item,label:item.label.replace("BBVA Empresas ","")}));
      const scopeLabel=`${selection.country==="ALL"?"Todos los mercados":countryName(selection.country,meta)} · ${D.config.platformLabels[selection.platform]||selection.platform}`;
      const viewLabel={overview:"Resumen",benchmark:"Benchmark",evolution:"Evolución"}[view];
      const chart=view==="evolution"?C.lineChart(displaySeries,{period,idPrefix:"report",format:fmt,monthLabel,allMarkets:selection.country==="ALL",context:scopeLabel}):null;
      const geography=buildGeography(bbva,meta,platforms,selection);
      const comparison=D.ratingComparison(snapshot,selection);
      const benchmark=buildBenchmark(competitive,meta);
      const benchmarkRows=view==="benchmark"?buildBenchmarkRows(competitive,meta):[];
      const movements=view==="evolution"?buildMovements(displaySeries,period):[];
      const monthly=view==="evolution"?D.monthlyRatingMatrix(trends,snapshot,selection,period,6):null;
      const newReviewImpact=view==="evolution"?D.monthlyNewReviewImpact(trends,snapshot,selection,period):null;
      const detail=buildDetail(bbva.filter(row=>D.isCriticalPlatform(row.platform)||finite(row.rating)),benchmark,meta);
      const signals=buildSignals(observed,benchmark,coverage,meta);
      const builders=[];
      if(view==="overview"){
        builders.push((context,page)=>executivePage(context,{average,coverage,leaders,volume,evolution,signals,observed,expected,scopeLabel},page,"01"));
        builders.push((context,page)=>comparisonPage(context,{comparison,selection,meta,scopeLabel},page,"02"));
        builders.push((context,page)=>geographyPage(context,{geography,platforms,scopeLabel},page,"03"));
        chunk(detail,12).forEach((rows,index)=>builders.push((context,page)=>detailPage(context,{detail:rows},page,chunkLabel(rows),`04${detail.length>12?String.fromCharCode(65+index):""}`)));
      }else if(view==="benchmark"){
        chunk(benchmarkRows,12).forEach((rows,index)=>builders.push((context,page)=>benchmarkPage(context,{benchmark:rows,segment,scopeLabel},page,chunkLabel(rows),`01${benchmarkRows.length>12?String.fromCharCode(65+index):""}`)));
      }else{
        builders.push((context,page)=>evolutionPage(context,{chart,movements,evolution,period,scopeLabel},page,"01"));
        builders.push((context,page)=>monthlyPulsePage(context,{monthly,scopeLabel},page,"02"));
        builders.push((context,page)=>newReviewImpactPage(context,{model:newReviewImpact,scopeLabel},page,"03"));
      }
      const totalPages=1+builders.length;
      const context={meta,generatedAt,totalPages,view,viewLabel,scopeLabel,segment};
      const pages=[coverPage(context,{average,coverage,leaders,volume,evolution}),...builders.map((builder,index)=>builder(context,index+2))];
      target.innerHTML=pages.join("");
      target.setAttribute("aria-hidden","false");
      return{pages:totalPages,rows:{geography:geography.length,benchmark:view==="benchmark"?benchmarkRows.length:benchmark.length,detail:detail.length,monthly:monthly?.rows?.length||0,newReviewImpact:newReviewImpact?.rows?.length||0},period};
    }

    const chunk=(rows,size)=>Array.from({length:Math.ceil(rows.length/size)},(_,index)=>rows.slice(index*size,(index+1)*size));
    const chunkLabel=rows=>{const countries=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>row.country||countryName(row.countryCode)))];return countries.length===1?countries[0]:`${countries[0]||"Sin datos"}–${countries.at(-1)||""}`};

    function buildGeography(rows,meta,platforms,selection){
      return Object.entries(D.groupBy(rows,row=>row.countryCode)).map(([countryCode,items])=>{
        const byPlatform=Object.fromEntries(platforms.map(platform=>[platform,items.find(row=>row.platform===platform)]));
        const rated=items.filter(row=>finite(row.rating));
        const essential=D.coverageRows(items,selection),essentialRated=essential.filter(row=>finite(row.rating));
        return{countryCode,country:countryName(countryCode,meta),rating:D.mean(rated.map(row=>row.rating)),delta:D.mean(rated.map(row=>row.delta30d)),coverage:essential.length?essentialRated.length/essential.length:null,rated:essentialRated.length,expected:essential.length,platforms:byPlatform};
      }).sort((a,b)=>a.country.localeCompare(b.country,"es"));
    }

    function buildBenchmark(rows,meta){
      return Object.values(D.groupBy(rows,row=>`${row.countryCode}|${row.platform}`)).map(group=>{
        const ranked=D.rankRows(group),bbva=ranked.find(row=>row.isBbva),leader=ranked[0],complete=ranked.length>1&&ranked.every(row=>row.rankingComplete);
        return{countryCode:group[0].countryCode,country:countryName(group[0].countryCode,meta),platform:group[0].platform,bbva,leader,complete,apps:ranked.length,gap:bbva&&leader?bbva.rating-leader.rating:null};
      }).sort((a,b)=>a.country.localeCompare(b.country,"es")||platformOrder(a.platform)-platformOrder(b.platform));
    }

    function buildMovements(series,period){
      return series.map(item=>{const points=[...(item.points||[])].filter(point=>finite(point.value)&&(!period||point.month<=period)).sort((a,b)=>a.month.localeCompare(b.month));const current=points.at(-1),previous=points.at(-2);return{label:item.label,current:current?.value,period:current?.month,delta:current&&previous?current.value-previous.value:null}}).filter(item=>finite(item.current)).sort((a,b)=>(a.delta??0)-(b.delta??0));
    }

    function buildBenchmarkRows(rows,meta){
      return Object.values(D.groupBy(rows,row=>`${row.countryCode}|${row.platform}`)).flatMap(group=>{
        const ranked=D.rankRows(group),complete=ranked.length>1&&ranked.every(row=>row.rankingComplete);
        return ranked.map(row=>({...row,country:countryName(row.countryCode,meta),apps:ranked.length,complete}));
      }).sort((a,b)=>a.country.localeCompare(b.country,"es")||platformOrder(a.platform)-platformOrder(b.platform)||a.computedRank-b.computedRank||D.appName(a).localeCompare(D.appName(b),"es"));
    }

    function buildDetail(rows,benchmark,meta){
      const rankMap=new Map(benchmark.map(item=>[`${item.countryCode}|${item.platform}`,item]));
      return [...rows].sort((a,b)=>countryName(a.countryCode,meta).localeCompare(countryName(b.countryCode,meta),"es")||platformOrder(a.platform)-platformOrder(b.platform)).map(row=>{
        const rank=rankMap.get(`${row.countryCode}|${row.platform}`);
        return{...row,country:countryName(row.countryCode,meta),computedRank:rank?.bbva?.computedRank,rankApps:rank?.apps,rankComplete:rank?.complete,leader:rank?.leader};
      });
    }

    function buildSignals(observed,benchmark,coverage,meta){
      const deltas=observed.filter(row=>finite(row.delta30d)).sort((a,b)=>a.delta30d-b.delta30d);
      const worst=deltas[0],best=deltas.at(-1);
      const opportunities=benchmark.filter(item=>item.complete&&item.bbva&&item.bbva.computedRank>1).sort((a,b)=>b.gap-a.gap);
      const defended=benchmark.filter(item=>item.complete&&item.bbva?.computedRank===1).sort((a,b)=>b.gap-a.gap);
      const cards=[];
      if(worst)cards.push({type:"attention",eyebrow:"Prioridad",title:`${countryName(worst.countryCode,meta)} · ${worst.platform}`,copy:`Es el mayor retroceso mensual de BBVA Empresas: ${signed(worst.delta30d)} puntos, con un rating actual de ${fmt(worst.rating,2)}.`});
      if(opportunities[0])cards.push({type:"opportunity",eyebrow:"Oportunidad competitiva",title:`A ${fmt(Math.abs(opportunities[0].gap),2)} pts del líder`,copy:`${opportunities[0].country} · ${opportunities[0].platform}: BBVA ocupa la posición ${opportunities[0].bbva.computedRank} de ${opportunities[0].apps}.`});
      if(defended[0])cards.push({type:"strength",eyebrow:"Fortaleza",title:`Liderazgo en ${defended[0].country}`,copy:`BBVA Empresas lidera ${defended[0].platform} con ${fmt(defended[0].bbva.rating,2)} frente a ${esc(defended[0].leader?.isBbva?"el resto del mercado":defended[0].leader?.appName||"el mercado")}.`});
      else if(best)cards.push({type:"strength",eyebrow:"Mejor impulso",title:`${countryName(best.countryCode,meta)} · ${best.platform}`,copy:`Mejora ${signed(best.delta30d)} puntos en el último mes y alcanza ${fmt(best.rating,2)}.`});
      if(!finite(coverage)||coverage<1)cards.push({type:"quality",eyebrow:"Calidad del dato",title:`Cobertura ${pct(coverage)}`,copy:"Conviene interpretar comparativas y promedios junto con las fuentes pendientes de actualización."});
      return cards.slice(0,4);
    }

    function pageShell(context,pageNumber,title,kicker,body,className=""){
      return`<section class="report-page ${className}"><header class="report-header"><div class="report-header-brand"><span class="bbva-logo" role="img" aria-label="BBVA Banca de Empresas e Instituciones"></span><i></i><strong>Market Pulse</strong></div><div><span>${esc(kicker)}</span><b>${esc(title)}</b></div><time>${esc(dateLabel(context.meta.dataDate))}</time></header><div class="report-content">${body}</div><footer class="report-footer"><span>Market Pulse · BBVA Banca de Empresas e Instituciones · Uso interno</span><span>${pageNumber} / ${context.totalPages}</span></footer></section>`;
    }

    function coverPage(context,data){
      const cards=context.view==="evolution"?[["Cierre",fmt(data.evolution.current,2),monthLabel(data.evolution.currentPeriod)],["Evolución mensual",signed(data.evolution.monthly),data.evolution.monthPeriod?`vs. ${monthLabel(data.evolution.monthPeriod)}`:"Sin comparable"],["Evolución anual",signed(data.evolution.annual),data.evolution.yearPeriod?`vs. ${monthLabel(data.evolution.yearPeriod)}`:"Sin comparable"],["Cobertura",pct(data.evolution.coverage),`${integer(data.evolution.observed)} de ${integer(data.evolution.expected)} apps`]]:context.view==="benchmark"?[["Rating BBVA",fmt(data.average,2),"Escala 1–5"],["Liderazgos",integer(data.leaders.value),`de ${integer(data.leaders.comparable)} comparables`],["Comparables",integer(data.leaders.comparable),"Mercados y stores"],["Universo",D.config.segmentLabels[context.segment]||context.segment,"Benchmark activo"]]:[["Rating medio",fmt(data.average,2),"Escala 1–5"],["Cobertura",pct(data.coverage),"Apps BBVA actualizadas"],["Liderazgos",integer(data.leaders.value),`de ${integer(data.leaders.comparable)} comparables`],["Reseñas",integer(data.volume.accumulated),"Volumen deduplicado"]];
      return`<section class="report-page report-cover"><div class="report-cover-top"><div class="report-cover-brand"><span class="bbva-logo" role="img" aria-label="BBVA Banca de Empresas e Instituciones"></span><i></i><strong>Market Pulse</strong></div><span class="report-confidential">USO INTERNO</span></div><div class="report-cover-main"><span class="report-overline">${esc(context.viewLabel)} · Ratings intelligence</span><h1>La voz del mercado,<br><em>convertida en decisión.</em></h1><p>Informe de ${esc(context.viewLabel)} para ${esc(context.scopeLabel)}. El contenido reproduce exactamente el contexto seleccionado al solicitar la descarga.</p><div class="report-cover-meta"><div><small>Datos a cierre de</small><strong>${esc(dateLabel(context.meta.dataDate))}</strong></div><div><small>Vista</small><strong>${esc(context.viewLabel)}</strong></div><div><small>Alcance</small><strong>${esc(context.scopeLabel)}</strong></div></div></div><div class="report-cover-strip">${cards.map(card=>`<article><span>${esc(card[0])}</span><strong>${esc(card[1])}</strong><small>${esc(card[2])}</small></article>`).join("")}</div><footer class="report-cover-footer"><span>Market Pulse · Generado ${esc(dateTimeLabel(context.generatedAt))}</span><span>01 / ${context.totalPages}</span></footer></section>`;
    }

    function executivePage(context,data,pageNumber,sectionNumber){
      const kpis=[
        ["Rating medio",fmt(data.average,2),data.scopeLabel],
        ["Evolución mensual",signed(data.evolution.monthly),data.evolution.monthPeriod?`vs. ${monthLabel(data.evolution.monthPeriod)}`:"Sin cierre comparable"],
        ["Evolución anual",signed(data.evolution.annual),data.evolution.yearPeriod?`vs. ${monthLabel(data.evolution.yearPeriod)}`:"Sin cierre comparable"],
        ["Liderazgos",`${integer(data.leaders.value)} / ${integer(data.leaders.comparable)}`,"Mercados y stores comparables"],
        ["Nuevas reseñas",integer(data.volume.period),"Últimos 30 días · deduplicado"],
        ["Cobertura fresca",pct(data.coverage),`${data.observed.length} con rating · ${data.expected} esperadas`]
      ];
      const body=`<div class="report-title"><span>${esc(sectionNumber)} · Lectura ejecutiva</span><h2>Qué merece atención ahora</h2><p>${esc(data.scopeLabel)} · indicadores preparados para decidir.</p></div><div class="report-kpi-grid">${kpis.map((item,index)=>`<article class="report-kpi ${index===1||index===2?tone(index===1?data.evolution.monthly:data.evolution.annual):""}"><span>${esc(item[0])}</span><strong>${esc(item[1])}</strong><small>${esc(item[2])}</small></article>`).join("")}</div><div class="report-section-label"><span>Señales para actuar</span><i></i></div><div class="report-signal-grid">${data.signals.map(signal=>`<article class="report-signal ${signal.type}"><span>${esc(signal.eyebrow)}</span><h3>${esc(signal.title)}</h3><p>${esc(signal.copy)}</p></article>`).join("")}</div><aside class="report-note"><strong>Contexto aplicado</strong><span>Este análisis utiliza únicamente el mercado y la plataforma seleccionados al descargar.</span></aside>`;
      return pageShell(context,pageNumber,"Resumen ejecutivo",data.scopeLabel,body);
    }

    function geographyPage(context,data,pageNumber,sectionNumber){
      const rows=data.geography.map(item=>`<tr><th><span class="report-flag">${esc(D.config.flags[item.countryCode]||"🌐")}</span><div><strong>${esc(item.country)}</strong><small>${item.rated} de ${item.expected} fuentes esenciales</small></div></th><td class="report-rating"><strong>${fmt(item.rating,2)}</strong><span class="${tone(item.delta)}">${signed(item.delta)}</span></td>${data.platforms.map(platform=>platformCell(item.platforms[platform],platform)).join("")}<td><span class="report-coverage"><i style="width:${Math.round((item.coverage||0)*100)}%"></i></span><small>${pct(item.coverage)}</small></td></tr>`).join("");
      const body=`<div class="report-title"><span>${esc(sectionNumber)} · Panorama geográfico</span><h2>BBVA Empresas, mercado a mercado</h2><p>${esc(data.scopeLabel)} · rating actual y variación a 30 días.</p></div><div class="report-table-wrap"><table class="report-table report-geo-table"><thead><tr><th>Mercado</th><th>Media</th>${data.platforms.map(platform=>`<th>${esc(platform)}</th>`).join("")}<th>Cobertura</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table></div><div class="report-legend"><span><i class="positive"></i> Mejora</span><span><i class="negative"></i> Retroceso</span><span><i class="neutral"></i> Estable o sin comparable</span><small>La variación indicada corresponde a 30 días.</small></div>`;
      return pageShell(context,pageNumber,"Panorama geográfico",data.scopeLabel,body);
    }

    function platformCell(row,platform){
      if(!row||!finite(row.rating))return platform==="Huawei"?`<td class="report-platform-cell"><strong>—</strong><small>Dato complementario</small></td>`:`<td class="report-platform-cell missing"><strong>—</strong><small>Fuente pendiente</small></td>`;
      return`<td class="report-platform-cell"><strong>${fmt(row.rating,2)}</strong><small class="${tone(row.delta30d)}">${signed(row.delta30d)}</small></td>`;
    }

    function benchmarkPage(context,data,pageNumber,rangeLabel,sectionNumber){
      const rows=data.benchmark.map(item=>`<tr class="${item.isBbva?"report-row-bbva":""}"><th><strong>${esc(item.country)}</strong><small>${esc(item.platform)}</small></th><td><span class="report-rank ${item.computedRank===1&&item.complete?"leader":""}">${item.computedRank} / ${item.apps}</span></td><td><strong>${esc(D.appName(item))}</strong><small>${item.isBbva?"BBVA Empresas":"Competidor"}</small></td><td><strong>${fmt(item.rating,2)}</strong><small class="${tone(item.delta30d)}">${signed(item.delta30d)}</small></td><td><strong>${integer(item.ratingCount)}</strong><small>acumuladas</small></td><td><span class="report-quality ${item.platform==="Huawei"||item.complete&&item.fresh?"complete":"pending"}">${item.platform==="Huawei"?"Dato complementario":item.complete&&item.fresh?"Actualizado":item.complete?"Carga anterior":"Cobertura parcial"}</span></td></tr>`).join("");
      const pageScope=context.totalPages>2&&rangeLabel?`${data.scopeLabel} · ${rangeLabel}`:data.scopeLabel;
      const body=`<div class="report-title compact"><span>${esc(sectionNumber)} · Posición competitiva</span><h2>Ranking completo del mercado</h2><p>${esc(D.config.segmentLabels[data.segment]||data.segment)} · ${esc(pageScope)}.</p></div><div class="report-table-wrap"><table class="report-table report-benchmark-table"><thead><tr><th>Mercado / store</th><th>Posición</th><th>Aplicación</th><th>Rating / Δ30d</th><th>Opiniones</th><th>Calidad</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table></div><aside class="report-note compact"><strong>Lectura</strong><span>El informe reproduce todas las aplicaciones visibles en Benchmark. La posición solo es comparable cuando la carga incluye el conjunto esperado.</span></aside>`;
      return pageShell(context,pageNumber,"Benchmark competitivo",pageScope,body);
    }

    function comparisonPage(context,data,pageNumber,sectionNumber){
      const label=row=>data.selection.country==="ALL"?`BBVA ${countryName(row.countryCode,data.meta)}`:row.isBbva?`BBVA ${countryName(row.countryCode,data.meta)}`:D.appName(row);
      const boards=data.comparison.map(group=>`<section><header><strong>${esc(group.platform)}</strong><span>${group.rows.length} referencias</span></header><ol>${group.rows.map(row=>`<li><span>${esc(label(row))}</span><i><b style="width:${Math.max(0,Math.min(100,row.rating/5*100))}%"></b></i><strong>${fmt(row.rating,1)}</strong></li>`).join("")}</ol></section>`).join("");
      const scope=data.selection.country==="ALL"?"BBVA Empresas por geografía":`BBVA Empresas y bancos de ${countryName(data.selection.country,data.meta)}`;
      const body=`<div class="report-title compact"><span>${esc(sectionNumber)} · Rating actual</span><h2>Comparativa por plataforma</h2><p>${esc(scope)} · escala pública 1–5.</p></div><div class="report-rating-compare">${boards||U.empty("Sin ratings comparables","No hay observaciones con el filtro seleccionado.")}</div>`;
      return pageShell(context,pageNumber,"Rating actual por plataforma",data.scopeLabel,body);
    }

    function evolutionPage(context,data,pageNumber,sectionNumber){
      const body=`<div class="report-title compact"><span>${esc(sectionNumber)} · Evolución</span><h2>La trayectoria detrás del dato actual</h2><p>${esc(data.scopeLabel)} · hasta ${esc(monthLabel(data.period))}.</p></div><div class="report-evolution-kpis"><article><span>Cierre</span><strong>${fmt(data.evolution.current,2)}</strong><small>${esc(monthLabel(data.evolution.currentPeriod))}</small></article><article class="${tone(data.evolution.monthly)}"><span>vs. mes anterior</span><strong>${signed(data.evolution.monthly)}</strong><small>${esc(monthLabel(data.evolution.monthPeriod))}</small></article><article class="${tone(data.evolution.annual)}"><span>vs. año anterior</span><strong>${signed(data.evolution.annual)}</strong><small>${esc(monthLabel(data.evolution.yearPeriod))}</small></article><article><span>Cobertura esencial</span><strong>${pct(data.evolution.coverage)}</strong><small>${integer(data.evolution.observed)} de ${integer(data.evolution.expected)} series Android/iOS</small></article></div><div class="report-evolution-layout"><section class="report-chart"><div>${data.chart.svg}</div><div class="report-chart-legend">${data.chart.legend}</div></section><aside class="report-movements"><h3>Último movimiento</h3><p>Variación frente al cierre anterior disponible.</p>${data.movements.slice(0,8).map(item=>`<div><span>${esc(item.label.replace("BBVA Empresas ",""))}</span><strong>${fmt(item.current,2)}</strong><b class="${tone(item.delta)}">${signed(item.delta)}</b></div>`).join("")}</aside></div>`;
      return pageShell(context,pageNumber,"Evolución de ratings",data.scopeLabel,body);
    }

    function monthlyPulsePage(context,data,pageNumber,sectionNumber){
      const model=data.monthly;
      const header=(model.periods||[]).map((month,index)=>`<th class="${index===model.periods.length-1?"latest":""}">${esc(monthLabel(month))}</th>`).join("");
      const rows=(model.rows||[]).map(row=>`<tr><th><strong>${esc(row.country)}</strong><small>${esc(row.countryCode)}</small></th>${row.cells.map((cell,index)=>`<td class="${index===row.cells.length-1?"latest":""}"><strong>${fmt(cell.rating,2)}</strong><em class="${tone(cell.delta)}">${signed(cell.delta)}</em>${cell.platforms.map(item=>`<small><b>${esc(item.platform)}</b> ${item.unavailable?"N/D":integer(item.value)}</small>`).join("")}</td>`).join("")}</tr>`).join("");
      const body=`<div class="report-title compact"><span>${esc(sectionNumber)} · Pulso mensual</span><h2>BBVA Empresas, cierre a cierre</h2><p>${esc(data.scopeLabel)} · hasta ${esc(monthLabel(model.periods?.at(-1)))}.</p></div><div class="report-table-wrap"><table class="report-table report-monthly-table"><thead><tr><th>Geografía</th>${header}</tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table></div><aside class="report-note compact"><strong>Opiniones acumuladas</strong><span>Se muestran por plataforma y respetan el corte seleccionado. N/D identifica una ficha Android compartida que no admite desglose fiable por geografía.</span></aside>`;
      return pageShell(context,pageNumber,"Pulso mensual de BBVA Empresas",data.scopeLabel,body);
    }

    function newReviewImpactPage(context,data,pageNumber,sectionNumber){
      const model=data.model||{},summary=model.summary||{},status=value=>!finite(value)?["Sin estimación","neutral"]:value>.005?["Impulsa el rating","positive"]:value<-.005?["Presiona el rating","negative"]:["Mantiene el nivel","neutral"];
      const reason=row=>row.reasons.includes("SHARED_LISTING")?"Ficha Android compartida sin desglose geográfico":row.reasons.includes("SHARED_CONFLICT")?"Cierres inconsistentes en la ficha compartida":row.reasons.includes("ROUNDING")?"Redondeo acumulado no fiable":row.reasons.includes("NO_NEW_REVIEWS")?"Sin nuevas opiniones":"Falta cierre comparable";
      const rows=(model.rows||[]).map(row=>{const rowSignal=status(row.impact);return`<tr><th><strong>${esc(row.country)}</strong><small>${esc(row.scopeLabel||row.countryCode)}</small></th><td><strong>${esc(row.platform)}</strong><small>${row.volumeScope==="SHARED_LISTING"?"Ficha multipaís":"Ficha independiente"}</small></td><td><strong>${finite(row.rating)?fmt(row.rating,2):"N/D"}</strong><small>${finite(row.rating)?"rating estimado / 5":esc(reason(row))}</small></td><td><strong>${finite(row.newReviews)?integer(row.newReviews):"N/D"}</strong><small>solo esta ficha</small></td><td><strong class="${rowSignal[1]}">${signed(row.impact)}</strong><small>vs. la misma ficha</small></td><td><span class="report-quality ${rowSignal[1]}">${esc(rowSignal[0])}</span></td></tr>`}).join("");
      const coverage=summary.totalSeries?summary.availableSeries/summary.totalSeries:null;
      const platformCards=(summary.platforms||[]).map(item=>{const signal=status(item.impact);return`<article><span>${esc(item.platform)}</span><strong>${finite(item.rating)?fmt(item.rating,2):"N/D"}</strong><small class="${signal[1]}">${finite(item.rating)?`${integer(item.newReviews)} opiniones · ${signed(item.impact)}`:"Sin estimación"}</small></article>`}).join("");
      const body=`<div class="report-title compact"><span>${esc(sectionNumber)} · Voz nueva del mes</span><h2>Qué nota dejan las nuevas opiniones</h2><p>${esc(data.scopeLabel)} · ${esc(monthLabel(model.previousPeriod))} a ${esc(monthLabel(model.period))} · cálculo separado por plataforma.</p></div><div class="report-new-review-summary">${platformCards}<article><span>Cobertura</span><strong>${pct(coverage)}</strong><small>${integer(summary.availableSeries)} de ${integer(summary.totalSeries)} fichas</small></article></div><div class="report-table-wrap"><table class="report-table report-new-review-table"><thead><tr><th>Mercado / ficha</th><th>Plataforma</th><th>Nota</th><th>Volumen</th><th>Efecto</th><th>Lectura</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table></div><aside class="report-note compact"><strong>Lectura responsable</strong><span>Cada estimación usa exclusivamente rating y volumen de la misma ficha y plataforma. Una ficha Android compartida se calcula una sola vez en alcance multipaís y nunca se atribuye a un mercado concreto. N/D evita mezclar perímetros o publicar resultados inestables.</span></aside>`;
      return pageShell(context,pageNumber,"Voz nueva del mes",data.scopeLabel,body);
    }

    function detailPage(context,data,pageNumber,rangeLabel,sectionNumber){
      const rows=data.detail.map(row=>`<tr><th><strong>${esc(row.country)}</strong><small>${esc(row.platform)}</small></th><td><strong>${fmt(row.rating,2)}</strong><small class="${tone(row.delta30d)}">${signed(row.delta30d)}</small></td><td>${row.rankComplete&&row.computedRank?`<strong>${row.computedRank} / ${row.rankApps}</strong>`:row.platform==="Huawei"?"—":"<span class=\"report-quality pending\">WIP</span>"}</td><td><strong>${row.leader?esc(row.leader.isBbva?"BBVA Empresas":row.leader.appName):"—"}</strong><small>${fmt(row.leader?.rating,2)}</small></td><td><strong>${row.volumeScope==="SHARED_LISTING"?"Compartido":integer(row.ratingCount)}</strong><small>${row.volumeScope==="SHARED_LISTING"?"Listing multipaís":`${integer(row.ratingCountDelta30d)} nuevas`}</small></td><td><span class="report-quality ${row.platform==="Huawei"||row.fresh&&finite(row.rating)?"complete":"pending"}">${row.platform==="Huawei"?"Dato complementario":row.fresh&&finite(row.rating)?"Actualizado":"Fuente pendiente"}</span></td></tr>`).join("");
      const body=`<div class="report-title compact"><span>${esc(sectionNumber)} · Anexo de gestión</span><h2>Detalle operativo por store</h2><p>${esc(rangeLabel)} · una fila por mercado y plataforma para facilitar el seguimiento local.</p></div><div class="report-table-wrap"><table class="report-table report-detail-table"><thead><tr><th>Mercado / store</th><th>Rating / Δ30d</th><th>Posición</th><th>Líder</th><th>Reseñas</th><th>Estado</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table></div>`;
      return pageShell(context,pageNumber,"Detalle por store",rangeLabel,body);
    }

    window.MarketsReport=Object.freeze({render});
  })();
</script>
